45!> The control structure for the MOM_neutral_diffusion module
46type, public :: neutral_diffusion_CS ; private
47 integer :: nkp1 !< Number of interfaces for a column = nk + 1
48 integer :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
49 integer :: niblock !< The i block size used in array calculations
50 integer :: njblock !< The j block size used in array calculations
51 integer :: deg = 2 !< Degree of polynomial used for reconstructions
52 logical :: continuous_reconstruction = .true. !< True if using continuous PPM reconstruction at interfaces
53 logical :: debug = .false. !< If true, write verbose debugging messages
54 logical :: hard_fail_heff !< Bring down the model if a problem with heff is detected
55 integer :: max_iter !< Maximum number of iterations if refine_position is defined
56 real :: drho_tol !< Convergence criterion representing density difference from true neutrality [R ~> kg m-3]
57 real :: x_tol !< Convergence criterion for how small an update of the position can be [nondim]
58 real :: ref_pres !< Reference pressure, negative if using locally referenced neutral
59 !! density [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
60 logical :: interior_only !< If true, only applies neutral diffusion in the ocean interior.
61 !! That is, the algorithm will exclude the surface and bottom boundary layers.
62 logical :: tapering = .false. !< If true, neutral diffusion linearly decays towards zero within a
63 !! transition zone defined using boundary layer depths. Only available when
64 !! interior_only=true.
65 logical :: KhTh_use_vert_struct !< If true, uses vertical structure
66 !! for tracer diffusivity.
67 logical :: use_unmasked_transport_bug !< If true, use an older form for the accumulation of
68 !! neutral-diffusion transports that were unmasked, as used prior to Jan 2018.
69 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:) :: hbl !< Boundary layer depth [H ~> m or kg m-2]
70 ! Coefficients used to apply tapering from neutral to horizontal direction
71 real, allocatable, dimension(:) :: coeff_l !< Non-dimensional coefficient in the left column,
72 !! at cell interfaces [nondim]
73 real, allocatable, dimension(:) :: coeff_r !< Non-dimensional coefficient in the right column,
74 !! at cell interfaces [nondim]
75 ! Array used when KhTh_use_vert_struct is true
76 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: Coef_h !< Coef_x and Coef_y averaged at t-points [L2 ~> m2]
77 ! Positions of neutral surfaces in both the u, v directions
78 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: uPoL !< Non-dimensional position with left layer uKoL-1, u-point [nondim]
79 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: uPoR !< Non-dimensional position with right layer uKoR-1, u-point [nondim]
80 integer, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: uKoL !< Index of left interface corresponding to neutral surface,
81 !! at a u-point
82 integer, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: uKoR !< Index of right interface corresponding to neutral surface,
83 !! at a u-point
84 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: uHeff !< Effective thickness at u-point [H ~> m or kg m-2]
85 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: vPoL !< Non-dimensional position with left layer uKoL-1, v-point [nondim]
86 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: vPoR !< Non-dimensional position with right layer uKoR-1, v-point [nondim]
87 integer, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: vKoL !< Index of left interface corresponding to neutral surface,
88 !! at a v-point
89 integer, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: vKoR !< Index of right interface corresponding to neutral surface,
90 !! at a v-point
91 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: vHeff !< Effective thickness at v-point [H ~> m or kg m-2]
92 ! Coefficients of polynomial reconstructions for temperature and salinity
93 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: ppoly_coeffs_T !< Polynomial coefficients of the
94 !! sub-gridscale temperatures [C ~> degC]
95 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: ppoly_coeffs_S !< Polynomial coefficients of the
96 !! sub-gridscale salinity [S ~> ppt]
97 ! Variables needed for continuous reconstructions
98 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: dRdT !< dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1] at interfaces
99 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: dRdS !< dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1] at interfaces
100 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: Tint !< Interface T [C ~> degC]
101 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: Sint !< Interface S [S ~> ppt]
102 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: Pint !< Interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
103 ! Variables needed for discontinuous reconstructions
104 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: T_i !< Top edge reconstruction of temperature [C ~> degC]
105 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: S_i !< Top edge reconstruction of salinity [S ~> ppt]
106 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: P_i !< Interface pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
107 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: dRdT_i !< dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1] at top edge
108 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:,:) :: dRdS_i !< dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1] at top edge
109 integer, allocatable, dimension(:,:) :: ns !< Number of interfaces in a column
110 logical, allocatable, dimension(:,:,:) :: stable_cell !< True if the cell is stably stratified wrt to the next cell
111 real :: R_to_kg_m3 = 1.0 !< A rescaling factor translating density to kg m-3 for
112 !! use in diagnostic messages [kg m-3 R-1 ~> 1].
113 type(diag_ctrl), pointer :: diag => NULL() !< A structure that is used to
114 !! regulate the timing of diagnostic output.
115 integer :: neutral_pos_method !< Method to find the position of a neutral surface within the layer
116 character(len=40) :: delta_rho_form !< Determine which (if any) approximation is made to the
117 !! equation describing the difference in density
118
119 integer :: id_uhEff_2d = -1 !< Diagnostic IDs
120 integer :: id_vhEff_2d = -1 !< Diagnostic IDs
121
122 type(EOS_type), pointer :: EOS => NULL() !< Equation of state parameters
123 type(remapping_CS) :: remap_CS !< Remapping control structure used to create sublayers
124 integer :: remap_answer_date !< The vintage of the order of arithmetic and expressions to use
125 !! for remapping. Values below 20190101 recover the remapping
126 !! answers from 2018, while higher values use more robust
127 !! forms of the same remapping expressions.
128 integer :: ndiff_answer_date !< The vintage of the order of arithmetic to use for the neutral
129 !! diffusion. Values of 20240330 or below recover the answers
130 !! from the original form of this code, while higher values use
131 !! mathematically equivalent expressions that recover rotational symmetry.
132 type(KPP_CS), pointer :: KPP_CSp => NULL() !< KPP control structure needed to get BLD
133 type(energetic_PBL_CS), pointer :: energetic_PBL_CSp => NULL()!< ePBL control structure needed to get MLD
134end type neutral_diffusion_CS

283 "Method used to find the neutral position \n"// &
284 "1. Delta_rho varies linearly, find 0 crossing \n"// &
285 "2. Alpha and beta vary linearly from top to bottom, \n"// &
