384!> Calculate remapping factors for u/v columns used to map adjoining columns to
385!! a shared coordinate space.
3860subroutine neutral_diffusion_calc_coeffs(G, GV, US, h, T, S, visc, CS, p_surf)
387 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
388 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
389 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
390 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
391 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: T !< Potential temperature [C ~> degC]
392 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: S !< Salinity [S ~> ppt]
393 type(vertvisc_type), intent(in) :: visc !< Structure with vertical viscosities,
394 !! boundary layer properties and related fields
395 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), pointer :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
396 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), optional, intent(in) :: p_surf !< Surface pressure to include in pressures used
397 !! for equation of state calculations [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
398
399 ! Local variables
400 integer, dimension(3,2) :: EOSdom ! The i-computational domain for the equation of state
401 integer :: i, j, k
402 ! Variables used for reconstructions
4030 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),2) :: ppoly_r_S ! Reconstruction slopes that are unused here, in units of a vertical
404 ! gradient, which for temperature would be [C H-1 ~> degC m-1 or degC m2 kg-1].
4050 real, dimension(SZI_(G), SZJ_(G)) :: hEff_sum ! Summed effective face thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
406 integer :: iMethod
4070 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1) :: ref_pres ! Reference pressure used to calculate alpha/beta [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
408 real :: h_neglect, h_neglect_edge ! Negligible thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4090 integer, dimension(SZI_(G), SZJ_(G)) :: k_top ! Index of the first layer within the boundary
4100 real, dimension(SZI_(G), SZJ_(G)) :: zeta_top ! Distance from the top of a layer to the intersection of the
411 ! top extent of the boundary layer (0 at top, 1 at bottom) [nondim]
4120 integer, dimension(SZI_(G), SZJ_(G)) :: k_bot ! Index of the last layer within the boundary
4130 real, dimension(SZI_(G), SZJ_(G)) :: zeta_bot ! Distance of the lower layer to the boundary layer depth [nondim]
414 real :: H_to_pa ! A conversion factor from thickness to rescaled pressure
415 ! [R L2 T-2 H-1 ~> Pa m-1 or Pa m2 kg-1]
416 real :: pa_to_H ! A conversion factor from rescaled pressure to thickness
417 ! (H) units [H T2 R-1 Z-2 ~> m Pa-1 or s2 m-1]
418 integer :: isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, iie, jje, nii, njj
419 integer :: nIIB ! The I block size for the zonal faces
420 integer :: nJJB ! The J block size for the meridional faces
421
422 ! block sizes
4230 nii = CS%niblock
4240 if (CS%niblock == 0) nii = (G%iec+1) - (G%isc-1) + 1
4250 njj = CS%njblock
4260 if (CS%njblock == 0) njj = (G%jec+1) - (G%jsc-1) + 1
427
4280 pa_to_H = 1. / (GV%H_to_RZ * GV%g_Earth)
4290 H_to_pa = GV%H_to_pa
430
431 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: k_top, k_bot, zeta_top, zeta_bot, ref_pres)
432
4330 do concurrent (j=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
4340 k_top(i,j) = 1 ; k_bot(i,j) = 1
4350 zeta_top(i,j) = 0. ; zeta_bot(i,j) = 0.
436 enddo
437
438 ! Check if hbl needs to be extracted
4390 if (CS%interior_only) then
4400 if (associated(visc%h_ML)) then
4410 CS%hbl(:,:) = visc%h_ML(:,:)
442 else
4430 call MOM_error(FATAL, "neutral_diffusion requires that visc%h_ML is associated.")
444 endif
4450 call pass_var(CS%hbl, G%Domain, halo=1)
446
447 ! get k-indices and zeta
448 !$omp target update from(k_top, k_bot, zeta_top, zeta_bot)
4490 do jsb=G%jsc-1,G%jec+1,njj ; do isb=G%isc-1,G%iec+1,nii
4500 jeb = min(jsb+njj-1, G%jec+1)
4510 ieb = min(isb+nii-1, G%iec+1)
452
4530 do j=jsb,jeb ; do i=isb,ieb
4540 if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) then
455 call boundary_k_range(SURFACE, G%ke, h(i,j,:), CS%hbl(i,j), k_top(i,j), zeta_top(i,j), k_bot(i,j), &
4560 zeta_bot(i,j))
457 endif
458 enddo ; enddo
459 enddo ; enddo
460 !$omp target update to(k_top, k_bot, zeta_top, zeta_bot)
461 ! TODO: add similar code for BOTTOM boundary layer
462 endif
463
4640 h_neglect = GV%H_subroundoff ; h_neglect_edge = GV%H_subroundoff
465
4660 if (.not. CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
4670 if (CS%remap_answer_date < 20190101) then
4680 if (GV%Boussinesq) then
4690 h_neglect = GV%m_to_H*1.0e-30 ; h_neglect_edge = GV%m_to_H*1.0e-10
470 else
4710 h_neglect = GV%kg_m2_to_H*1.0e-30 ; h_neglect_edge = GV%kg_m2_to_H*1.0e-10
472 endif
473 endif
474 endif
475
4760 if (CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
4770 do concurrent (k=1:GV%ke+1, j=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
4780 CS%dRdT(i,j,k) = 0.
4790 CS%dRdS(i,j,k) = 0.
480 enddo
481 else
482 !$omp target update from(T, S)
4830 CS%T_i(:,:,:,:) = 0.
4840 CS%S_i(:,:,:,:) = 0.
4850 CS%dRdT_i(:,:,:,:) = 0.
4860 CS%dRdS_i(:,:,:,:) = 0.
4870 CS%ns(:,:) = 0.
4880 CS%stable_cell(:,:,:) = .true.
489 endif
490
491 ! Calculate pressure at interfaces and layer averaged alpha/beta
4920 if (present(p_surf)) then
4930 do concurrent (j=G%jsc-1:G%jec+1, i=G%isc-1:G%iec+1)
4940 CS%Pint(i,j,1) = p_surf(i,j)
495 enddo
496 else
4970 CS%Pint(:,:,1) = 0.
498 endif
4990 do concurrent (j=G%jsc-1:G%jec+1)
5000 do k=1,GV%ke
5010 do concurrent (i=G%isc-1:G%iec+1)
5020 CS%Pint(i,j,k+1) = CS%Pint(i,j,k) + h(i,j,k)*(GV%g_Earth*GV%H_to_RZ)
503 enddo
504 enddo
505 enddo
506
507 ! Pressures at the interfaces, this is redundant as P_i(k,1) = P_i(k-1,2) however retain this
508 ! for now to ensure consistency of indexing for discontinuous reconstructions
5090 if (.not. CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
5100 if (present(p_surf)) then
5110 do j=G%jsc-1,G%jec+1 ; do i=G%isc-1,G%iec+1
5120 CS%P_i(i,j,1,1) = p_surf(i,j)
5130 CS%P_i(i,j,1,2) = p_surf(i,j) + h(i,j,1)*(GV%H_to_RZ*GV%g_Earth)
514 enddo ; enddo
515 else
5160 do j=G%jsc-1,G%jec+1 ; do i=G%isc-1,G%iec+1
5170 CS%P_i(i,j,1,1) = 0.
5180 CS%P_i(i,j,1,2) = h(i,j,1)*(GV%H_to_RZ*GV%g_Earth)
519 enddo ; enddo
520 endif
5210 do k=2,GV%ke ; do j=G%jsc-1,G%jec+1 ; do i=G%isc-1,G%iec+1
5220 CS%P_i(i,j,k,1) = CS%P_i(i,j,k-1,2)
5230 CS%P_i(i,j,k,2) = CS%P_i(i,j,k-1,2) + h(i,j,k)*(GV%H_to_RZ*GV%g_Earth)
524 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
525 endif
526
527 ! If doing along isopycnal diffusion (as opposed to neutral diffusion, set the reference pressure)
5280 if (CS%ref_pres>=0.) then
5290 do concurrent (k=1:GV%ke+1, j=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
5300 ref_pres(i,j,k) = CS%ref_pres
531 enddo
532 else
5330 do concurrent (k=1:GV%ke+1, j=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
5340 ref_pres(i,j,k) = CS%Pint(i,j,k)
535 enddo
536 endif
537
538 ! EOSdom(:) = EOS_domain(G%HI, halo=1)
5390 do jsb=G%jsc-1,G%jec+1,njj ; do isb=G%isc-1,G%iec+1,nii
5400 jeb = min(jsb+njj-1, G%jec+1)
5410 ieb = min(isb+nii-1, G%iec+1)
542
5430 EOSdom(1,1) = isb - G%isd + 1 ; EOSdom(1,2) = ieb - G%isd + 1
5440 EOSdom(2,1) = jsb - G%jsd + 1 ; EOSdom(2,2) = jeb - G%jsd + 1
5450 EOSdom(3,1) = 1 ; EOSdom(3,2) = merge(GV%ke+1, GV%ke, CS%continuous_reconstruction)
546
5470 if (CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
548 call interface_scalar_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, GV%ke, h, T, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
5490 CS%Tint, 2, h_neglect)
550 call interface_scalar_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, GV%ke, h, S, G%isd, G%ied, G%jsd, G%jed, &
5510 CS%Sint, 2, h_neglect)
5520 call calculate_density_derivs(CS%Tint, CS%Sint, ref_pres, CS%dRdT, CS%dRdS, CS%EOS, EOSdom)
553 else
5540 do j=jsb,jeb ; do i=isb,ieb
555 call build_reconstructions_1d( CS%remap_CS, GV%ke, h(i,j,:), T(i,j,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j,:,:), &
5560 CS%T_i(i,j,:,:), ppoly_r_S, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
557 call build_reconstructions_1d( CS%remap_CS, GV%ke, h(i,j,:), S(i,j,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j,:,:), &
5580 CS%S_i(i,j,:,:), ppoly_r_S, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
559 enddo ; enddo
5600 do j=jsb,jeb ; do k=1,GV%ke ; do i=isb,ieb
5610 CS%T_i(i,j,k,1) = evaluation_polynomial( CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j,k,:), CS%deg+1, 0. )
5620 CS%T_i(i,j,k,2) = evaluation_polynomial( CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j,k,:), CS%deg+1, 1. )
5630 CS%S_i(i,j,k,1) = evaluation_polynomial( CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j,k,:), CS%deg+1, 0. )
5640 CS%S_i(i,j,k,2) = evaluation_polynomial( CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j,k,:), CS%deg+1, 1. )
565 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
566 ! Calculate derivatives for the top interface
567 call calculate_density_derivs(CS%T_i(:,:,:,1), CS%S_i(:,:,:,1), ref_pres(:,:,1:GV%ke), CS%dRdT_i(:,:,:,1), &
5680 CS%dRdS_i(:,:,:,1), CS%EOS, EOSdom)
569 ! Calculate derivatives at the bottom interface
570 call calculate_density_derivs(CS%T_i(:,:,:,2), CS%S_i(:,:,:,2), ref_pres(:,:,2:GV%ke+1), CS%dRdT_i(:,:,:,2), &
5710 CS%dRdS_i(:,:,:,2), CS%EOS, EOSdom)
572
5730 do j=jsb,jeb ; do i=isb,ieb
5740 call mark_unstable_cells( CS, GV%ke, CS%T_i(i,j,:,:), CS%S_i(i,j,:,:), CS%P_i(i,j,:,:), CS%stable_cell(i,j,:) )
575 enddo ; enddo
5760 if (CS%interior_only) then
5770 do j=jsb,jeb ; do i=isb,ieb
5780 if (.not. CS%stable_cell(i,j,k_bot(i,j))) zeta_bot(i,j) = -1.
579 ! set values in the surface and bottom boundary layer to false.
5800 do k = 1, k_bot(i,j)
5810 CS%stable_cell(i,j,k) = .false.
582 enddo
583 enddo ; enddo
584 !$omp target update to(zeta_bot)
585 endif
586 endif
587 enddo ; enddo
588
589 ! Neutral surface factors at U points
5900 nIIB = CS%niblock
5910 if (CS%niblock == 0) nIIB = G%iec - G%isc + 2
5920 njj = CS%njblock
5930 if (CS%njblock == 0) njj = G%jec - G%jsc + 1
594 call zonal_neutral_positions(G, GV%ke, CS%nsurf, CS%Pint, CS%Tint, CS%Sint, CS%dRdT, CS%dRdS, k_bot, zeta_bot, &
5950 CS%uPoL, CS%uPoR, CS%uKoL, CS%uKoR, CS%uhEff, nIIB, njj, h, CS)
596
597 ! Neutral surface factors at V points
5980 nii = CS%niblock
5990 if (CS%niblock == 0) nii = G%iec - G%isc + 1
6000 nJJB = CS%njblock
6010 if (CS%njblock == 0) nJJB = G%jec - G%jsc + 2
602 call meridional_neutral_positions(G, GV%ke, CS%nsurf, CS%Pint, CS%Tint, CS%Sint, CS%dRdT, CS%dRdS, &
6030 k_bot, zeta_bot, CS%vPoL, CS%vPoR, CS%vKoL, CS%vKoR, CS%vhEff, nii, nJJB, h, CS)
604
605 ! Continuous reconstructions calculate hEff as the difference between the pressures of the
606 ! neutral surfaces which need to be reconverted to thickness units. The discontinuous version
607 ! calculates hEff from the nondimensional fraction of the layer spanned by adjacent neutral
608 ! surfaces, so hEff is already in thickness units.
6090 if (CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
6100 if (CS%use_unmasked_transport_bug) then
611 ! This option is not recommended but needed to recover answers prior to Jan 2018.
612 ! It is independent of the other 2018 answers flags.
6130 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, j=G%jsc:G%jec, I=G%isc-1:G%iec)
6140 CS%uhEff(I,j,k) = CS%uhEff(I,j,k) / H_to_pa
615 enddo
6160 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, J=G%jsc-1:G%jec, i=G%isc:G%iec)
6170 CS%vhEff(I,j,k) = CS%vhEff(I,j,k) / H_to_pa
618 enddo
619 else
6200 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, j=G%jsc:G%jec, I=G%isc-1:G%iec, G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0)
6210 CS%uhEff(I,j,k) = CS%uhEff(I,j,k) * pa_to_H
622 enddo
6230 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, J=G%jsc-1:G%jec, i=G%isc:G%iec, G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0)
6240 CS%vhEff(i,J,k) = CS%vhEff(i,J,k) * pa_to_H
625 enddo
626 endif
627 endif
628
6290 if (CS%id_uhEff_2d>0) then
6300 hEff_sum(:,:) = 0.
6310 do k = 1,CS%nsurf-1 ; do j=G%jsc,G%jec ; do i=G%isc-1,G%iec
6320 hEff_sum(i,j) = hEff_sum(i,j) + CS%uhEff(i,j,k)
633 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
6340 call post_data(CS%id_uhEff_2d, hEff_sum, CS%diag)
635 endif
6360 if (CS%id_vhEff_2d>0) then
6370 hEff_sum(:,:) = 0.
6380 do k = 1,CS%nsurf-1 ; do j=G%jsc-1,G%jec ; do i=G%isc,G%iec
6390 hEff_sum(i,j) = hEff_sum(i,j) + CS%vhEff(i,j,k)
640 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
6410 call post_data(CS%id_vhEff_2d, hEff_sum, CS%diag)
642 endif
643
644 !$omp target exit data map(release: k_top, k_bot, zeta_top, zeta_bot, ref_pres)
645
6460end subroutine neutral_diffusion_calc_coeffs
647
648!> Update tracer concentration due to neutral diffusion; layer thickness unchanged by this update.
6490subroutine neutral_diffusion(G, GV, h, Coef_x, Coef_y, dt, Reg, US, CS)
650 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
651 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< ocean vertical grid structure
652 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
653 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(in) :: Coef_x !< dt * Kh * dy / dx at u-points [L2 ~> m2]
654 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(in) :: Coef_y !< dt * Kh * dx / dy at v-points [L2 ~> m2]
655 real, intent(in) :: dt !< Tracer time step * I_numitts [T ~> s]
656 !! (I_numitts is in tracer_hordiff)
657 type(tracer_registry_type), pointer :: Reg !< Tracer registry
658 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
659 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), pointer :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
660
661 ! Local variables
6620 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),CS%nsurf-1) :: uFlx ! Zonal flux of tracer in units that vary between a
663 ! thickness times a concentration ([C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature) or a
664 ! volume or mass times a concentration ([C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg] for temperature),
665 ! depending on the setting of CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct.
6660 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),CS%nsurf-1) :: vFlx ! Meridional flux of tracer in units that vary between a
667 ! thickness times a concentration ([C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature) or a
668 ! volume or mass times a concentration ([C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg] for temperature),
669 ! depending on the setting of CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct.
6700 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)) :: tendency ! tendency array for diagnostics
671 ! [H conc T-1 ~> m conc s-1 or kg m-2 conc s-1]
672 ! For temperature these units are
673 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1].
6740 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: tendency_2d ! Depth integrated content tendency for diagnostics
675 ! [H conc T-1 ~> m conc s-1 or kg m-2 conc s-1].
676 ! For temperature these units are
677 ! [C H T-1 ~> degC m s-1 or degC kg m-2 s-1].
6780 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: trans_x_2d ! Depth integrated diffusive tracer x-transport
679 ! diagnostic. For temperature this has units of
680 ! [C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg].
6810 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: trans_y_2d ! depth integrated diffusive tracer y-transport
682 ! diagnostic. For temperature this has units of
683 ! [C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg].
684 real :: normalize ! normalization used for averaging Coef_x and Coef_y to t-points [nondim].
685
686 type(tracer_type), pointer :: Tracer => NULL() ! Pointer to the current tracer
687
688 integer :: i, j, k, m, ks
689 integer :: nIIB ! The I block size for the zonal faces
690 integer :: njj ! The j block size for the zonal faces
691 integer :: nii ! The i block size for the meridional faces
692 integer :: nJJB ! The J block size for the meridional faces
693 logical :: do_tend ! If true, store the tracer content tendency for diagnostics
694 integer :: ns ! The number of neutral surfaces
695 real :: Idt ! The inverse of the time step [T-1 ~> s-1]
696 real :: h_neglect, h_neglect_edge ! Negligible thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
697
698 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: uFlx, vFlx, tendency, trans_x_2d, trans_y_2d) map(to: Coef_x, Coef_y)
699
7000 h_neglect = GV%H_subroundoff ; h_neglect_edge = GV%H_subroundoff
701
7020 if (.not. CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
7030 if (CS%remap_answer_date < 20190101) then
7040 h_neglect = GV%m_to_H*1.0e-30 ; h_neglect_edge = GV%m_to_H*1.0e-10
705 endif
706 endif
707
7080 if (CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct) then
709 ! Compute Coef at h points
7100 CS%Coef_h(:,:,:) = 0.
7110 do j = G%jsc,G%jec ; do i = G%isc,G%iec
7120 if (G%mask2dT(i,j)>0.) then
713 normalize = 1.0 / ((G%mask2dCu(I-1,j)+G%mask2dCu(I,j)) + &
7140 (G%mask2dCv(i,J-1)+G%mask2dCv(i,J)) + 1.0e-37)
7150 do k = 1, GV%ke+1
716 CS%Coef_h(i,j,k) = normalize*G%mask2dT(i,j)*((Coef_x(I-1,j,k)+Coef_x(I,j,k)) + &
7170 (Coef_y(i,J-1,k)+Coef_y(i,J,k)))
718 enddo
719 endif
720 enddo ; enddo
7210 call pass_var(CS%Coef_h,G%Domain)
722 endif
723
7240 ns = CS%nsurf
7250 nIIB = CS%niblock
7260 if (CS%niblock == 0) nIIB = G%iec - G%isc + 2
7270 njj = CS%njblock
7280 if (CS%njblock == 0) njj = G%jec - G%jsc + 1
7290 nii = CS%niblock
7300 if (CS%niblock == 0) nii = G%iec - G%isc + 1
7310 nJJB = CS%njblock
7320 if (CS%njblock == 0) nJJB = G%jec - G%jsc + 2
733
7340 do m = 1,Reg%ntr ! Loop over tracer registry
735
7360 tracer => Reg%Tr(m)
7370 do_tend = (tracer%id_dfxy_conc > 0 .or. tracer%id_dfxy_cont > 0 .or. tracer%id_dfxy_cont_2d > 0)
738
739 ! for diagnostics
740 if (tracer%id_dfxy_conc > 0 .or. tracer%id_dfxy_cont > 0 .or. tracer%id_dfxy_cont_2d > 0 .or. &
7410 tracer%id_dfx_2d > 0 .or. tracer%id_dfy_2d > 0) then
7420 Idt = 1.0 / dt
7430 tendency(:,:,:) = 0.0
744 !$omp target update to(tendency)
745 endif
746
7470 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, j=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isdB:G%iedB)
7480 uFlx(i,j,k) = 0.
749 enddo
7500 do concurrent (k=1:CS%nsurf-1, j=G%jsdB:G%jedB, i=G%isd:G%ied)
7510 vFlx(i,j,k) = 0.
752 enddo
753
754
755 ! x-flux
756 call zonal_neutral_flux(G, GV, h, tracer%t, CS%nsurf, CS%uPoL, CS%uPoR, CS%uKoL, CS%uKoR, &
757 CS%uhEff, uFlx, h_neglect, nIIB, njj, CS%continuous_reconstruction, CS%tapering, &
7580 CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct, CS%deg, CS%remap_CS, h_neglect_edge, CS%hbl, CS%Coef_h)
759
760 ! y-flux
761 call meridional_neutral_flux(G, GV, h, tracer%t, CS%nsurf, CS%vPoL, CS%vPoR, CS%vKoL, CS%vKoR, &
762 CS%vhEff, vFlx, h_neglect, nii, nJJB, CS%continuous_reconstruction, CS%tapering, &
7630 CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct, CS%deg, CS%remap_CS, h_neglect_edge, CS%hbl, CS%Coef_h)
764
765 ! Update the tracer concentration from divergence of neutral diffusive flux components, noting
766 ! that uFlx and vFlx use an unexpected sign convention.
767 call neutral_flux_divergence(G, GV, h, Coef_x, Coef_y, CS%nsurf, CS%uKoL, CS%uKoR, CS%vKoL, CS%vKoR, &
768 uFlx, vFlx, CS%ndiff_answer_date, tracer%conc_underflow, tracer%t, &
7690 do_tend, tendency, Idt, nii, njj, CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct)
770 !$omp target update from(tendency) if(do_tend)
771
772 ! Do user controlled underflow of the tracer concentrations.
7730 if (tracer%conc_underflow > 0.0) then
774 !$omp target update from(Reg%Tr(m)%t)
7750 do k=1,GV%ke ; do j=G%jsc,G%jec ; do i=G%isc,G%iec
7760 if (abs(tracer%t(i,j,k)) < tracer%conc_underflow) tracer%t(i,j,k) = 0.0
777 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
778 !$omp target update to(Reg%Tr(m)%t)
779 endif
780
781 ! Diagnose vertically summed zonal flux, giving zonal tracer transport from ndiff.
782 ! Note sign corresponds to downgradient flux convention.
7830 if (tracer%id_dfx_2d > 0) then
784
7850 if (CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct) then
7860 do j = G%jsc,G%jec ; do I = G%isc-1,G%iec
7870 trans_x_2d(I,j) = 0.
7880 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j)>0.) then
7890 do ks = 1,CS%nsurf-1
7900 trans_x_2d(I,j) = trans_x_2d(I,j) - uFlx(I,j,ks)
791 enddo
7920 trans_x_2d(I,j) = trans_x_2d(I,j) * Idt
793 endif
794 enddo ; enddo
795 else
7960 do concurrent (j=G%jsc:G%jec, I=G%isc-1:G%iec) DO_LOCALITY(local(ks))
7970 trans_x_2d(I,j) = 0.
7980 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j)>0.) then
7990 do ks = 1,ns-1
8000 trans_x_2d(I,j) = trans_x_2d(I,j) - Coef_x(I,j,1) * uFlx(I,j,ks)
801 enddo
8020 trans_x_2d(I,j) = trans_x_2d(I,j) * Idt
803 endif
804 enddo
805 !$omp target update from(trans_x_2d)
806 endif
807
8080 call post_data(tracer%id_dfx_2d, trans_x_2d(:,:), CS%diag)
809 endif
810
811 ! Diagnose vertically summed merid flux, giving meridional tracer transport from ndiff.
812 ! Note sign corresponds to downgradient flux convention.
8130 if (tracer%id_dfy_2d > 0) then
814
8150 if (CS%KhTh_use_vert_struct) then
8160 do J = G%jsc-1,G%jec ; do i = G%isc,G%iec
8170 trans_y_2d(i,J) = 0.
8180 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J)>0.) then
8190 do ks = 1,CS%nsurf-1
8200 trans_y_2d(i,J) = trans_y_2d(i,J) - vFlx(i,J,ks)
821 enddo
8220 trans_y_2d(i,J) = trans_y_2d(i,J) * Idt
823 endif
824 enddo ; enddo
825 else
8260 do concurrent (J=G%jsc-1:G%jec, i=G%isc:G%iec) DO_LOCALITY(local(ks))
8270 trans_y_2d(i,J) = 0.
8280 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J)>0.) then
8290 do ks = 1,ns-1
8300 trans_y_2d(i,J) = trans_y_2d(i,J) - Coef_y(i,J,1) * vFlx(i,J,ks)
831 enddo
8320 trans_y_2d(i,J) = trans_y_2d(i,J) * Idt
833 endif
834 enddo
835 !$omp target update from(trans_y_2d)
836 endif
837
8380 call post_data(tracer%id_dfy_2d, trans_y_2d(:,:), CS%diag)
839 endif
840
841 ! post tendency of layer-integrated tracer content
8420 if (tracer%id_dfxy_cont > 0) then
8430 call post_data(tracer%id_dfxy_cont, tendency(:,:,:), CS%diag)
844 endif
845
846 ! post depth summed tendency for tracer content
8470 if (tracer%id_dfxy_cont_2d > 0) then
8480 tendency_2d(:,:) = 0.
8490 do j = G%jsc,G%jec ; do i = G%isc,G%iec
8500 do k = 1, GV%ke
8510 tendency_2d(i,j) = tendency_2d(i,j) + tendency(i,j,k)
852 enddo
853 enddo ; enddo
8540 call post_data(tracer%id_dfxy_cont_2d, tendency_2d(:,:), CS%diag)
855 endif
856
857 ! post tendency of tracer concentration; this step must be
858 ! done after posting tracer content tendency, since we alter
859 ! the tendency array.
8600 if (tracer%id_dfxy_conc > 0) then
8610 do k = 1, GV%ke ; do j = G%jsc,G%jec ; do i = G%isc,G%iec
8620 tendency(i,j,k) = tendency(i,j,k) / ( h(i,j,k) + GV%H_subroundoff )
863 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
8640 call post_data(tracer%id_dfxy_conc, tendency, CS%diag)
865 endif
866 enddo ! Loop over tracer registry
867
868 !$omp target exit data map(release: uFlx, vFlx, tendency, trans_x_2d, trans_y_2d, Coef_x, Coef_y)
869
8700end subroutine neutral_diffusion
871
872!> Computes linear tapering coefficients at interfaces of the left and right columns
873!! within a region defined by the boundary layer depths in the two columns.
8740subroutine compute_tapering_coeffs(ne, bld_l, bld_r, coeff_l, coeff_r, h_l, h_r)
875 integer, intent(in) :: ne !< Number of interfaces
876 real, intent(in) :: bld_l !< Boundary layer depth, left column [H ~> m or kg m-2]
877 real, intent(in) :: bld_r !< Boundary layer depth, right column [H ~> m or kg m-2]
878 real, dimension(ne-1), intent(in) :: h_l !< Layer thickness, left column [H ~> m or kg m-2]
879 real, dimension(ne-1), intent(in) :: h_r !< Layer thickness, right column [H ~> m or kg m-2]
880 real, dimension(ne), intent(inout) :: coeff_l !< Tapering coefficient, left column [nondim]
881 real, dimension(ne), intent(inout) :: coeff_r !< Tapering coefficient, right column [nondim]
882
883 ! Local variables
884 real :: min_bld ! Minimum of the boundary layer depth in two adjacent columns [H ~> m or kg m-2]
885 real :: max_bld ! Maximum of the boundary layer depth in two adjacent columns [H ~> m or kg m-2]
886 integer :: dummy1 ! dummy integer
887 real :: dummy2 ! dummy real [nondim]
888 integer :: k_min_l, k_min_r, k_max_l, k_max_r ! Min/max vertical indices in two adjacent columns
889 real :: zeta_l, zeta_r ! dummy variables [nondim]
890 integer :: k ! vertical index
891
892 ! Initialize coefficients
8930 coeff_l(:) = 1.0
8940 coeff_r(:) = 1.0
895
896 ! Calculate vertical indices containing the boundary layer depths
8970 max_bld = MAX(bld_l, bld_r)
8980 min_bld = MIN(bld_l, bld_r)
899
900 ! k_min
901 call boundary_k_range(SURFACE, ne-1, h_l, min_bld, dummy1, dummy2, k_min_l, &
9020 zeta_l)
903 call boundary_k_range(SURFACE, ne-1, h_r, min_bld, dummy1, dummy2, k_min_r, &
9040 zeta_r)
905
906 ! k_max
907 call boundary_k_range(SURFACE, ne-1, h_l, max_bld, dummy1, dummy2, k_max_l, &
9080 zeta_l)
909 call boundary_k_range(SURFACE, ne-1, h_r, max_bld, dummy1, dummy2, k_max_r, &
9100 zeta_r)
911 ! left
9120 do k=1,k_min_l
9130 coeff_l(k) = 0.0
914 enddo
9150 do k=k_min_l+1,k_max_l+1
9160 coeff_l(k) = (real(k - k_min_l) + 1.0)/(real(k_max_l - k_min_l) + 2.0)
917 enddo
918
919 ! right
9200 do k=1,k_min_r
9210 coeff_r(k) = 0.0
922 enddo
9230 do k=k_min_r+1,k_max_r+1
9240 coeff_r(k) = (real(k - k_min_r) + 1.0)/(real(k_max_r - k_min_r) + 2.0)
925 enddo
926
9270end subroutine compute_tapering_coeffs
928
929!> Returns interface scalar, Si, for a column of layer values, S.
9300subroutine interface_scalar(nk, h, S, Si, i_method, h_neglect)
931 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
932 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
933 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: S !< Layer scalar (or concentrations) in arbitrary
934 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
935 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(inout) :: Si !< Interface scalar (or concentrations) in arbitrary
936 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
937 integer, intent(in) :: i_method !< =1 use average of PLM edges
938 !! =2 use continuous PPM edge interpolation
939 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
940 ! Local variables
941 integer :: k, km2, kp1
9420 real, dimension(nk) :: diff ! Difference in scalar concentrations between layer centers in arbitrary
943 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
944 real :: Sb, Sa ! Values of scalar concentrations at the upper and lower edges of a layer in arbitrary
945 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
946
9470 call PLM_diff(nk, h, S, 2, 1, diff)
9480 Si(1) = S(1) - 0.5 * diff(1)
9490 if (i_method==1) then
9500 do k = 2, nk
951 ! Average of the two edge values (will be bounded and,
952 ! when slopes are unlimited, notionally second-order accurate)
9530 Sa = S(k-1) + 0.5 * diff(k-1) ! Lower edge value of a PLM reconstruction for layer above
9540 Sb = S(k) - 0.5 * diff(k) ! Upper edge value of a PLM reconstruction for layer below
9550 Si(k) = 0.5 * ( Sa + Sb )
956 enddo
9570 elseif (i_method==2) then
9580 do k = 2, nk
959 ! PPM quasi-fourth order interpolation for edge values following
960 ! equation 1.6 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
9610 km2 = max(1, k-2)
9620 kp1 = min(nk, k+1)
9630 Si(k) = ppm_edge(h(km2), h(k-1), h(k), h(kp1), S(k-1), S(k), diff(k-1), diff(k), h_neglect)
964 enddo
965 endif
9660 Si(nk+1) = S(nk) + 0.5 * diff(nk)
967
9680end subroutine interface_scalar
969
970!> Returns interface scalar, Si, for a column of layer values, S.
9710subroutine interface_scalar_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, h, S, isdw, iedw, jsdw, jedw, Si, i_method, h_neglect)
972 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G
973 integer, intent(in) :: isb !< This block's starting i index.
974 integer, intent(in) :: ieb !< This block's ending i index.
975 integer, intent(in) :: jsb !< This block's starting j index.
976 integer, intent(in) :: jeb !< This block's ending j index.
977 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
978 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
979 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: S !< Layer scalar (or concentrations) in arbitrary
980 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
981 integer, intent(in) :: isdw !< The lower i bound of the memory of Si
982 integer, intent(in) :: iedw !< The upper i bound of the memory of Si
983 integer, intent(in) :: jsdw !< The lower j bound of the memory of Si
984 integer, intent(in) :: jedw !< The upper j bound of the memory of Si
985 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk+1), intent(inout) :: Si !< Interface scalar (or concentrations) in arbitrary
986 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
987 integer, intent(in) :: i_method !< =1 use average of PLM edges
988 !! =2 use continuous PPM edge interpolation
989 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
990 ! Local variables
991 integer :: i, j, k, km2, kp1
9920 real, dimension(isb:ieb,jsb:jeb,nk) :: diff ! Difference in scalar concentrations between layer centers in arbitrary
993 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
994 real :: Sb, Sa ! Values of scalar concentrations at the upper and lower edges of a layer in arbitrary
995 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
996
997 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: diff)
998
9990 call PLM_diff_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, h, S, 2, 1, diff)
1000
10010 do concurrent (j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb)
10020 Si(i,j,1) = S(i,j,1) - 0.5 * diff(i,j,1)
1003 enddo
1004
10050 if (i_method==1) then
10060 do concurrent (k=2:nk, j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb) DO_LOCALITY(local(Sa, Sb))
1007 ! Average of the two edge values (will be bounded and,
1008 ! when slopes are unlimited, notionally second-order accurate)
10090 Sa = S(i,j,k-1) + 0.5 * diff(i,j,k-1) ! Lower edge value of a PLM reconstruction for layer above
10100 Sb = S(i,j,k) - 0.5 * diff(i,j,k) ! Upper edge value of a PLM reconstruction for layer below
10110 Si(i,j,k) = 0.5 * ( Sa + Sb )
1012 enddo
10130 elseif (i_method==2) then
10140 do concurrent (k=2:nk, j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb) DO_LOCALITY(local(km2, kp1))
1015 ! PPM quasi-fourth order interpolation for edge values following
1016 ! equation 1.6 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
10170 km2 = max(1, k-2)
10180 kp1 = min(nk, k+1)
10190 Si(i,j,k) = ppm_edge(h(i,j,km2), h(i,j,k-1), h(i,j,k), h(i,j,kp1), S(i,j,k-1), S(i,j,k), diff(i,j,k-1), diff(i,j,k), h_neglect)
1020 enddo
1021 endif
1022
10230 do concurrent (j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb)
10240 Si(i,j,nk+1) = S(i,j,nk) + 0.5 * diff(i,j,nk)
1025 enddo
1026
1027 !$omp target exit data map(release: diff)
1028
10290end subroutine interface_scalar_block
1030
1031!> Returns the PPM quasi-fourth order edge value at k+1/2 following
1032!! equation 1.6 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
1033!! The returned units are the same as those of Ak (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
10340pure real function ppm_edge(hkm1, hk, hkp1, hkp2, Ak, Akp1, Pk, Pkp1, h_neglect)
1035 real, intent(in) :: hkm1 !< Width of cell k-1 in [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1036 real, intent(in) :: hk !< Width of cell k in [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1037 real, intent(in) :: hkp1 !< Width of cell k+1 in [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1038 real, intent(in) :: hkp2 !< Width of cell k+2 in [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1039 real, intent(in) :: Ak !< Average scalar value of cell k in arbitrary concentration
1040 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1041 real, intent(in) :: Akp1 !< Average scalar value of cell k+1 in arbitrary concentration
1042 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1043 real, intent(in) :: Pk !< PLM slope for cell k in arbitrary concentration
1044 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1045 real, intent(in) :: Pkp1 !< PLM slope for cell k+1 in arbitrary concentration
1046 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1047 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1048
1049 ! Local variables
1050 real :: R_hk_hkp1, R_2hk_hkp1, R_hk_2hkp1 ! Reciprocals of combinations of thicknesses [H-1 ~> m-1 or m2 kg-1]
1051 real :: f1 ! A work variable with units of an inverse cell width [H-1 ~> m-1 or m2 kg-1]
1052 real :: f2, f3, f4 ! Work variables with units of the cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1053
10540 R_hk_hkp1 = hk + hkp1
10550 if (R_hk_hkp1 <= 0.) then
10560 ppm_edge = 0.5 * ( Ak + Akp1 )
10570 return
1058 endif
10590 R_hk_hkp1 = 1. / R_hk_hkp1
10600 if (hk<hkp1) then
10610 ppm_edge = Ak + ( hk * R_hk_hkp1 ) * ( Akp1 - Ak )
1062 else
10630 ppm_edge = Akp1 + ( hkp1 * R_hk_hkp1 ) * ( Ak - Akp1 )
1064 endif
1065
10660 R_2hk_hkp1 = 1. / ( ( 2. * hk + hkp1 ) + h_neglect )
10670 R_hk_2hkp1 = 1. / ( ( hk + 2. * hkp1 ) + h_neglect )
10680 f1 = 1./ ( ( hk + hkp1) + ( hkm1 + hkp2 ) )
1069 f2 = 2. * ( hkp1 * hk ) * R_hk_hkp1 * &
10700 ( ( hkm1 + hk ) * R_2hk_hkp1 - ( hkp2 + hkp1 ) * R_hk_2hkp1 )
10710 f3 = hk * ( hkm1 + hk ) * R_2hk_hkp1
10720 f4 = hkp1 * ( hkp1 + hkp2 ) * R_hk_2hkp1
1073
10740 ppm_edge = ppm_edge + f1 * ( f2 * ( Akp1 - Ak ) - ( f3 * Pkp1 - f4 * Pk ) )
1075
10760end function ppm_edge
1077
1078!> Returns the average of a PPM reconstruction between two fractional positions in the same
1079!! arbitrary concentration units as aMean (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
10800real function ppm_ave(xL, xR, aL, aR, aMean)
1081 real, intent(in) :: xL !< Fraction position of left bound (0,1) [nondim]
1082 real, intent(in) :: xR !< Fraction position of right bound (0,1) [nondim]
1083 real, intent(in) :: aL !< Left edge scalar value, at x=0, in arbitrary concentration
1084 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1085 real, intent(in) :: aR !< Right edge scalar value, at x=1 in arbitrary concentration
1086 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1087 real, intent(in) :: aMean !< Average scalar value of cell in arbitrary concentration
1088 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1089
1090 ! Local variables
1091 real :: dx ! Distance between the bounds [nondim]
1092
10930 dx = xR - xL
1094
10950 if (dx<0.) then
10960 stop 'ppm_ave: dx<0 should not happened!'
10970 elseif (dx>1.) then
10980 stop 'ppm_ave: dx>1 should not happened!'
1099 endif
11000 ppm_ave = ppm_ave_unchecked(xL, xR, aL, aR, aMean)
11010end function ppm_ave
1102
1103!> Returns the average of a PPM reconstruction between two fractional positions in the same
1104!! arbitrary concentration units as aMean (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature), without
1105!! checking that 0 <= xR - xL <= 1 as ppm_ave does
11060pure real function ppm_ave_unchecked(xL, xR, aL, aR, aMean)
1107 real, intent(in) :: xL !< Fraction position of left bound (0,1) [nondim]
1108 real, intent(in) :: xR !< Fraction position of right bound (0,1) [nondim]
1109 real, intent(in) :: aL !< Left edge scalar value, at x=0, in arbitrary concentration
1110 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1111 real, intent(in) :: aR !< Right edge scalar value, at x=1 in arbitrary concentration
1112 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1113 real, intent(in) :: aMean !< Average scalar value of cell in arbitrary concentration
1114 !! units (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1115 !$omp declare target
1116
1117 ! Local variables
1118 real :: dx ! Distance between the bounds [nondim]
1119 real :: xave ! Average fractional position [nondim]
1120 real :: a6, a6o3 ! Terms proportional to the normalized scalar curvature in the same arbitrary
1121 ! concentration units as aMean (e.g. usually [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1122
11230 dx = xR - xL
11240 xave = 0.5 * ( xR + xL )
11250 a6o3 = 2. * aMean - ( aL + aR ) ! a6 / 3.
11260 a6 = 3. * a6o3
1127
11280 if (dx==0.) then
11290 ppm_ave_unchecked = aL + ( aR - aL ) * xR + a6 * xR * ( 1. - xR )
1130 else
11310 ppm_ave_unchecked = ( aL + xave * ( ( aR - aL ) + a6 ) ) - a6o3 * ( xR**2 + xR * xL + xL**2 )
1132 endif
11330end function ppm_ave_unchecked
1134
1135!> A true signum function that returns either -abs(a), when x<0; or abs(a) when x>0; or 0 when x=0.
1136!! The returned units are the same as those of a [arbitrary].
11370pure real function signum(a,x)
1138 real, intent(in) :: a !< The magnitude argument in arbitrary units [arbitrary]
1139 real, intent(in) :: x !< The sign (or zero) argument [arbitrary]
1140
11410 signum = sign(a,x)
11420 if (x==0.) signum = 0.
1143
11440end function signum
1145
1146!> Returns PLM slopes for a column where the slopes are the difference in value across each cell.
1147!! The limiting follows equation 1.8 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
11480subroutine PLM_diff(nk, h, S, c_method, b_method, diff)
1149 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
1150 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1151 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: S !< Layer salinity (conc, e.g. ppt) or other tracer
1152 !! concentration in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
1153 integer, intent(in) :: c_method !< Method to use for the centered difference
1154 integer, intent(in) :: b_method !< =1, use PCM in first/last cell, =2 uses linear extrapolation
1155 real, dimension(nk), intent(inout) :: diff !< Scalar difference across layer (conc, e.g. ppt)
1156 !! in the same arbitrary units as S [A ~> a],
1157 !! determined by the following values for c_method:
1158 !! 1. Second order finite difference (not recommended)
1159 !! 2. Second order finite volume (used in original PPM)
1160 !! 3. Finite-volume weighted least squares linear fit
1161 !! \todo The use of c_method to choose a scheme is inefficient
1162 !! and should eventually be moved up the call tree.
1163
1164 ! Local variables
1165 integer :: k
1166 real :: hkm1, hk, hkp1 ! Successive layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1167 real :: Skm1, Sk, Skp1 ! Successive layer tracer concentrations in the same arbitrary units as S [A ~> a]
1168 real :: diff_l, diff_r, diff_c ! Differences in tracer concentrations in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
1169
11700 do k = 2, nk-1
11710 hkm1 = h(k-1)
11720 hk = h(k)
11730 hkp1 = h(k+1)
1174
11750 if ( ( hkp1 + hk ) * ( hkm1 + hk ) > 0.) then
11760 Skm1 = S(k-1)
11770 Sk = S(k)
11780 Skp1 = S(k+1)
11790 if (c_method==1) then
1180 ! Simple centered diff (from White)
11810 if ( hk + 0.5 * (hkm1 + hkp1) /= 0. ) then
11820 diff_c = ( Skp1 - Skm1 ) * ( hk / ( hk + 0.5 * (hkm1 + hkp1) ) )
1183 else
11840 diff_c = 0.
1185 endif
11860 elseif (c_method==2) then
1187 ! Second order accurate centered finite-volume slope (from Colella and Woodward, JCP 1984)
11880 diff_c = fv_diff(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
11890 elseif (c_method==3) then
1190 ! Second order accurate finite-volume least squares slope
11910 diff_c = hk * fvlsq_slope(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
1192 endif
1193 ! Limit centered slope by twice the side differenced slopes
11940 diff_l = 2. * ( Sk - Skm1 )
11950 diff_r = 2. * ( Skp1 - Sk )
11960 if ( signum(1., diff_l) * signum(1., diff_r) <= 0. ) then
11970 diff(k) = 0. ! PCM for local extrema
1198 else
11990 diff(k) = sign( min( abs(diff_l), abs(diff_c), abs(diff_r) ), diff_c )
1200 endif
1201 else
12020 diff(k) = 0. ! PCM next to vanished layers
1203 endif
1204 enddo
12050 if (b_method==1) then ! PCM for top and bottom layer
12060 diff(1) = 0.
12070 diff(nk) = 0.
12080 elseif (b_method==2) then ! Linear extrapolation for top and bottom interfaces
12090 diff(1) = ( S(2) - S(1) ) * 2. * ( h(1) / ( h(1) + h(2) ) )
12100 diff(nk) = S(nk) - S(nk-1) * 2. * ( h(nk) / ( h(nk-1) + h(nk) ) )
1211 endif
1212
12130end subroutine PLM_diff
1214
1215!> Returns PLM slopes for a column where the slopes are the difference in value across each cell.
1216!! The limiting follows equation 1.8 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
12170subroutine PLM_diff_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, h, S, c_method, b_method, diff)
1218 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G
1219 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
1220 integer, intent(in) :: isb !< The starting i-index of this block
1221 integer, intent(in) :: ieb !< The last i-index of this block
1222 integer, intent(in) :: jsb !< The starting j-index of this block
1223 integer, intent(in) :: jeb !< The last j-index of this block
1224 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1225 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: S !< Layer salinity (conc, e.g. ppt) or other tracer
1226 !! concentration in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
1227 integer, intent(in) :: c_method !< Method to use for the centered difference
1228 integer, intent(in) :: b_method !< =1, use PCM in first/last cell, =2 uses linear extrapolation
1229 real, dimension(isb:ieb,jsb:jeb,nk), intent(inout) :: diff !< Scalar difference across layer (conc, e.g. ppt)
1230 !! in the same arbitrary units as S [A ~> a],
1231 !! determined by the following values for c_method:
1232 !! 1. Second order finite difference (not recommended)
1233 !! 2. Second order finite volume (used in original PPM)
1234 !! 3. Finite-volume weighted least squares linear fit
1235 !! \todo The use of c_method to choose a scheme is inefficient
1236 !! and should eventually be moved up the call tree.
1237
1238 ! Local variables
1239 integer :: i, j, k
1240 real :: hkm1, hk, hkp1 ! Successive layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units
1241 real :: Skm1, Sk, Skp1 ! Successive layer tracer concentrations in the same arbitrary units as S [A ~> a]
1242 real :: diff_l, diff_r, diff_c ! Differences in tracer concentrations in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
1243
12440 do concurrent (j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb)
12450 if (b_method == 1) then
12460 diff(i,j,1) = 0.0
12470 elseif (b_method == 2) then
12480 diff(i,j,1) = ( S(i,j,2) - S(i,j,1) ) * 2. * ( h(i,j,1) / ( h(i,j,1) + h(i,j,2) ) )
1249 endif
1250 enddo
1251
1252 do concurrent (k=2:nk-1, j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb) &
12530 DO_LOCALITY(local(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1, diff_l, diff_r, diff_c))
12540 hkm1 = h(i,j,k-1)
12550 hk = h(i,j,k)
12560 hkp1 = h(i,j,k+1)
1257
12580 if ( ( hkp1 + hk ) * ( hkm1 + hk ) > 0.) then
12590 Skm1 = S(i,j,k-1)
12600 Sk = S(i,j,k)
12610 Skp1 = S(i,j,k+1)
12620 if (c_method==1) then
1263 ! Simple centered diff (from White)
12640 if ( hk + 0.5 * (hkm1 + hkp1) /= 0. ) then
12650 diff_c = ( Skp1 - Skm1 ) * ( hk / ( hk + 0.5 * (hkm1 + hkp1) ) )
1266 else
12670 diff_c = 0.
1268 endif
12690 elseif (c_method==2) then
1270 ! Second order accurate centered finite-volume slope (from Colella and Woodward, JCP 1984)
12710 diff_c = fv_diff(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
12720 elseif (c_method==3) then
1273 ! Second order accurate finite-volume least squares slope
12740 diff_c = hk * fvlsq_slope(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
1275 endif
1276 ! Limit centered slope by twice the side differenced slopes
12770 diff_l = 2. * ( Sk - Skm1 )
12780 diff_r = 2. * ( Skp1 - Sk )
12790 if ( signum(1., diff_l) * signum(1., diff_r) <= 0. ) then
12800 diff(i,j,k) = 0. ! PCM for local extrema
1281 else
12820 diff(i,j,k) = sign( min( abs(diff_l), abs(diff_c), abs(diff_r) ), diff_c )
1283 endif
1284 else
12850 diff(i,j,k) = 0. ! PCM next to vanished layers
1286 endif
1287 enddo
1288
12890 do concurrent (j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb)
12900 if (b_method == 1) then
12910 diff(i,j,nk) = 0.0
12920 elseif (b_method == 2) then
12930 diff(i,j,nk) = S(i,j,nk) - S(i,j,nk-1) * 2. * ( h(i,j,nk) / ( h(i,j,nk-1) + h(i,j,nk) ) )
1294 endif
1295 enddo
1296
12970end subroutine PLM_diff_block
1298
1299!> Returns the cell-centered second-order finite volume (unlimited PLM) slope using three
1300!! consecutive cell widths and average values. Slope is returned as a difference across
1301!! the central cell (i.e. units of scalar S, e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
1302!! Discretization follows equation 1.7 in Colella & Woodward, 1984: JCP 54, 174-201.
13030pure real function fv_diff(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
1304 real, intent(in) :: hkm1 !< Left cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1305 real, intent(in) :: hk !< Center cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1306 real, intent(in) :: hkp1 !< Right cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1307 real, intent(in) :: Skm1 !< Left cell average value in arbitrary concentration
1308 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1309 real, intent(in) :: Sk !< Center cell average value in arbitrary concentration
1310 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1311 real, intent(in) :: Skp1 !< Right cell average value in arbitrary concentration
1312 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1313
1314 ! Local variables
1315 real :: h_sum, hp, hm ! At first sums of thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2], then changed into
1316 ! their reciprocals [H-1 ~> m-1 or m2 kg-1]
1317
13180 h_sum = ( hkm1 + hkp1 ) + hk
13190 if (h_sum /= 0.) h_sum = 1./ h_sum
13200 hm = hkm1 + hk
13210 if (hm /= 0.) hm = 1./ hm
13220 hp = hkp1 + hk
13230 if (hp /= 0.) hp = 1./ hp
1324 fv_diff = ( hk * h_sum ) * &
1325 ( ( 2. * hkm1 + hk ) * hp * ( Skp1 - Sk ) &
13260 + ( 2. * hkp1 + hk ) * hm * ( Sk - Skm1 ) )
13270end function fv_diff
1328
1329
1330!> Returns the cell-centered second-order weighted least squares slope
1331!! using three consecutive cell widths and average values. Slope is returned
1332!! as a gradient (i.e. units of scalar S over width units). For example, for temperature
1333!! fvlsq_slope would usually be returned in units of [C H-1 ~> degC m-1 or degC m2 kg-1].
13340pure real function fvlsq_slope(hkm1, hk, hkp1, Skm1, Sk, Skp1)
1335 real, intent(in) :: hkm1 !< Left cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1336 real, intent(in) :: hk !< Center cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1337 real, intent(in) :: hkp1 !< Right cell width [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1338 real, intent(in) :: Skm1 !< Left cell average value in arbitrary concentration
1339 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1340 real, intent(in) :: Sk !< Center cell average value often in arbitrary concentration
1341 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1342 real, intent(in) :: Skp1 !< Right cell average value often in arbitrary concentration
1343 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
1344
1345 ! Local variables
1346 real :: xkm1, xkp1 ! Distances between layer centers [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1347 real :: h_sum ! Sum of the successive cell widths [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other arbitrary units
1348 real :: hx_sum ! Thicknesses times distances [H2 ~> m2 or kg2 m-4]
1349 real :: hxsq_sum ! Thicknesses times squared distances [H3 ~> m3 or kg3 m-6]
1350 real :: det ! The denominator in the weighted slope calculation [H4 ~> m4 or kg4 m-8]
1351 real :: hxy_sum ! Sum of layer concentrations times thicknesses and distances in units that
1352 ! depend on those of Sk (e.g. [C H2 ~> degC m2 or degC kg2 m-4] for temperature)
1353 real :: hy_sum ! Sum of layer concentrations times thicknesses in units that depend on
1354 ! those of Sk (e.g. [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature)
1355
13560 xkm1 = -0.5 * ( hk + hkm1 )
13570 xkp1 = 0.5 * ( hk + hkp1 )
13580 h_sum = ( hkm1 + hkp1 ) + hk
13590 hx_sum = hkm1*xkm1 + hkp1*xkp1
13600 hxsq_sum = hkm1*(xkm1**2) + hkp1*(xkp1**2)
13610 hxy_sum = hkm1*xkm1*Skm1 + hkp1*xkp1*Skp1
13620 hy_sum = ( hkm1*Skm1 + hkp1*Skp1 ) + hk*Sk
13630 det = h_sum * hxsq_sum - hx_sum**2
13640 if (det /= 0.) then
1365 !a = ( hxsq_sum * hy_sum - hx_sum*hxy_sum ) / det ! a would be mean of straight line fit
13660 fvlsq_slope = ( h_sum * hxy_sum - hx_sum*hy_sum ) / det ! Gradient of straight line fit
1367 else
13680 fvlsq_slope = 0. ! Adcroft's reciprocal rule
1369 endif
13700end function fvlsq_slope
1371
1372
1373!> Returns positions within left/right columns of combined interfaces using continuous reconstructions of T/S
13740subroutine find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous(nk, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, Pr, Tr, Sr, &
13750 dRdTr, dRdSr, PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
1376 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
1377 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Pl !< Left-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
1378 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
1379 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Sl !< Left-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
1380 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTl !< Left-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
1381 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSl !< Left-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
1382 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Pr !< Right-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
1383 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
1384 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Sr !< Right-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
1385 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTr !< Left-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
1386 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSr !< Left-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
1387 real, dimension(2*nk+2), intent(inout) :: PoL !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
1388 !! layer KoL of left column [nondim]
1389 real, dimension(2*nk+2), intent(inout) :: PoR !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
1390 !! layer KoR of right column [nondim]
1391 integer, dimension(2*nk+2), intent(inout) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
1392 integer, dimension(2*nk+2), intent(inout) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
1393 real, dimension(2*nk+1), intent(inout) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral surfaces
1394 !! [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units following Pl and Pr.
1395 integer, optional, intent(in) :: bl_kl !< Layer index of the boundary layer (left)
1396 integer, optional, intent(in) :: bl_kr !< Layer index of the boundary layer (right)
1397 real, optional, intent(in) :: bl_zl !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (left) [nondim]
1398 real, optional, intent(in) :: bl_zr !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (right) [nondim]
1399
1400 ! Local variables
1401 integer :: ns ! Number of neutral surfaces
1402 integer :: k_surface ! Index of neutral surface
1403 integer :: kl ! Index of left interface
1404 integer :: kr ! Index of right interface
1405 logical :: searching_left_column ! True if searching for the position of a right interface in the left column
1406 logical :: searching_right_column ! True if searching for the position of a left interface in the right column
1407 logical :: reached_bottom ! True if one of the bottom-most interfaces has been used as the target
1408 integer :: krm1, klm1
1409 real :: dRho, dRhoTop, dRhoBot ! Potential density differences at various points [R ~> kg m-3]
1410 real :: hL, hR ! Pressure thicknesses [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
1411 integer :: lastK_left, lastK_right ! Layers used during the last iteration
1412 real :: lastP_left, lastP_right ! Fractional positions during the last iteration [nondim]
1413 logical :: interior_limit
1414
14150 ns = 2*nk+2
1416
1417 ! Initialize variables for the search
14180 kr = 1
14190 kl = 1
14200 lastP_right = 0.
14210 lastP_left = 0.
14220 lastK_right = 1
14230 lastK_left = 1
14240 reached_bottom = .false.
1425
1426 ! Check to see if we should limit the diffusion to the interior
14270 interior_limit = PRESENT(bl_kl) .and. PRESENT(bl_kr) .and. PRESENT(bl_zr) .and. PRESENT(bl_zl)
1428
1429 ! Loop over each neutral surface, working from top to bottom
14300 neutral_surfaces: do k_surface = 1, ns
14310 klm1 = max(kl-1, 1)
14320 if (klm1>nk) stop 'find_neutral_surface_positions(): klm1 went out of bounds!'
14330 krm1 = max(kr-1, 1)
14340 if (krm1>nk) stop 'find_neutral_surface_positions(): krm1 went out of bounds!'
1435
1436 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr) - rho(kl)
1437 dRho = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(kr) + dRdTl(kl) ) * ( Tr(kr) - Tl(kl) ) &
14380 + ( dRdSr(kr) + dRdSl(kl) ) * ( Sr(kr) - Sl(kl) ) )
1439 ! Which column has the lighter surface for the current indexes, kr and kl
14400 if (.not. reached_bottom) then
14410 if (dRho < 0.) then
14420 searching_left_column = .true.
14430 searching_right_column = .false.
14440 elseif (dRho > 0.) then
14450 searching_right_column = .true.
14460 searching_left_column = .false.
1447 else ! dRho == 0.
14480 if (kl + kr == 2) then ! Still at surface
14490 searching_left_column = .true.
14500 searching_right_column = .false.
1451 else ! Not the surface so we simply change direction
14520 searching_left_column = .not. searching_left_column
14530 searching_right_column = .not. searching_right_column
1454 endif
1455 endif
1456 endif
1457
14580 if (searching_left_column) then
1459 ! Interpolate for the neutral surface position within the left column, layer klm1
1460 ! Potential density difference, rho(kl-1) - rho(kr) (should be negative)
1461 dRhoTop = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTl(klm1) + dRdTr(kr) ) * ( Tl(klm1) - Tr(kr) ) &
14620 + ( dRdSl(klm1) + dRdSr(kr) ) * ( Sl(klm1) - Sr(kr) ) )
1463 ! Potential density difference, rho(kl) - rho(kr) (will be positive)
1464 dRhoBot = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTl(klm1+1) + dRdTr(kr) ) * ( Tl(klm1+1) - Tr(kr) ) &
14650 + ( dRdSl(klm1+1) + dRdSr(kr) ) * ( Sl(klm1+1) - Sr(kr) ) )
1466
1467 ! Because we are looking left, the right surface, kr, is lighter than klm1+1 and should be denser than klm1
1468 ! unless we are still at the top of the left column (kl=1)
14690 if (dRhoTop > 0. .or. kr+kl==2) then
14700 PoL(k_surface) = 0. ! The right surface is lighter than anything in layer klm1
14710 elseif (dRhoTop >= dRhoBot) then ! Left layer is unstratified
14720 PoL(k_surface) = 1.
1473 else
1474 ! Linearly interpolate for the position between Pl(kl-1) and Pl(kl) where the density difference
1475 ! between right and left is zero. The Pl here are only used to handle massless layers.
14760 PoL(k_surface) = interpolate_for_nondim_position( dRhoTop, Pl(klm1), dRhoBot, Pl(klm1+1) )
1477 endif
14780 if (PoL(k_surface)>=1. .and. klm1<nk) then ! >= is really ==, when PoL==1 we point to the bottom of the cell
14790 klm1 = klm1 + 1
14800 PoL(k_surface) = PoL(k_surface) - 1.
1481 endif
14820 if (real(klm1-lastK_left)+(PoL(k_surface)-lastP_left)<0.) then
14830 PoL(k_surface) = lastP_left
14840 klm1 = lastK_left
1485 endif
14860 KoL(k_surface) = klm1
14870 if (kr <= nk) then
14880 PoR(k_surface) = 0.
14890 KoR(k_surface) = kr
1490 else
14910 PoR(k_surface) = 1.
14920 KoR(k_surface) = nk
1493 endif
14940 if (kr <= nk) then
14950 kr = kr + 1
1496 else
14970 reached_bottom = .true.
14980 searching_right_column = .true.
14990 searching_left_column = .false.
1500 endif
15010 elseif (searching_right_column) then
1502 ! Interpolate for the neutral surface position within the right column, layer krm1
1503 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr-1) - rho(kl) (should be negative)
1504 dRhoTop = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(krm1) + dRdTl(kl) ) * ( Tr(krm1) - Tl(kl) ) + &
15050 ( dRdSr(krm1) + dRdSl(kl) ) * ( Sr(krm1) - Sl(kl) ) )
1506 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr) - rho(kl) (will be positive)
1507 dRhoBot = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(krm1+1) + dRdTl(kl) ) * ( Tr(krm1+1) - Tl(kl) ) + &
15080 ( dRdSr(krm1+1) + dRdSl(kl) ) * ( Sr(krm1+1) - Sl(kl) ) )
1509
1510 ! Because we are looking right, the left surface, kl, is lighter than krm1+1 and should be denser than krm1
1511 ! unless we are still at the top of the right column (kr=1)
15120 if (dRhoTop >= 0. .or. kr+kl==2) then
15130 PoR(k_surface) = 0. ! The left surface is lighter than anything in layer krm1
15140 elseif (dRhoTop >= dRhoBot) then ! Right layer is unstratified
15150 PoR(k_surface) = 1.
1516 else
1517 ! Linearly interpolate for the position between Pr(kr-1) and Pr(kr) where the density difference
1518 ! between right and left is zero. The Pr here are only used to handle massless layers.
15190 PoR(k_surface) = interpolate_for_nondim_position( dRhoTop, Pr(krm1), dRhoBot, Pr(krm1+1) )
1520 endif
15210 if (PoR(k_surface)>=1. .and. krm1<nk) then ! >= is really ==, when PoR==1 we point to the bottom of the cell
15220 krm1 = krm1 + 1
15230 PoR(k_surface) = PoR(k_surface) - 1.
1524 endif
15250 if (real(krm1-lastK_right)+(PoR(k_surface)-lastP_right)<0.) then
15260 PoR(k_surface) = lastP_right
15270 krm1 = lastK_right
1528 endif
15290 KoR(k_surface) = krm1
15300 if (kl <= nk) then
15310 PoL(k_surface) = 0.
15320 KoL(k_surface) = kl
1533 else
15340 PoL(k_surface) = 1.
15350 KoL(k_surface) = nk
1536 endif
15370 if (kl <= nk) then
15380 kl = kl + 1
1539 else
15400 reached_bottom = .true.
15410 searching_right_column = .false.
15420 searching_left_column = .true.
1543 endif
1544 else
15450 stop 'Else what?'
1546 endif
15470 if (interior_limit) then
15480 if (KoL(k_surface)<=bl_kl) then
15490 KoL(k_surface) = bl_kl
15500 if (PoL(k_surface)<bl_zl) then
15510 PoL(k_surface) = bl_zl
1552 endif
1553 endif
15540 if (KoR(k_surface)<=bl_kr) then
15550 KoR(k_surface) = bl_kr
15560 if (PoR(k_surface)<bl_zr) then
15570 PoR(k_surface) = bl_zr
1558 endif
1559 endif
1560 endif
1561
15620 lastK_left = KoL(k_surface) ; lastP_left = PoL(k_surface)
15630 lastK_right = KoR(k_surface) ; lastP_right = PoR(k_surface)
1564 ! Effective thickness
1565 ! NOTE: This would be better expressed in terms of the layers thicknesses rather
1566 ! than as differences of position - AJA
15670 if (k_surface>1) then
15680 hL = absolute_position(nk,ns,Pl,KoL,PoL,k_surface) - absolute_position(nk,ns,Pl,KoL,PoL,k_surface-1)
15690 hR = absolute_position(nk,ns,Pr,KoR,PoR,k_surface) - absolute_position(nk,ns,Pr,KoR,PoR,k_surface-1)
15700 if ( hL + hR > 0.) then
15710 hEff(k_surface-1) = 2. * hL * hR / ( hL + hR ) ! Harmonic mean of layer thicknesses
1572 else
15730 hEff(k_surface-1) = 0.
1574 endif
1575 endif
1576
1577 enddo neutral_surfaces
1578
15790end subroutine find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous
1580
1581!> Finds the positions of the neutral surfaces between the pairs of columns on either side of a block of
1582!! faces, with continuous reconstructions, as find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous does for one face,
1583!! stepping all of the faces through the neutral surfaces together. The columns are passed in block-sized
1584!! arrays, so the same search serves the zonal and the meridional faces. Instead of stopping, the search
1585!! returns in ierr the largest code of the failures that find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous stops on:
1586!! 1 (klm1 out of bounds), 2 (krm1 out of bounds), 3 (no column to search), 4 (from
1587!! interpolate_for_nondim_position) and 5 (from absolute_position).
15880subroutine neutral_positions_block(nIb, nJb, IIe, JJe, nk, ns, do_face, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, &
15890 Pr, Tr, Sr, dRdTr, dRdSr, interior_limit, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr, &
15900 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, ierr)
1591 integer, intent(in) :: nIb !< The first dimension of the block-sized arrays
1592 integer, intent(in) :: nJb !< The second dimension of the block-sized arrays
1593 integer, intent(in) :: IIe !< The number of faces of this block in the first dimension
1594 integer, intent(in) :: JJe !< The number of faces of this block in the second dimension
1595 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
1596 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
1597 logical, dimension(nIb,nJb), intent(in) :: do_face !< True for the faces to search
1598 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Pl !< Left-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
1599 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
1600 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Sl !< Left-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
1601 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTl !< Left-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
1602 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSl !< Left-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
1603 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Pr !< Right-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
1604 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
1605 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: Sr !< Right-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
1606 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTr !< Right-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
1607 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSr !< Right-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
1608 logical, intent(in) :: interior_limit !< If true, limit the neutral surfaces
1609 !! to below the boundary layers given by bl_*
1610 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb), intent(in) :: bl_kl !< Layer index of the boundary layer (left)
1611 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb), intent(in) :: bl_kr !< Layer index of the boundary layer (right)
1612 real, dimension(nIb,nJb), intent(in) :: bl_zl !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (left) [nondim]
1613 real, dimension(nIb,nJb), intent(in) :: bl_zr !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (right) [nondim]
1614 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,ns), intent(inout) :: PoL !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
1615 !! layer KoL of left column [nondim]
1616 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,ns), intent(inout) :: PoR !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
1617 !! layer KoR of right column [nondim]
1618 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb,ns), intent(inout) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
1619 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb,ns), intent(inout) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
1620 real, dimension(nIb,nJb,ns-1), intent(inout) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral surfaces
1621 !! [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units following Pl and Pr.
1622 integer, intent(out) :: ierr !< 0, or the largest code of the failures found
1623
1624 ! Local variables
1625 ! The search state of each face of the block, carried from one neutral surface to the next
16260 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: kl_b, kr_b ! Indices of the left and right interfaces
16270 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: lastK_left_b, lastK_right_b ! Layers used during the last step
16280 real, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: lastP_left_b, lastP_right_b ! Fractional positions during the last step [nondim]
16290 logical, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: reached_bottom_b ! True if one of the bottom-most interfaces has been used
16300 logical, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: searching_left_b ! True if searching for a position in the left column
16310 logical, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: searching_right_b ! True if searching for a position in the right column
16320 integer, dimension(nIb,nJb) :: err_b ! The largest failure code found for each face
1633 integer :: k_surface ! Index of neutral surface
1634 integer :: kl ! Index of left interface
1635 integer :: kr ! Index of right interface
1636 logical :: searching_left_column ! True if searching for the position of a right interface in the left column
1637 logical :: searching_right_column ! True if searching for the position of a left interface in the right column
1638 logical :: reached_bottom ! True if one of the bottom-most interfaces has been used as the target
1639 integer :: krm1, klm1
1640 real :: dRho, dRhoTop, dRhoBot ! Potential density differences at various points [R ~> kg m-3]
1641 real :: hL, hR ! Pressure thicknesses [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
1642 integer :: lastK_left, lastK_right ! Layers used during the last iteration
1643 real :: lastP_left, lastP_right ! Fractional positions during the last iteration [nondim]
1644 real :: pos ! A fractional position from interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked [nondim]
1645 logical :: bad ! True if interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked failed
1646 integer :: err ! The largest failure code found for a face
1647 integer :: kt, kb ! Layers of the top and bottom neutral surfaces of a neutral layer
1648 integer :: II, JJ
1649
1650 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: kl_b, kr_b, lastK_left_b, lastK_right_b, lastP_left_b, lastP_right_b, &
1651 !$omp reached_bottom_b, searching_left_b, searching_right_b, err_b)
1652
1653 ! Initialize variables for the search
16540 do concurrent (JJ=1:JJe, II=1:IIe)
16550 kr_b(II,JJ) = 1
16560 kl_b(II,JJ) = 1
16570 lastP_right_b(II,JJ) = 0.
16580 lastP_left_b(II,JJ) = 0.
16590 lastK_right_b(II,JJ) = 1
16600 lastK_left_b(II,JJ) = 1
16610 reached_bottom_b(II,JJ) = .false.
16620 searching_left_b(II,JJ) = .false.
16630 searching_right_b(II,JJ) = .false.
16640 err_b(II,JJ) = 0
1665 enddo
1666
1667 ! Loop over each neutral surface, working from top to bottom
16680 do concurrent (JJ=1:JJe) DO_LOCALITY(local(k_surface))
16690 do k_surface = 1, ns
1670 do concurrent (II=1:IIe, do_face(II,JJ) .and. (err_b(II,JJ) == 0)) &
1671 DO_LOCALITY(local(kl, kr, klm1, krm1, lastK_left, lastK_right, kt, kb, err)) &
1672 DO_LOCALITY(local(dRho, dRhoTop, dRhoBot, hL, hR, lastP_left, lastP_right, pos)) &
16730 DO_LOCALITY(local(searching_left_column, searching_right_column, reached_bottom, bad))
16740 kl = kl_b(II,JJ) ; kr = kr_b(II,JJ)
16750 lastK_left = lastK_left_b(II,JJ) ; lastK_right = lastK_right_b(II,JJ)
16760 lastP_left = lastP_left_b(II,JJ) ; lastP_right = lastP_right_b(II,JJ)
16770 reached_bottom = reached_bottom_b(II,JJ)
16780 searching_left_column = searching_left_b(II,JJ) ; searching_right_column = searching_right_b(II,JJ)
16790 err = 0
1680
16810 klm1 = max(kl-1, 1)
16820 if (klm1>nk) err = max(err, 1)
16830 krm1 = max(kr-1, 1)
16840 if (krm1>nk) err = max(err, 2)
1685
1686 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr) - rho(kl)
1687 dRho = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(II,JJ,kr) + dRdTl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Tr(II,JJ,kr) - Tl(II,JJ,kl) ) &
16880 + ( dRdSr(II,JJ,kr) + dRdSl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Sr(II,JJ,kr) - Sl(II,JJ,kl) ) )
1689 ! Which column has the lighter surface for the current indexes, kr and kl
16900 if (.not. reached_bottom) then
16910 if (dRho < 0.) then
16920 searching_left_column = .true.
16930 searching_right_column = .false.
16940 elseif (dRho > 0.) then
16950 searching_right_column = .true.
16960 searching_left_column = .false.
1697 else ! dRho == 0.
16980 if (kl + kr == 2) then ! Still at surface
16990 searching_left_column = .true.
17000 searching_right_column = .false.
1701 else ! Not the surface so we simply change direction
17020 searching_left_column = .not. searching_left_column
17030 searching_right_column = .not. searching_right_column
1704 endif
1705 endif
1706 endif
1707
17080 if (err > 0) then
1709 continue ! Stop searching this face; ierr reports why.
17100 elseif (searching_left_column) then
1711 ! Interpolate for the neutral surface position within the left column, layer klm1
1712 ! Potential density difference, rho(kl-1) - rho(kr) (should be negative)
1713 dRhoTop = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTl(II,JJ,klm1) + dRdTr(II,JJ,kr) ) * ( Tl(II,JJ,klm1) - Tr(II,JJ,kr) ) &
17140 + ( dRdSl(II,JJ,klm1) + dRdSr(II,JJ,kr) ) * ( Sl(II,JJ,klm1) - Sr(II,JJ,kr) ) )
1715 ! Potential density difference, rho(kl) - rho(kr) (will be positive)
1716 dRhoBot = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTl(II,JJ,klm1+1) + dRdTr(II,JJ,kr) ) * ( Tl(II,JJ,klm1+1) - Tr(II,JJ,kr) ) &
17170 + ( dRdSl(II,JJ,klm1+1) + dRdSr(II,JJ,kr) ) * ( Sl(II,JJ,klm1+1) - Sr(II,JJ,kr) ) )
1718
1719 ! Because we are looking left, the right surface, kr, is lighter than klm1+1 and should be denser than klm1
1720 ! unless we are still at the top of the left column (kl=1)
17210 if (dRhoTop > 0. .or. kr+kl==2) then
17220 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = 0. ! The right surface is lighter than anything in layer klm1
17230 elseif (dRhoTop >= dRhoBot) then ! Left layer is unstratified
17240 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = 1.
1725 else
1726 ! Linearly interpolate for the position between Pl(kl-1) and Pl(kl) where the density difference
1727 ! between right and left is zero. The Pl here are only used to handle massless layers.
1728 call interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked(dRhoTop, Pl(II,JJ,klm1), dRhoBot, Pl(II,JJ,klm1+1), &
17290 pos, bad)
17300 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = pos
17310 if (bad) err = max(err, 4)
1732 endif
17330 if (PoL(II,JJ,k_surface)>=1. .and. klm1<nk) then ! >= is really ==, when PoL==1 we point to the bottom
17340 klm1 = klm1 + 1
17350 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) - 1.
1736 endif
17370 if (real(klm1-lastK_left)+(PoL(II,JJ,k_surface)-lastP_left)<0.) then
17380 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = lastP_left
17390 klm1 = lastK_left
1740 endif
17410 KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = klm1
17420 if (kr <= nk) then
17430 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = 0.
17440 KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = kr
1745 else
17460 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = 1.
17470 KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = nk
1748 endif
17490 if (kr <= nk) then
17500 kr = kr + 1
1751 else
17520 reached_bottom = .true.
17530 searching_right_column = .true.
17540 searching_left_column = .false.
1755 endif
17560 elseif (searching_right_column) then
1757 ! Interpolate for the neutral surface position within the right column, layer krm1
1758 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr-1) - rho(kl) (should be negative)
1759 dRhoTop = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(II,JJ,krm1) + dRdTl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Tr(II,JJ,krm1) - Tl(II,JJ,kl) ) + &
17600 ( dRdSr(II,JJ,krm1) + dRdSl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Sr(II,JJ,krm1) - Sl(II,JJ,kl) ) )
1761 ! Potential density difference, rho(kr) - rho(kl) (will be positive)
1762 dRhoBot = 0.5 * ( ( dRdTr(II,JJ,krm1+1) + dRdTl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Tr(II,JJ,krm1+1) - Tl(II,JJ,kl) ) + &
17630 ( dRdSr(II,JJ,krm1+1) + dRdSl(II,JJ,kl) ) * ( Sr(II,JJ,krm1+1) - Sl(II,JJ,kl) ) )
1764
1765 ! Because we are looking right, the left surface, kl, is lighter than krm1+1 and should be denser than krm1
1766 ! unless we are still at the top of the right column (kr=1)
17670 if (dRhoTop >= 0. .or. kr+kl==2) then
17680 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = 0. ! The left surface is lighter than anything in layer krm1
17690 elseif (dRhoTop >= dRhoBot) then ! Right layer is unstratified
17700 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = 1.
1771 else
1772 ! Linearly interpolate for the position between Pr(kr-1) and Pr(kr) where the density difference
1773 ! between right and left is zero. The Pr here are only used to handle massless layers.
1774 call interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked(dRhoTop, Pr(II,JJ,krm1), dRhoBot, Pr(II,JJ,krm1+1), &
17750 pos, bad)
17760 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = pos
17770 if (bad) err = max(err, 4)
1778 endif
17790 if (PoR(II,JJ,k_surface)>=1. .and. krm1<nk) then ! >= is really ==, when PoR==1 we point to the bottom
17800 krm1 = krm1 + 1
17810 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) - 1.
1782 endif
17830 if (real(krm1-lastK_right)+(PoR(II,JJ,k_surface)-lastP_right)<0.) then
17840 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = lastP_right
17850 krm1 = lastK_right
1786 endif
17870 KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = krm1
17880 if (kl <= nk) then
17890 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = 0.
17900 KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = kl
1791 else
17920 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = 1.
17930 KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = nk
1794 endif
17950 if (kl <= nk) then
17960 kl = kl + 1
1797 else
17980 reached_bottom = .true.
17990 searching_right_column = .false.
18000 searching_left_column = .true.
1801 endif
1802 else
18030 err = max(err, 3)
1804 endif
18050 if (interior_limit .and. (err == 0)) then
18060 if (KoL(II,JJ,k_surface)<=bl_kl(II,JJ)) then
18070 KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = bl_kl(II,JJ)
18080 if (PoL(II,JJ,k_surface)<bl_zl(II,JJ)) then
18090 PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) = bl_zl(II,JJ)
1810 endif
1811 endif
18120 if (KoR(II,JJ,k_surface)<=bl_kr(II,JJ)) then
18130 KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = bl_kr(II,JJ)
18140 if (PoR(II,JJ,k_surface)<bl_zr(II,JJ)) then
18150 PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) = bl_zr(II,JJ)
1816 endif
1817 endif
1818 endif
1819
18200 if (err == 0) then
18210 lastK_left = KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) ; lastP_left = PoL(II,JJ,k_surface)
18220 lastK_right = KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) ; lastP_right = PoR(II,JJ,k_surface)
1823 ! Effective thickness, from the absolute positions of the neutral surfaces as absolute_position gives them
1824 ! NOTE: This would be better expressed in terms of the layers thicknesses rather
1825 ! than as differences of position - AJA
18260 if (k_surface>1) then
18270 if (max(KoL(II,JJ,k_surface), KoL(II,JJ,k_surface-1), KoR(II,JJ,k_surface), &
1828 KoR(II,JJ,k_surface-1)) > nk) then
18290 err = max(err, 5)
1830 else
18310 kb = KoL(II,JJ,k_surface) ; kt = KoL(II,JJ,k_surface-1)
1832 hL = (Pl(II,JJ,kb) + PoL(II,JJ,k_surface) * ( Pl(II,JJ,kb+1) - Pl(II,JJ,kb) )) - &
18330 (Pl(II,JJ,kt) + PoL(II,JJ,k_surface-1) * ( Pl(II,JJ,kt+1) - Pl(II,JJ,kt) ))
18340 kb = KoR(II,JJ,k_surface) ; kt = KoR(II,JJ,k_surface-1)
1835 hR = (Pr(II,JJ,kb) + PoR(II,JJ,k_surface) * ( Pr(II,JJ,kb+1) - Pr(II,JJ,kb) )) - &
18360 (Pr(II,JJ,kt) + PoR(II,JJ,k_surface-1) * ( Pr(II,JJ,kt+1) - Pr(II,JJ,kt) ))
18370 if ( hL + hR > 0.) then
18380 hEff(II,JJ,k_surface-1) = 2. * hL * hR / ( hL + hR ) ! Harmonic mean of layer thicknesses
1839 else
18400 hEff(II,JJ,k_surface-1) = 0.
1841 endif
1842 endif
1843 endif
1844 endif
1845
18460 kl_b(II,JJ) = kl ; kr_b(II,JJ) = kr
18470 lastK_left_b(II,JJ) = lastK_left ; lastK_right_b(II,JJ) = lastK_right
18480 lastP_left_b(II,JJ) = lastP_left ; lastP_right_b(II,JJ) = lastP_right
18490 reached_bottom_b(II,JJ) = reached_bottom
18500 searching_left_b(II,JJ) = searching_left_column ; searching_right_b(II,JJ) = searching_right_column
18510 err_b(II,JJ) = err
1852 enddo
1853 enddo
1854 enddo
1855
18560 ierr = 0
18570 do concurrent (JJ=1:JJe, II=1:IIe) DO_LOCALITY(reduce(max: ierr))
18580 ierr = max(ierr, err_b(II,JJ))
1859 enddo
1860
1861 !$omp target exit data map(release: kl_b, kr_b, lastK_left_b, lastK_right_b, lastP_left_b, lastP_right_b, &
1862 !$omp reached_bottom_b, searching_left_b, searching_right_b, err_b)
1863
18640end subroutine neutral_positions_block
1865
1866!> Finds the positions of the neutral surfaces between the columns on either side of each zonal face, a
1867!! block of faces at a time in the form of zonal_mass_flux. With continuous reconstructions this is done on
1868!! the device, and with discontinuous reconstructions face by face on the host. The positions are reset
1869!! everywhere first.
18700subroutine zonal_neutral_positions(G, nk, ns, Pint, Tint, Sint, dRdT, dRdS, k_bot, zeta_bot, &
18710 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, nIIB, njj, h, CS)
1872 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
1873 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
1874 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
1875 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Pint !< Interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
1876 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Tint !< Interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
1877 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Sint !< Interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
1878 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: dRdT !< dRho/dT at interfaces, used with
1879 !! continuous reconstructions [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
1880 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: dRdS !< dRho/dS at interfaces, used with
1881 !! continuous reconstructions [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
1882 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(in) :: k_bot !< Index of the last layer within the boundary
1883 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(in) :: zeta_bot !< Fractional position of the bottom of the
1884 !! boundary layer within layer k_bot [nondim]
1885 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: PoL !< Fractional position of neutral surface
1886 !! within layer KoL of the western column [nondim]
1887 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: PoR !< Fractional position of neutral surface
1888 !! within layer KoR of the eastern column [nondim]
1889 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: KoL !< Index of first western interface above
1890 !! neutral surface
1891 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: KoR !< Index of first eastern interface above
1892 !! neutral surface
1893 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns-1), intent(inout) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
1894 !! surfaces [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa], or
1895 !! [H ~> m or kg m-2] with discontinuous reconstructions
1896 integer, intent(in) :: nIIB !< I block size for array calculations
1897 integer, intent(in) :: njj !< j block size for array calculations
1898 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
1899 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
1900
1901 ! Local variables
1902 ! The block-sized work arrays below hold the western (l) and eastern (r) columns of the block's zonal faces.
19030 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,nk+1) :: Pl_b, Pr_b ! Interface pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
19040 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,nk+1) :: Tl_b, Tr_b ! Interface potential temperatures [C ~> degC]
19050 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,nk+1) :: Sl_b, Sr_b ! Interface salinities [S ~> ppt]
19060 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,nk+1) :: dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b ! dRho/dT at interfaces [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
19070 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,nk+1) :: dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b ! dRho/dS at interfaces [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
19080 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,ns) :: PoL_b, PoR_b ! Fractional positions of the neutral surfaces [nondim]
19090 integer, dimension(nIIB,njj,ns) :: KoL_b, KoR_b ! Layer indices of the neutral surfaces
19100 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,ns-1) :: hEff_b ! Effective thicknesses [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
19110 logical, dimension(nIIB,njj) :: do_face ! True for the ocean faces of the block
19120 integer, dimension(nIIB,njj) :: bl_kl, bl_kr ! Layer indices of the boundary layers
19130 real, dimension(nIIB,njj) :: bl_zl, bl_zr ! Fractional positions of the boundary layers [nondim]
1914 integer :: ierr, ierr_max ! Failure codes from neutral_positions_block
1915 integer :: I, j, k, ks, II, jj
1916 integer :: jsb, jeb ! The j-index bounds of the current block.
1917 integer :: IsbB, IebB ! The I-index bounds of the current block.
1918 integer :: jje, IIe ! The block-local j- and I-index end bounds.
1919
19200 if (.not.CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
19210 PoL(:,:,:) = 0. ; PoR(:,:,:) = 0. ; KoL(:,:,:) = 1 ; KoR(:,:,:) = 1
19220 hEff(:,:,:) = 0.
19230 do jsb=G%jsc,G%jec,njj ; do IsbB=G%isc-1,G%iec,nIIB
19240 IebB = min(IsbB + nIIB - 1, G%iec)
19250 jeb = min(jsb + njj - 1, G%jec)
19260 IIe = IebB - IsbB + 1
19270 jje = jeb - jsb + 1
1928
19290 do jj=1,jje ; do II=1,IIe
19300 I = IsbB + II - 1
19310 j = jsb + jj - 1
1932
19330 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0) then
1934 call find_neutral_surface_positions_discontinuous(CS, nk, &
1935 CS%P_i(i,j,:,:), h(i,j,:), CS%T_i(i,j,:,:), CS%S_i(i,j,:,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j,:,:), &
1936 CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j,:,:),CS%stable_cell(i,j,:), &
1937 CS%P_i(i+1,j,:,:), h(i+1,j,:), CS%T_i(i+1,j,:,:), CS%S_i(i+1,j,:,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i+1,j,:,:), &
1938 CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i+1,j,:,:), CS%stable_cell(i+1,j,:), &
1939 PoL(I,j,:), PoR(I,j,:), KoL(I,j,:), KoR(I,j,:), hEff(I,j,:), &
19400 hard_fail_heff = CS%hard_fail_heff)
1941 endif
1942 enddo ; enddo
1943 enddo ; enddo
1944 !$omp target update to(PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff)
19450 return
1946 endif
1947
19480 do concurrent (k=1:ns, j=G%jsd:G%jed, I=G%isd:G%ied)
19490 PoL(I,j,k) = 0. ; PoR(I,j,k) = 0. ; KoL(I,j,k) = 1 ; KoR(I,j,k) = 1
1950 enddo
19510 do concurrent (k=1:ns-1, j=G%jsd:G%jed, I=G%isd:G%ied)
19520 hEff(I,j,k) = 0.
1953 enddo
1954
1955 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Pl_b, Pr_b, Tl_b, Tr_b, Sl_b, Sr_b, dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b, &
1956 !$omp PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, do_face, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
1957
19580 ierr_max = 0
19590 do jsb=G%jsc,G%jec,njj ; do IsbB=G%isc-1,G%iec,nIIB
19600 IebB = min(IsbB + nIIB - 1, G%iec)
19610 jeb = min(jsb + njj - 1, G%jec)
19620 IIe = IebB - IsbB + 1
19630 jje = jeb - jsb + 1
1964
19650 do concurrent (k=1:nk+1, jj=1:jje, II=1:IIe) DO_LOCALITY(local(I, j))
19660 I = IsbB + II - 1
19670 j = jsb + jj - 1
1968
19690 Pl_b(II,jj,k) = Pint(i,j,k) ; Pr_b(II,jj,k) = Pint(i+1,j,k)
19700 Tl_b(II,jj,k) = Tint(i,j,k) ; Tr_b(II,jj,k) = Tint(i+1,j,k)
19710 Sl_b(II,jj,k) = Sint(i,j,k) ; Sr_b(II,jj,k) = Sint(i+1,j,k)
19720 dRdTl_b(II,jj,k) = dRdT(i,j,k) ; dRdTr_b(II,jj,k) = dRdT(i+1,j,k)
19730 dRdSl_b(II,jj,k) = dRdS(i,j,k) ; dRdSr_b(II,jj,k) = dRdS(i+1,j,k)
1974 enddo
19750 do concurrent (jj=1:jje, II=1:IIe) DO_LOCALITY(local(I, j))
19760 I = IsbB + II - 1
19770 j = jsb + jj - 1
1978
19790 do_face(II,jj) = (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0)
19800 bl_kl(II,jj) = k_bot(I,j) ; bl_kr(II,jj) = k_bot(I+1,j)
19810 bl_zl(II,jj) = zeta_bot(I,j) ; bl_zr(II,jj) = zeta_bot(I+1,j)
1982 enddo
1983
1984 call neutral_positions_block(nIIB, njj, IIe, jje, nk, ns, do_face, Pl_b, Tl_b, Sl_b, dRdTl_b, dRdSl_b, &
1985 Pr_b, Tr_b, Sr_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSr_b, .true., bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr, &
19860 PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, ierr)
19870 ierr_max = max(ierr_max, ierr)
1988
19890 do concurrent (ks=1:ns, jj=1:jje, II=1:IIe, do_face(II,jj)) DO_LOCALITY(local(I, j))
19900 I = IsbB + II - 1
19910 j = jsb + jj - 1
1992
19930 PoL(I,j,ks) = PoL_b(II,jj,ks) ; PoR(I,j,ks) = PoR_b(II,jj,ks)
19940 KoL(I,j,ks) = KoL_b(II,jj,ks) ; KoR(I,j,ks) = KoR_b(II,jj,ks)
1995 enddo
19960 do concurrent (ks=1:ns-1, jj=1:jje, II=1:IIe, do_face(II,jj)) DO_LOCALITY(local(I, j))
19970 I = IsbB + II - 1
19980 j = jsb + jj - 1
1999
20000 hEff(I,j,ks) = hEff_b(II,jj,ks)
2001 enddo
2002 enddo ; enddo
2003
2004 !$omp target exit data map(release: Pl_b, Pr_b, Tl_b, Tr_b, Sl_b, Sr_b, dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b, &
2005 !$omp PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, do_face, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
2006
20070 if (ierr_max > 0) call neutral_positions_error(ierr_max, "zonal_neutral_positions")
2008
20090end subroutine zonal_neutral_positions
