2011!> Finds the positions of the neutral surfaces between the columns on either side of each meridional face,
2012!! a block of faces at a time in the form of meridional_mass_flux. With continuous reconstructions this is
2013!! done on the device, and with discontinuous reconstructions face by face on the host. The positions are
2014!! reset everywhere first.
20150subroutine meridional_neutral_positions(G, nk, ns, Pint, Tint, Sint, dRdT, dRdS, k_bot, zeta_bot, &
20160 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, nii, nJJB, h, CS)
2017 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
2018 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
2019 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
2020 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Pint !< Interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2021 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Tint !< Interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
2022 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), intent(in) :: Sint !< Interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
2023 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: dRdT !< dRho/dT at interfaces, used with
2024 !! continuous reconstructions [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2025 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: dRdS !< dRho/dS at interfaces, used with
2026 !! continuous reconstructions [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2027 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(in) :: k_bot !< Index of the last layer within the boundary
2028 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(in) :: zeta_bot !< Fractional position of the bottom of the
2029 !! boundary layer within layer k_bot [nondim]
2030 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: PoL !< Fractional position of neutral surface
2031 !! within layer KoL of the southern column [nondim]
2032 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: PoR !< Fractional position of neutral surface
2033 !! within layer KoR of the northern column [nondim]
2034 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: KoL !< Index of first southern interface above
2035 !! neutral surface
2036 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns), intent(inout) :: KoR !< Index of first northern interface above
2037 !! neutral surface
2038 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),ns-1), intent(inout) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
2039 !! surfaces [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa], or
2040 !! [H ~> m or kg m-2] with discontinuous reconstructions
2041 integer, intent(in) :: nii !< i block size for array calculations
2042 integer, intent(in) :: nJJB !< J block size for array calculations
2043 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2044 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
2045
2046 ! Local variables
2047 ! The block-sized work arrays below hold the southern (l) and northern (r) columns of the block's meridional
2048 ! faces.
20490 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,nk+1) :: Pl_b, Pr_b ! Interface pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
20500 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,nk+1) :: Tl_b, Tr_b ! Interface potential temperatures [C ~> degC]
20510 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,nk+1) :: Sl_b, Sr_b ! Interface salinities [S ~> ppt]
20520 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,nk+1) :: dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b ! dRho/dT at interfaces [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
20530 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,nk+1) :: dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b ! dRho/dS at interfaces [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
20540 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,ns) :: PoL_b, PoR_b ! Fractional positions of the neutral surfaces [nondim]
20550 integer, dimension(nii,nJJB,ns) :: KoL_b, KoR_b ! Layer indices of the neutral surfaces
20560 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,ns-1) :: hEff_b ! Effective thicknesses [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
20570 logical, dimension(nii,nJJB) :: do_face ! True for the ocean faces of the block
20580 integer, dimension(nii,nJJB) :: bl_kl, bl_kr ! Layer indices of the boundary layers
20590 real, dimension(nii,nJJB) :: bl_zl, bl_zr ! Fractional positions of the boundary layers [nondim]
2060 integer :: ierr, ierr_max ! Failure codes from neutral_positions_block
2061 integer :: i, J, k, ks, ii, JJ
2062 integer :: isb, ieb ! The i-index bounds of the current block.
2063 integer :: JsbB, JebB ! The J-index bounds of the current block.
2064 integer :: iie, JJe ! The block-local i- and J-index end bounds.
2065
20660 if (.not.CS%continuous_reconstruction) then
20670 PoL(:,:,:) = 0. ; PoR(:,:,:) = 0. ; KoL(:,:,:) = 1 ; KoR(:,:,:) = 1
20680 hEff(:,:,:) = 0.
20690 do JsbB=G%jsc-1,G%jec,nJJB ; do isb=G%isc,G%iec,nii
20700 ieb = min(isb + nii - 1, G%iec)
20710 JebB = min(JsbB + nJJB - 1, G%jec)
20720 iie = ieb - isb + 1
20730 JJe = JebB - JsbB + 1
2074
20750 do JJ=1,JJe ; do ii=1,iie
20760 i = isb + ii - 1
20770 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
2078
20790 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0) then
2080 call find_neutral_surface_positions_discontinuous(CS, nk, &
2081 CS%P_i(i,j,:,:), h(i,j,:), CS%T_i(i,j,:,:), CS%S_i(i,j,:,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j,:,:), &
2082 CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j,:,:),CS%stable_cell(i,j,:), &
2083 CS%P_i(i,j+1,:,:), h(i,j+1,:), CS%T_i(i,j+1,:,:), CS%S_i(i,j+1,:,:), CS%ppoly_coeffs_T(i,j+1,:,:), &
2084 CS%ppoly_coeffs_S(i,j+1,:,:), CS%stable_cell(i,j+1,:), &
2085 PoL(I,j,:), PoR(I,j,:), KoL(I,j,:), KoR(I,j,:), hEff(I,j,:), &
20860 hard_fail_heff = CS%hard_fail_heff)
2087 endif
2088 enddo ; enddo
2089 enddo ; enddo
2090 !$omp target update to(PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff)
20910 return
2092 endif
2093
20940 do concurrent (k=1:ns, J=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
20950 PoL(i,J,k) = 0. ; PoR(i,J,k) = 0. ; KoL(i,J,k) = 1 ; KoR(i,J,k) = 1
2096 enddo
20970 do concurrent (k=1:ns-1, J=G%jsd:G%jed, i=G%isd:G%ied)
20980 hEff(i,J,k) = 0.
2099 enddo
2100
2101 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Pl_b, Pr_b, Tl_b, Tr_b, Sl_b, Sr_b, dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b, &
2102 !$omp PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, do_face, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
2103
21040 ierr_max = 0
21050 do JsbB=G%jsc-1,G%jec,nJJB ; do isb=G%isc,G%iec,nii
21060 ieb = min(isb + nii - 1, G%iec)
21070 JebB = min(JsbB + nJJB - 1, G%jec)
21080 iie = ieb - isb + 1
21090 JJe = JebB - JsbB + 1
2110
21110 do concurrent (k=1:nk+1, JJ=1:JJe, ii=1:iie) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, J))
21120 i = isb + ii - 1
21130 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
2114
21150 Pl_b(ii,JJ,k) = Pint(i,j,k) ; Pr_b(ii,JJ,k) = Pint(i,j+1,k)
21160 Tl_b(ii,JJ,k) = Tint(i,j,k) ; Tr_b(ii,JJ,k) = Tint(i,j+1,k)
21170 Sl_b(ii,JJ,k) = Sint(i,j,k) ; Sr_b(ii,JJ,k) = Sint(i,j+1,k)
21180 dRdTl_b(ii,JJ,k) = dRdT(i,j,k) ; dRdTr_b(ii,JJ,k) = dRdT(i,j+1,k)
21190 dRdSl_b(ii,JJ,k) = dRdS(i,j,k) ; dRdSr_b(ii,JJ,k) = dRdS(i,j+1,k)
2120 enddo
21210 do concurrent (JJ=1:JJe, ii=1:iie) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, J))
21220 i = isb + ii - 1
21230 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
2124
21250 do_face(ii,JJ) = (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0)
21260 bl_kl(ii,JJ) = k_bot(i,J) ; bl_kr(ii,JJ) = k_bot(i,J+1)
21270 bl_zl(ii,JJ) = zeta_bot(i,J) ; bl_zr(ii,JJ) = zeta_bot(i,J+1)
2128 enddo
2129
2130 call neutral_positions_block(nii, nJJB, iie, JJe, nk, ns, do_face, Pl_b, Tl_b, Sl_b, dRdTl_b, dRdSl_b, &
2131 Pr_b, Tr_b, Sr_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSr_b, .true., bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr, &
21320 PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, ierr)
21330 ierr_max = max(ierr_max, ierr)
2134
21350 do concurrent (ks=1:ns, JJ=1:JJe, ii=1:iie, do_face(ii,JJ)) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, J))
21360 i = isb + ii - 1
21370 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
2138
21390 PoL(i,J,ks) = PoL_b(ii,JJ,ks) ; PoR(i,J,ks) = PoR_b(ii,JJ,ks)
21400 KoL(i,J,ks) = KoL_b(ii,JJ,ks) ; KoR(i,J,ks) = KoR_b(ii,JJ,ks)
2141 enddo
21420 do concurrent (ks=1:ns-1, JJ=1:JJe, ii=1:iie, do_face(ii,JJ)) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, J))
21430 i = isb + ii - 1
21440 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
2145
21460 hEff(i,J,ks) = hEff_b(ii,JJ,ks)
2147 enddo
2148 enddo ; enddo
2149
2150 !$omp target exit data map(release: Pl_b, Pr_b, Tl_b, Tr_b, Sl_b, Sr_b, dRdTl_b, dRdTr_b, dRdSl_b, dRdSr_b, &
2151 !$omp PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, do_face, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
2152
21530 if (ierr_max > 0) call neutral_positions_error(ierr_max, "meridional_neutral_positions")
2154
21550end subroutine meridional_neutral_positions

2157!> Brings the model down with the message that find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous and the functions it
2158!! calls stop with, for a failure code returned by neutral_positions_block.
21590subroutine neutral_positions_error(ierr, caller)
2160 integer, intent(in) :: ierr !< The failure code from neutral_positions_block
2161 character(len=*), intent(in) :: caller !< The name of the calling routine
2162
2163 character(len=120) :: mesg
2164
21650 select case (ierr)
21660 case (1) ; mesg = 'find_neutral_surface_positions(): klm1 went out of bounds!'
21670 case (2) ; mesg = 'find_neutral_surface_positions(): krm1 went out of bounds!'
21680 case (3) ; mesg = 'find_neutral_surface_positions(): Else what?'
21690 case (4) ; mesg = 'interpolate_for_nondim_position: (Ppos < Pneg) or (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos)'
21700 case (5) ; mesg = 'absolute_position: k>nk is out of bounds!'
21710 case default ; mesg = 'Unexpected failure.'
2172 end select
21730 call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM_neutral_diffusion, "//trim(caller)//": "//trim(mesg))
2174
21750end subroutine neutral_positions_error
2176
2177!> Returns the non-dimensional position between Pneg and Ppos where the
2178!! interpolated density difference equals zero [nondim].
2179!! The result is always bounded to be between 0 and 1.
21800real function interpolate_for_nondim_position(dRhoNeg, Pneg, dRhoPos, Ppos)
2181 real, intent(in) :: dRhoNeg !< Negative density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2182 real, intent(in) :: Pneg !< Position of negative density difference [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or [nondim]
2183 real, intent(in) :: dRhoPos !< Positive density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2184 real, intent(in) :: Ppos !< Position of positive density difference [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or [nondim]
2185
2186 character(len=120) :: mesg
2187 logical :: bad ! True if the positions or density differences are out of order
2188
2189 call interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked(dRhoNeg, Pneg, dRhoPos, Ppos, &
21900 interpolate_for_nondim_position, bad)
21910 if (bad) then ! ((Ppos < Pneg) .or. (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos) )
2192 ! Error handling for problematic cases. It is expected that this should never occur.
21930 write(mesg,*) 'dRhoNeg, Pneg, dRhoPos, Ppos', dRhoNeg, Pneg, dRhoPos, Ppos
21940 call MOM_error(WARNING, 'interpolate_for_nondim_position: '//trim(mesg))
2195 ! write(stderr,*) trim(mesg)
21960 if ((Ppos < Pneg) .and. (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos)) then
21970 mesg = '(Ppos < Pneg) and (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos)'
21980 elseif (Ppos < Pneg) then
21990 mesg = 'Ppos < Pneg'
22000 elseif (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos) then
22010 mesg = trim(mesg)//'; dRhoNeg > dRhoPos'
2202 else ! This should never happen.
22030 mesg = 'Unexpected failure.'
2204 endif
22050 call MOM_error(FATAL, 'interpolate_for_nondim_position: '//trim(mesg))
2206 endif
2207
22080end function interpolate_for_nondim_position
2209
2210!> Calculates the non-dimensional position between Pneg and Ppos where the interpolated density difference
2211!! equals zero, as interpolate_for_nondim_position does, but returns a flag instead of stopping on the
2212!! problematic cases.
22130pure subroutine interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked(dRhoNeg, Pneg, dRhoPos, Ppos, pos, bad)
2214 real, intent(in) :: dRhoNeg !< Negative density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2215 real, intent(in) :: Pneg !< Position of negative density difference [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or [nondim]
2216 real, intent(in) :: dRhoPos !< Positive density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2217 real, intent(in) :: Ppos !< Position of positive density difference [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or [nondim]
2218 real, intent(out) :: pos !< The position between Pneg and Ppos where the interpolated density
2219 !! difference is zero, bounded between 0 and 1 [nondim]
2220 logical, intent(out) :: bad !< True if Ppos < Pneg or dRhoNeg > dRhoPos, which is not expected
2221
22220 bad = .false.
22230 if ((Ppos > Pneg) .and. (dRhoPos - dRhoNeg >= 0. )) then
22240 if ( dRhoPos - dRhoNeg > 0. ) then
22250 pos = min( 1., max( 0., -dRhoNeg / ( dRhoPos - dRhoNeg ) ) )
22260 elseif (dRhoPos - dRhoNeg == 0) then
22270 if (dRhoNeg > 0.) then
22280 pos = 0.
22290 elseif (dRhoNeg < 0.) then
22300 pos = 1.
2231 else ! dRhoPos = dRhoNeg = 0
22320 pos = 0.5
2233 endif
2234 else ! dRhoPos - dRhoNeg < 0
22350 pos = 0.5
2236 endif
22370 elseif (Ppos == Pneg) then ! Handle vanished or inverted layers
22380 pos = 0.5
2239 else ! ((Ppos < Pneg) .or. (dRhoNeg > dRhoPos) )
22400 pos = 0.5
22410 bad = .true.
2242 endif
2243
22440end subroutine interpolate_for_nondim_position_unchecked
2245
2246!> Higher order version of find_neutral_surface_positions. Returns positions within left/right columns
2247!! of combined interfaces using intracell reconstructions of T/S. Note that the polynomial reconstructions
2248!! of T and S are optional to aid with unit testing, but will always be passed otherwise
22490subroutine find_neutral_surface_positions_discontinuous(CS, nk, &
22500 Pres_l, hcol_l, Tl, Sl, ppoly_T_l, ppoly_S_l, stable_l, &
22510 Pres_r, hcol_r, Tr, Sr, ppoly_T_r, ppoly_S_r, stable_r, &
22520 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, zeta_bot_L, zeta_bot_R, k_bot_L, k_bot_R, hard_fail_heff)
2253
2254 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
2255 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
2256 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Pres_l !< Left-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2257 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hcol_l !< Left-column layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2258 !! or other units
2259 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column top interface potential
2260 !! temperature [C ~> degC]
2261 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Sl !< Left-column top interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
2262 real, dimension(:,:), intent(in) :: ppoly_T_l !< Left-column coefficients of T reconstruction [C ~> degC]
2263 real, dimension(:,:), intent(in) :: ppoly_S_l !< Left-column coefficients of S reconstruction [S ~> ppt]
2264 logical, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: stable_l !< True where the left-column is stable
2265 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Pres_r !< Right-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2266 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hcol_r !< Left-column layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2267 !! or other units
2268 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column top interface potential
2269 !! temperature [C ~> degC]
2270 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: Sr !< Right-column top interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
2271 real, dimension(:,:), intent(in) :: ppoly_T_r !< Right-column coefficients of T
2272 !! reconstruction [C ~> degC]
2273 real, dimension(:,:), intent(in) :: ppoly_S_r !< Right-column coefficients of S reconstruction [S ~> ppt]
2274 logical, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: stable_r !< True where the right-column is stable
2275 real, dimension(4*nk), intent(inout) :: PoL !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
2276 !! layer KoL of left column [nondim]
2277 real, dimension(4*nk), intent(inout) :: PoR !< Fractional position of neutral surface within
2278 !! layer KoR of right column [nondim]
2279 integer, dimension(4*nk), intent(inout) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
2280 integer, dimension(4*nk), intent(inout) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
2281 real, dimension(4*nk-1), intent(inout) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral surfaces
2282 !! [H ~> m or kg m-2] or other units taken from hcol_l
2283 real, optional, intent(in) :: zeta_bot_L!< Non-dimensional distance to where the boundary layer
2284 !! intersects the cell (left) [nondim]
2285 real, optional, intent(in) :: zeta_bot_R!< Non-dimensional distance to where the boundary layer
2286 !! intersects the cell (right) [nondim]
2287
2288 integer, optional, intent(in) :: k_bot_L !< k-index for the boundary layer (left) [nondim]
2289 integer, optional, intent(in) :: k_bot_R !< k-index for the boundary layer (right) [nondim]
2290 logical, optional, intent(in) :: hard_fail_heff !< If true (default) bring down the model if the
2291 !! neutral surfaces ever cross
2292 ! Local variables
2293 integer :: ns ! Number of neutral surfaces
2294 integer :: k_surface ! Index of neutral surface
2295 integer :: kl_left, kl_right ! Index of layers on the left/right
2296 integer :: ki_left, ki_right ! Index of interfaces on the left/right
2297 logical :: searching_left_column ! True if searching for the position of a right interface in the left column
2298 logical :: searching_right_column ! True if searching for the position of a left interface in the right column
2299 logical :: reached_bottom ! True if one of the bottom-most interfaces has been used as the target
2300 logical :: fail_heff ! Fail if negative thickness are encountered. By default this
2301 ! is true, but it can take its value from hard_fail_heff.
2302 real :: dRho ! A density difference between columns [R ~> kg m-3]
2303 real :: hL, hR ! Left and right layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2] or units from hcol_l
2304 real :: lastP_left, lastP_right ! Previous positions for left and right [nondim]
2305 integer :: k_init_L, k_init_R ! Starting indices layers for left and right
2306 real :: p_init_L, p_init_R ! Starting positions for left and right [nondim]
2307 ! Initialize variables for the search
23080 ns = 4*nk
23090 ki_right = 1
23100 ki_left = 1
23110 kl_left = 1
23120 kl_right = 1
23130 lastP_left = 0.
23140 lastP_right = 0.
23150 reached_bottom = .false.
23160 searching_left_column = .false.
23170 searching_right_column = .false.
2318
23190 fail_heff = .true.
23200 if (PRESENT(hard_fail_heff)) fail_heff = hard_fail_heff
2321
23220 if (PRESENT(k_bot_L) .and. PRESENT(k_bot_R) .and. PRESENT(zeta_bot_L) .and. PRESENT(zeta_bot_R)) then
23230 k_init_L = k_bot_L ; k_init_R = k_bot_R
23240 p_init_L = zeta_bot_L ; p_init_R = zeta_bot_R
23250 lastP_left = zeta_bot_L ; lastP_right = zeta_bot_R
23260 kl_left = k_bot_L ; kl_right = k_bot_R
2327 else
23280 k_init_L = 1 ; k_init_R = 1
23290 p_init_L = 0. ; p_init_R = 0.
2330 endif
2331 ! Loop over each neutral surface, working from top to bottom
23320 neutral_surfaces: do k_surface = 1, ns
2333
23340 if (k_surface == ns) then
23350 PoL(k_surface) = 1.
23360 PoR(k_surface) = 1.
23370 KoL(k_surface) = nk
23380 KoR(k_surface) = nk
2339 ! If the layers are unstable, then simply point the surface to the previous location
23400 elseif (.not. stable_l(kl_left)) then
23410 if (k_surface > 1) then
23420 PoL(k_surface) = ki_left - 1 ! Top interface is at position = 0., Bottom is at position = 1
23430 KoL(k_surface) = kl_left
23440 PoR(k_surface) = PoR(k_surface-1)
23450 KoR(k_surface) = KoR(k_surface-1)
2346 else
23470 PoR(k_surface) = p_init_R
23480 KoR(k_surface) = k_init_R
23490 PoL(k_surface) = p_init_L
23500 KoL(k_Surface) = k_init_L
2351 endif
23520 call increment_interface(nk, kl_left, ki_left, reached_bottom, searching_left_column, searching_right_column)
23530 searching_left_column = .true.
23540 searching_right_column = .false.
23550 elseif (.not. stable_r(kl_right)) then ! Check the right layer for stability
23560 if (k_surface > 1) then
23570 PoR(k_surface) = ki_right - 1 ! Top interface is at position = 0., Bottom is at position = 1
23580 KoR(k_surface) = kl_right
23590 PoL(k_surface) = PoL(k_surface-1)
23600 KoL(k_surface) = KoL(k_surface-1)
2361 else
23620 PoR(k_surface) = 0.
23630 KoR(k_surface) = 1
23640 PoL(k_surface) = 0.
23650 KoL(k_surface) = 1
2366 endif
23670 call increment_interface(nk, kl_right, ki_right, reached_bottom, searching_right_column, searching_left_column)
23680 searching_left_column = .false.
23690 searching_right_column = .true.
2370 else ! Layers are stable so need to figure out whether we need to search right or left
2371 ! For convenience, the left column uses the searched "from" interface variables, and the right column
2372 ! uses the searched 'to'. These will get reset in subsequent calc_delta_rho calls
2373
2374 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, &
2375 Tr(kl_right, ki_right), Sr(kl_right, ki_right), Pres_r(kl_right,ki_right), &
2376 Tl(kl_left, ki_left), Sl(kl_left, ki_left) , Pres_l(kl_left,ki_left), &
23770 dRho)
23780 if (CS%debug) write(stdout,'(A,I0,A,E12.4,A,I0,A,I0,A,I0,A,I0)') &
23790 "k_surface=",k_surface, " dRho=",CS%R_to_kg_m3*dRho, &
23800 "kl_left=",kl_left, " ki_left=",ki_left, " kl_right=",kl_right, " ki_right=",ki_right
2381 ! Which column has the lighter surface for the current indexes, kr and kl
23820 if (.not. reached_bottom) then
23830 if (dRho < 0.) then
23840 searching_left_column = .true.
23850 searching_right_column = .false.
23860 elseif (dRho > 0.) then
23870 searching_left_column = .false.
23880 searching_right_column = .true.
2389 else ! dRho == 0.
23900 if ( ( kl_left + kl_right == 2 ) .and. (ki_left + ki_right == 2) ) then ! Still at surface
23910 searching_left_column = .true.
23920 searching_right_column = .false.
2393 else ! Not the surface so we simply change direction
23940 searching_left_column = .not. searching_left_column
23950 searching_right_column = .not. searching_right_column
2396 endif
2397 endif
2398 endif
23990 if (searching_left_column) then
2400 ! Position of the right interface is known and all quantities are fixed
24010 PoR(k_surface) = ki_right - 1.
24020 KoR(k_surface) = kl_right
2403 PoL(k_surface) = search_other_column(CS, k_surface, lastP_left, &
2404 Tr(kl_right, ki_right), Sr(kl_right, ki_right), Pres_r(kl_right, ki_right), &
2405 Tl(kl_left,1), Sl(kl_left,1), Pres_l(kl_left,1), &
2406 Tl(kl_left,2), Sl(kl_left,2), Pres_l(kl_left,2), &
24070 ppoly_T_l(kl_left,:), ppoly_S_l(kl_left,:))
24080 KoL(k_surface) = kl_left
2409
24100 if (CS%debug) then
24110 write(stdout,'(A,I0)') "Searching left layer ", kl_left
24120 write(stdout,'(A,I0,1X,I0)') "Searching from right: ", kl_right, ki_right
24130 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Reference: ", Tr(kl_right,ki_right), Sr(kl_right,ki_right)
24140 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Top L: ", Tl(kl_left,1), Sl(kl_left,1)
24150 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Bot L: ", Tl(kl_left,2), Sl(kl_left,2)
2416 endif
24170 call increment_interface(nk, kl_right, ki_right, reached_bottom, searching_right_column, searching_left_column)
24180 lastP_left = PoL(k_surface)
2419 ! If the right layer increments, then we need to reset the last position on the right
24200 if ( kl_right == (KoR(k_surface) + 1) ) lastP_right = 0.
2421
24220 elseif (searching_right_column) then
2423 ! Position of the right interface is known and all quantities are fixed
24240 PoL(k_surface) = ki_left - 1.
24250 KoL(k_surface) = kl_left
2426 PoR(k_surface) = search_other_column(CS, k_surface, lastP_right, &
2427 Tl(kl_left, ki_left), Sl(kl_left, ki_left), Pres_l(kl_left, ki_left), &
2428 Tr(kl_right,1), Sr(kl_right,1), Pres_r(kl_right,1), &
2429 Tr(kl_right,2), Sr(kl_right,2), Pres_r(kl_right,2), &
24300 ppoly_T_r(kl_right,:), ppoly_S_r(kl_right,:))
24310 KoR(k_surface) = kl_right
2432
24330 if (CS%debug) then
24340 write(stdout,'(A,I0)') "Searching right layer ", kl_right
24350 write(stdout,'(A,I0,1X,I0)') "Searching from left: ", kl_left, ki_left
24360 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Reference: ", Tl(kl_left,ki_left), Sl(kl_left,ki_left)
24370 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Top L: ", Tr(kl_right,1), Sr(kl_right,1)
24380 write(stdout,*) "Temp/Salt Bot L: ", Tr(kl_right,2), Sr(kl_right,2)
2439 endif
24400 call increment_interface(nk, kl_left, ki_left, reached_bottom, searching_left_column, searching_right_column)
24410 lastP_right = PoR(k_surface)
2442 ! If the right layer increments, then we need to reset the last position on the right
24430 if ( kl_left == (KoL(k_surface) + 1) ) lastP_left = 0.
2444 else
24450 stop 'Else what?'
2446 endif
24470 if (CS%debug) write(stdout,'(A,I3,A,ES16.6,A,I0,A,ES16.6)') "KoL:", KoL(k_surface), " PoL:", PoL(k_surface), &
24480 " KoR:", KoR(k_surface), " PoR:", PoR(k_surface)
2449 endif
2450 ! Effective thickness
24510 if (k_surface>1) then
24520 if ( KoL(k_surface) == KoL(k_surface-1) .and. KoR(k_surface) == KoR(k_surface-1) ) then
24530 hL = (PoL(k_surface) - PoL(k_surface-1))*hcol_l(KoL(k_surface))
24540 hR = (PoR(k_surface) - PoR(k_surface-1))*hcol_r(KoR(k_surface))
24550 if (hL < 0. .or. hR < 0.) then
24560 if (fail_heff) then
24570 call MOM_error(FATAL,"Negative thicknesses in neutral diffusion")
2458 else
24590 if (searching_left_column) then
24600 PoL(k_surface) = PoL(k_surface-1)
24610 KoL(k_surface) = KoL(k_surface-1)
24620 elseif (searching_right_column) then
24630 PoR(k_surface) = PoR(k_surface-1)
24640 KoR(k_surface) = KoR(k_surface-1)
2465 endif
2466 endif
24670 elseif ( hL + hR == 0. ) then
24680 hEff(k_surface-1) = 0.
2469 else
24700 hEff(k_surface-1) = 2. * ( (hL * hR) / ( hL + hR ) )! Harmonic mean
24710 if ( KoL(k_surface) /= KoL(k_surface-1) ) then
24720 call MOM_error(FATAL,"Neutral sublayer spans multiple layers")
2473 endif
24740 if ( KoR(k_surface) /= KoR(k_surface-1) ) then
24750 call MOM_error(FATAL,"Neutral sublayer spans multiple layers")
2476 endif
2477 endif
2478 else
24790 hEff(k_surface-1) = 0.
2480 endif
2481 endif
2482 enddo neutral_surfaces
24830end subroutine find_neutral_surface_positions_discontinuous
2484
2485!> Sweep down through the column and mark as stable if the bottom interface of a cell is denser than the top
24860subroutine mark_unstable_cells(CS, nk, T, S, P, stable_cell)
2487 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
2488 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels in a column
2489 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: T !< Temperature at interfaces [C ~> degC]
2490 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: S !< Salinity at interfaces [S ~> ppt]
2491 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in) :: P !< Pressure at interfaces [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2492 logical, dimension(nk), intent( out) :: stable_cell !< True if this cell is unstably stratified
2493
2494 integer :: k
2495 real :: delta_rho ! A density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2496
24970 do k = 1,nk
2498 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs( CS, T(k,2), S(k,2), max(P(k,2), CS%ref_pres), &
24990 T(k,1), S(k,1), max(P(k,1), CS%ref_pres), delta_rho )
25000 stable_cell(k) = (delta_rho > 0.)
2501 enddo
25020end subroutine mark_unstable_cells
2503
2504!> Searches the "other" (searched) column for the position of the neutral surface, returning
2505!! the fractional postion within the layer [nondim]
25060real function search_other_column(CS, ksurf, pos_last, T_from, S_from, P_from, T_top, S_top, P_top, &
25070 T_bot, S_bot, P_bot, T_poly, S_poly ) result(pos)
2508 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), intent(in ) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
2509 integer, intent(in ) :: ksurf !< Current index of neutral surface
2510 real, intent(in ) :: pos_last !< Last position within the current layer, used as the lower
2511 !! bound in the root finding algorithm [nondim]
2512 real, intent(in ) :: T_from !< Temperature at the searched from interface [C ~> degC]
2513 real, intent(in ) :: S_from !< Salinity at the searched from interface [S ~> ppt]
2514 real, intent(in ) :: P_from !< Pressure at the searched from interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2515 real, intent(in ) :: T_top !< Temperature at the searched to top interface [C ~> degC]
2516 real, intent(in ) :: S_top !< Salinity at the searched to top interface [S ~> ppt]
2517 real, intent(in ) :: P_top !< Pressure at the searched to top interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2518 !! interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2519 real, intent(in ) :: T_bot !< Temperature at the searched to bottom interface [C ~> degC]
2520 real, intent(in ) :: S_bot !< Salinity at the searched to bottom interface [S ~> ppt]
2521 real, intent(in ) :: P_bot !< Pressure at the searched to bottom
2522 !! interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2523 real, dimension(:), intent(in ) :: T_poly !< Temperature polynomial reconstruction
2524 !! coefficients [C ~> degC]
2525 real, dimension(:), intent(in ) :: S_poly !< Salinity polynomial reconstruction
2526 !! coefficients [S ~> ppt]
2527 ! Local variables
2528 real :: dRhotop, dRhobot ! Density differences [R ~> kg m-3]
2529 real :: dRdT_top, dRdT_bot, dRdT_from ! Partial derivatives of density with temperature [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2530 real :: dRdS_top, dRdS_bot, dRdS_from ! Partial derivatives of density with salinity [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2531
2532 ! Calculate the difference in density at the tops or the bottom
25330 if (CS%neutral_pos_method == 1 .or. CS%neutral_pos_method == 3) then
25340 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, T_top, S_top, P_top, T_from, S_from, P_from, dRhoTop)
25350 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, T_bot, S_bot, P_bot, T_from, S_from, P_from, dRhoBot)
25360 elseif (CS%neutral_pos_method == 2) then
2537 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, T_top, S_top, P_top, T_from, S_from, P_from, dRhoTop, &
25380 dRdT_top, dRdS_top, dRdT_from, dRdS_from)
2539 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, T_bot, S_bot, P_bot, T_from, S_from, P_from, dRhoBot, &
25400 dRdT_bot, dRdS_bot, dRdT_from, dRdS_from)
2541 endif
2542
2543 ! Handle all the special cases EXCEPT if it connects within the layer
25440 if ( (dRhoTop > 0.) .or. (ksurf == 1) ) then ! First interface or lighter than anything in layer
25450 pos = pos_last
25460 elseif ( dRhoTop > dRhoBot ) then ! Unstably stratified
25470 pos = 1.
25480 elseif ( dRhoTop < 0. .and. dRhoBot < 0.) then ! Denser than anything in layer
25490 pos = 1.
25500 elseif ( dRhoTop == 0. .and. dRhoBot == 0. ) then ! Perfectly unstratified
25510 pos = 1.
25520 elseif ( dRhoBot == 0. ) then ! Matches perfectly at the Top
25530 pos = 1.
25540 elseif ( dRhoTop == 0. ) then ! Matches perfectly at the Bottom
25550 pos = pos_last
2556 else ! Neutral surface within layer
25570 pos = -1
2558 endif
2559
2560 ! Can safely return if position is >= 0 otherwise will need to find the position within the layer
25610 if (pos>=0) return
2562
25630 if (CS%neutral_pos_method==1) then
25640 pos = interpolate_for_nondim_position( dRhoTop, P_top, dRhoBot, P_bot )
2565 ! For the 'Linear' case of finding the neutral position, the reference pressure to use is the average
2566 ! of the midpoint of the layer being searched and the interface being searched from
25670 elseif (CS%neutral_pos_method == 2) then
2568 pos = find_neutral_pos_linear( CS, pos_last, T_from, S_from, dRdT_from, dRdS_from, &
25690 dRdT_top, dRdS_top, dRdT_bot, dRdS_bot, T_poly, S_poly )
25700 elseif (CS%neutral_pos_method == 3) then
25710 pos = find_neutral_pos_full( CS, pos_last, T_from, S_from, P_from, P_top, P_bot, T_poly, S_poly)
2572 endif
2573
25740end function search_other_column
2575
2576!> Increments the interface which was just connected and also set flags if the bottom is reached
25770subroutine increment_interface(nk, kl, ki, reached_bottom, searching_this_column, searching_other_column)
2578 integer, intent(in ) :: nk !< Number of vertical levels
2579 integer, intent(inout) :: kl !< Current layer (potentially updated)
2580 integer, intent(inout) :: ki !< Current interface
2581 logical, intent(inout) :: reached_bottom !< Updated when kl == nk and ki == 2
2582 logical, intent(inout) :: searching_this_column !< Updated when kl == nk and ki == 2
2583 logical, intent(inout) :: searching_other_column !< Updated when kl == nk and ki == 2
2584
25850 reached_bottom = .false.
25860 if (ki == 2) then ! At the bottom interface
25870 if ((ki == 2) .and. (kl < nk) ) then ! Not at the bottom so just go to the next layer
25880 kl = kl+1
25890 ki = 1
25900 elseif ((kl == nk) .and. (ki==2)) then
25910 reached_bottom = .true.
25920 searching_this_column = .false.
25930 searching_other_column = .true.
2594 endif
25950 elseif (ki==1) then ! At the top interface
25960 ki = 2 ! Next interface is same layer, but bottom interface
2597 else
25980 call MOM_error(FATAL,"Unanticipated eventuality in increment_interface")
2599 endif
26000end subroutine increment_interface
2601
2602!> Search a layer to find where delta_rho = 0 based on a linear interpolation of alpha and beta of the top and bottom
2603!! being searched and polynomial reconstructions of T and S. Compressibility is not needed because either, we are
2604!! assuming incompressibility in the equation of state for this module or alpha and beta are calculated having been
2605!! displaced to the average pressures of the two pressures We need Newton's method because the T and S reconstructions
2606!! make delta_rho a polynomial function of z if using PPM or higher. If Newton's method would search fall out of the
2607!! interval [0,1], a bisection step would be taken instead. Also this linearization of alpha, beta means that second
2608!! derivatives of the EOS are not needed. Note that delta in variable names below refers to horizontal differences and
2609!! 'd' refers to vertical differences
26100function find_neutral_pos_linear( CS, z0, T_ref, S_ref, dRdT_ref, dRdS_ref, &
26110 dRdT_top, dRdS_top, dRdT_bot, dRdS_bot, ppoly_T, ppoly_S ) result( z )
2612 type(neutral_diffusion_CS),intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure with parameters for this module
2613 real, intent(in) :: z0 !< Lower bound of position, also serves as the
2614 !! initial guess [nondim]
2615 real, intent(in) :: T_ref !< Temperature at the searched from interface [C ~> degC]
2616 real, intent(in) :: S_ref !< Salinity at the searched from interface [S ~> ppt]
2617 real, intent(in) :: dRdT_ref !< dRho/dT at the searched from interface
2618 !! [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2619 real, intent(in) :: dRdS_ref !< dRho/dS at the searched from interface
2620 !! [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2621 real, intent(in) :: dRdT_top !< dRho/dT at top of layer being searched
2622 !! [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2623 real, intent(in) :: dRdS_top !< dRho/dS at top of layer being searched
2624 !! [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2625 real, intent(in) :: dRdT_bot !< dRho/dT at bottom of layer being searched
2626 !! [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2627 real, intent(in) :: dRdS_bot !< dRho/dS at bottom of layer being searched
2628 !! [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2629 real, dimension(:), intent(in) :: ppoly_T !< Coefficients of the polynomial reconstruction of T within
2630 !! the layer to be searched [C ~> degC].
2631 real, dimension(:), intent(in) :: ppoly_S !< Coefficients of the polynomial reconstruction of S within
2632 !! the layer to be searched [S ~> ppt].
2633 real :: z !< Position where drho = 0 [nondim]
2634 ! Local variables
2635 real :: dRdT_diff ! Difference in the partial derivative of density with temperature across the
2636 ! layer [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2637 real :: dRdS_diff ! Difference in the partial derivative of density with salinity across the
2638 ! layer [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2639 real :: drho, drho_dz ! Density anomaly and its derivative with fractional position [R ~> kg m-3]
2640 real :: dRdT_z ! Partial derivative of density with temperature at a point [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2641 real :: dRdS_z ! Partial derivative of density with salinity at a point [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2642 real :: T_z, dT_dz ! Temperature at a point and its derivative with fractional position [C ~> degC]
2643 real :: S_z, dS_dz ! Salinity at a point and its derivative with fractional position [S ~> ppt]
2644 real :: drho_min, drho_max ! Bounds on density differences [R ~> kg m-3]
2645 real :: ztest, zmin, zmax ! Fractional positions in the cell [nondim]
2646 real :: a1, a2 ! Fractional weights of the top and bottom values [nondim]
2647 integer :: iter
2648 integer :: nterm
2649
26500 nterm = SIZE(ppoly_T)
2651
2652 ! Position independent quantities
26530 dRdT_diff = dRdT_bot - dRdT_top
26540 dRdS_diff = dRdS_bot - dRdS_top
2655 ! Initial starting drho (used for bisection)
26560 zmin = z0 ! Lower bounding interval
26570 zmax = 1. ! Maximum bounding interval (bottom of layer)
26580 a1 = 1. - zmin
26590 a2 = zmin
26600 T_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, zmin )
26610 S_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, zmin )
26620 dRdT_z = a1*dRdT_top + a2*dRdT_bot
26630 dRdS_z = a1*dRdS_top + a2*dRdS_bot
26640 drho_min = 0.5*((dRdT_z+dRdT_ref)*(T_z-T_ref) + (dRdS_z+dRdS_ref)*(S_z-S_ref))
2665
26660 T_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, 1. )
26670 S_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, 1. )
26680 drho_max = 0.5*((dRdT_bot+dRdT_ref)*(T_z-T_ref) + (dRdS_bot+dRdS_ref)*(S_z-S_ref))
2669
26700 if (drho_min >= 0.) then
26710 z = z0
26720 return
26730 elseif (drho_max == 0.) then
26740 z = 1.
26750 return
2676 endif
26770 if ( SIGN(1.,drho_min) == SIGN(1.,drho_max) ) then
26780 call MOM_error(FATAL, "drho_min is the same sign as dhro_max")
2679 endif
2680
26810 z = z0
26820 ztest = z0
26830 do iter = 1, CS%max_iter
2684 ! Calculate quantities at the current nondimensional position
26850 a1 = 1.-z
26860 a2 = z
26870 dRdT_z = a1*dRdT_top + a2*dRdT_bot
26880 dRdS_z = a1*dRdS_top + a2*dRdS_bot
26890 T_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, z )
26900 S_z = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, z )
26910 drho = 0.5*((dRdT_z+dRdT_ref)*(T_z-T_ref) + (dRdS_z+dRdS_ref)*(S_z-S_ref))
2692
2693 ! Check for convergence
26940 if (ABS(drho) <= CS%drho_tol) exit
2695 ! Update bisection bracketing intervals
26960 if (drho < 0. .and. drho > drho_min) then
26970 drho_min = drho
26980 zmin = z
26990 elseif (drho > 0. .and. drho < drho_max) then
27000 drho_max = drho
27010 zmax = z
2702 endif
2703
2704 ! Calculate a Newton step
27050 dT_dz = first_derivative_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, z )
27060 dS_dz = first_derivative_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, z )
2707 drho_dz = 0.5*( (dRdT_diff*(T_z - T_ref) + (dRdT_ref+dRdT_z)*dT_dz) + &
27080 (dRdS_diff*(S_z - S_ref) + (dRdS_ref+dRdS_z)*dS_dz) )
2709
27100 ztest = z - drho/drho_dz
2711 ! Take a bisection if z falls out of [zmin,zmax]
27120 if (ztest < zmin .or. ztest > zmax) then
27130 if ( drho < 0. ) then
27140 ztest = 0.5*(z + zmax)
2715 else
27160 ztest = 0.5*(zmin + z)
2717 endif
2718 endif
2719
2720 ! Test to ensure we haven't stalled out
27210 if ( abs(z-ztest) <= CS%x_tol ) exit
2722 ! Reset for next iteration
27230 z = ztest
2724 enddo
2725
27260end function find_neutral_pos_linear
2727
2728!> Use the full equation of state to calculate the difference in locally referenced potential density. The derivatives
2729!! in this case are not trivial to calculate, so instead we use a regula falsi method
27300function find_neutral_pos_full( CS, z0, T_ref, S_ref, P_ref, P_top, P_bot, ppoly_T, ppoly_S ) result( z )
2731 type(neutral_diffusion_CS),intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure with parameters for this module
2732 real, intent(in) :: z0 !< Lower bound of position, also serves as the
2733 !! initial guess [nondim]
2734 real, intent(in) :: T_ref !< Temperature at the searched from interface [C ~> degC]
2735 real, intent(in) :: S_ref !< Salinity at the searched from interface [S ~> ppt]
2736 real, intent(in) :: P_ref !< Pressure at the searched from interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2737 real, intent(in) :: P_top !< Pressure at top of layer being searched [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2738 real, intent(in) :: P_bot !< Pressure at bottom of layer being searched [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2739 real, dimension(:), intent(in) :: ppoly_T !< Coefficients of the polynomial reconstruction of T within
2740 !! the layer to be searched [C ~> degC]
2741 real, dimension(:), intent(in) :: ppoly_S !< Coefficients of the polynomial reconstruction of T within
2742 !! the layer to be searched [S ~> ppt]
2743 real :: z !< Position where drho = 0 [nondim]
2744 ! Local variables
2745 integer :: iter
2746 integer :: nterm
2747
2748 real :: drho_a, drho_b, drho_c ! Density differences [R ~> kg m-3]
2749 real :: a, b, c ! Fractional positions [nondim]
2750 real :: Ta, Tb, Tc ! Temperatures [C ~> degC]
2751 real :: Sa, Sb, Sc ! Salinities [S ~> ppt]
2752 real :: Pa, Pb, Pc ! Pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2753 integer :: side
2754
27550 side = 0
2756 ! Set the first two evaluation to the endpoints of the interval
27570 b = z0 ; c = 1
27580 nterm = SIZE(ppoly_T)
2759
2760 ! Calculate drho at the minimum bound
27610 Tb = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, b )
27620 Sb = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, b )
27630 Pb = P_top*(1.-b) + P_bot*b
27640 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, Tb, Sb, Pb, T_ref, S_ref, P_ref, drho_b)
2765
2766 ! Calculate drho at the maximum bound
27670 Tc = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, 1. )
27680 Sc = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, 1. )
27690 Pc = P_Bot
27700 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, Tc, Sc, Pc, T_ref, S_ref, P_ref, drho_c)
2771
27720 if (drho_b >= 0.) then
27730 z = z0
27740 return
27750 elseif (drho_c == 0.) then
27760 z = 1.
27770 return
2778 endif
27790 if ( SIGN(1.,drho_b) == SIGN(1.,drho_c) ) then
27800 z = z0
27810 return
2782 endif
2783
27840 do iter = 1, CS%max_iter
2785 ! Calculate new position and evaluate if we have converged
27860 a = (drho_b*c - drho_c*b)/(drho_b-drho_c)
27870 Ta = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_T, nterm, a )
27880 Sa = evaluation_polynomial( ppoly_S, nterm, a )
27890 Pa = P_top*(1.-a) + P_bot*a
27900 call calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, Ta, Sa, Pa, T_ref, S_ref, P_ref, drho_a)
27910 if (ABS(drho_a) < CS%drho_tol) then
27920 z = a
27930 return
2794 endif
2795
27960 if (drho_a*drho_c > 0.) then
27970 if ( ABS(a-c)<CS%x_tol) then
27980 z = a
27990 return
2800 endif
28010 c = a ; drho_c = drho_a
28020 if (side == -1) drho_b = 0.5*drho_b
28030 side = -1
28040 elseif ( drho_b*drho_a > 0 ) then
28050 if ( ABS(a-b)<CS%x_tol) then
28060 z = a
28070 return
2808 endif
28090 b = a ; drho_b = drho_a
28100 if (side == 1) drho_c = 0.5*drho_c
28110 side = 1
2812 else
28130 z = a
28140 return
2815 endif
2816 enddo
2817
28180 z = a
2819
28200end function find_neutral_pos_full
2821
2822!> Calculate the difference in density between two points in a variety of ways
28230subroutine calc_delta_rho_and_derivs(CS, T1, S1, p1_in, T2, S2, p2_in, drho, &
2824 drdt1_out, drds1_out, drdt2_out, drds2_out )
2825 type(neutral_diffusion_CS) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
2826 real, intent(in ) :: T1 !< Temperature at point 1 [C ~> degC]
2827 real, intent(in ) :: S1 !< Salinity at point 1 [S ~> ppt]
2828 real, intent(in ) :: p1_in !< Pressure at point 1 [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2829 real, intent(in ) :: T2 !< Temperature at point 2 [C ~> degC]
2830 real, intent(in ) :: S2 !< Salinity at point 2 [S ~> ppt]
2831 real, intent(in ) :: p2_in !< Pressure at point 2 [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2832 real, intent( out) :: drho !< Difference in density between the two points [R ~> kg m-3]
2833 real, optional, intent( out) :: dRdT1_out !< drho_dt at point 1 [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2834 real, optional, intent( out) :: dRdS1_out !< drho_ds at point 1 [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2835 real, optional, intent( out) :: dRdT2_out !< drho_dt at point 2 [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2836 real, optional, intent( out) :: dRdS2_out !< drho_ds at point 2 [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2837 ! Local variables
2838 real :: rho1, rho2 ! Densities [R ~> kg m-3]
2839 real :: p1, p2, pmid ! Pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2840 real :: drdt1, drdt2 ! Partial derivatives of density with temperature [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2841 real :: drds1, drds2 ! Partial derivatives of density with salinity [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2842
2843 ! Use the same reference pressure or the in-situ pressure
28440 if (CS%ref_pres > 0.) then
28450 p1 = CS%ref_pres
28460 p2 = CS%ref_pres
2847 else
28480 p1 = p1_in
28490 p2 = p2_in
2850 endif
2851
2852 ! Use the full linear equation of state to calculate the difference in density (expensive!)
28530 if (TRIM(CS%delta_rho_form) == 'full') then
28540 pmid = 0.5 * (p1 + p2)
28550 call calculate_density(T1, S1, pmid, rho1, CS%EOS)
28560 call calculate_density(T2, S2, pmid, rho2, CS%EOS)
28570 drho = rho1 - rho2
2858 ! Use the density derivatives at the average of pressures and the differences in temperature
28590 elseif (TRIM(CS%delta_rho_form) == 'mid_pressure') then
28600 pmid = 0.5 * (p1 + p2)
28610 if (CS%ref_pres>=0) pmid = CS%ref_pres
28620 call calculate_density_derivs(T1, S1, pmid, drdt1, drds1, CS%EOS)
28630 call calculate_density_derivs(T2, S2, pmid, drdt2, drds2, CS%EOS)
28640 drho = delta_rho_from_derivs( T1, S1, p1, drdt1, drds1, T2, S2, p2, drdt2, drds2)
28650 elseif (TRIM(CS%delta_rho_form) == 'local_pressure') then
28660 call calculate_density_derivs(T1, S1, p1, drdt1, drds1, CS%EOS)
28670 call calculate_density_derivs(T2, S2, p2, drdt2, drds2, CS%EOS)
28680 drho = delta_rho_from_derivs( T1, S1, p1, drdt1, drds1, T2, S2, p2, drdt2, drds2)
2869 else
28700 call MOM_error(FATAL, "delta_rho_form is not recognized")
2871 endif
2872
28730 if (PRESENT(drdt1_out)) drdt1_out = drdt1
28740 if (PRESENT(drds1_out)) drds1_out = drds1
28750 if (PRESENT(drdt2_out)) drdt2_out = drdt2
28760 if (PRESENT(drds2_out)) drds2_out = drds2
2877
28780end subroutine calc_delta_rho_and_derivs
2879
2880!> Calculate delta rho from derivatives and gradients of properties
2881!! \f$ \Delta \rho = \frac{1}{2}\left[ (\alpha_1 + \alpha_2)*(T_1-T_2) +
2882!! (\beta_1 + \beta_2)*(S_1-S_2) +
2883!! (\gamma^{-1}_1 + \gamma^{-1}_2)*(P_1-P_2) \right] \f$
28840function delta_rho_from_derivs( T1, S1, P1, dRdT1, dRdS1, &
2885 T2, S2, P2, dRdT2, dRdS2 ) result (drho)
2886 real :: T1 !< Temperature at point 1 [C ~> degC]
2887 real :: S1 !< Salinity at point 1 [S ~> ppt]
2888 real :: P1 !< Pressure at point 1 [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2889 real :: dRdT1 !< The partial derivative of density with temperature at point 1 [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2890 real :: dRdS1 !< The partial derivative of density with salinity at point 1 [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2891 real :: T2 !< Temperature at point 2 [C ~> degC]
2892 real :: S2 !< Salinity at point 2 [S ~> ppt]
2893 real :: P2 !< Pressure at point 2 [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2894 real :: dRdT2 !< The partial derivative of density with temperature at point 2 [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
2895 real :: dRdS2 !< The partial derivative of density with salinity at point 2 [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
2896 ! Local variables
2897 real :: drho ! The density difference [R ~> kg m-3]
2898
28990 drho = 0.5 * ( (dRdT1+dRdT2)*(T1-T2) + (dRdS1+dRdS2)*(S1-S2))
2900
29010end function delta_rho_from_derivs
2902
2903!> Converts non-dimensional position within a layer to absolute position (for debugging)
29040function absolute_position(n,ns,Pint,Karr,NParr,k_surface)
2905 integer, intent(in) :: n !< Number of levels
2906 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
2907 real, intent(in) :: Pint(n+1) !< Position of interfaces [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
2908 integer, intent(in) :: Karr(ns) !< Index of interface above position
2909 real, intent(in) :: NParr(ns) !< Non-dimensional position within layer Karr(:) [nondim]
2910 integer, intent(in) :: k_surface !< k-interface to query
2911 real :: absolute_position !< The absolute position of a location [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2912 !! or other units following Pint
2913 ! Local variables
2914 integer :: k
2915
29160 k = Karr(k_surface)
29170 if (k>n) stop 'absolute_position: k>nk is out of bounds!'
29180 absolute_position = Pint(k) + NParr(k_surface) * ( Pint(k+1) - Pint(k) )
2919
29200end function absolute_position
2921
2922!> Converts non-dimensional positions within layers to absolute positions (for debugging)
29230function absolute_positions(n,ns,Pint,Karr,NParr)
2924 integer, intent(in) :: n !< Number of levels
2925 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
2926 real, intent(in) :: Pint(n+1) !< Position of interface [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
2927 integer, intent(in) :: Karr(ns) !< Indexes of interfaces about positions
2928 real, intent(in) :: NParr(ns) !< Non-dimensional positions within layers Karr(:) [nondim]
2929
2930 real, dimension(ns) :: absolute_positions !< Absolute positions [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
2931 !! or other units following Pint
2932
2933 ! Local variables
2934 integer :: k_surface
2935
29360 do k_surface = 1, ns
29370 absolute_positions(k_surface) = absolute_position(n,ns,Pint,Karr,NParr,k_surface)
2938 enddo
2939
2940end function absolute_positions
2941
2942!> Returns a single column of neutral diffusion fluxes of a tracer.
29430subroutine neutral_surface_flux(nk, nsurf, deg, hl, hr, Tl, Tr, PiL, PiR, KoL, KoR, &
29440 hEff, Flx, continuous, h_neglect, remap_CS, h_neglect_edge, &
29450 coeff_l, coeff_r)
2946 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
2947 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
2948 integer, intent(in) :: deg !< Degree of polynomial reconstructions
2949 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hl !< Left-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2950 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hr !< Right-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2951 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column layer tracer in arbitrary concentration
2952 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
2953 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column layer tracer in arbitrary concentration
2954 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
2955 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiL !< Fractional position of neutral surface
2956 !! within layer KoL of left column [nondim]
2957 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiR !< Fractional position of neutral surface
2958 !! within layer KoR of right column [nondim]
2959 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
2960 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
2961 real, dimension(nsurf-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
2962 !! surfaces [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2963 real, dimension(nsurf-1), intent(inout) :: Flx !< Flux of tracer between pairs of neutral layers
2964 !! in units (conc H or conc H L2) that depend on
2965 !! the presence and units of coeff_l and coeff_r.
2966 !! If the tracer is temperature, this could have
2967 !! units of [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] or
2968 !! [C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg] if coeff_l has
2969 !! units of [L2 ~> m2]
2970 logical, intent(in) :: continuous !< True if using continuous reconstruction
2971 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small width for the purpose
2972 !! of cell reconstructions [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2973 type(remapping_CS), optional, intent(in) :: remap_CS !< Remapping control structure used
2974 !! to create sublayers
2975 real, optional, intent(in) :: h_neglect_edge !< A negligibly small width used for edge value
2976 !! calculations if continuous is false [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2977 real, dimension(nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: coeff_l !< Left-column diffusivity [L2 ~> m2] or [nondim]
2978 real, dimension(nk+1), optional, intent(in) :: coeff_r !< Right-column diffusivity [L2 ~> m2] or [nondim]
2979
2980 ! Local variables
2981 integer :: k_sublayer, klb, klt, krb, krt
2982 real :: T_right_sub, T_left_sub ! Tracer concentrations averaged over sub-intervals in the right and left
2983 ! columns in arbitrary concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2984 real :: T_right_layer, T_left_layer ! Tracer concentrations averaged over layers in the right and left
2985 ! columns in arbitrary concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2986 real :: T_right_top, T_right_bottom, T_right_top_int, T_right_bot_int ! Tracer concentrations
2987 ! at various positions in the right column in arbitrary
2988 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2989 real :: T_left_top, T_left_bottom, T_left_top_int, T_left_bot_int ! Tracer concentrations
2990 ! at various positions in the left column in arbitrary
2991 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2992 real :: dT_layer, dT_ave, dT_sublayer ! Differences in vertically averaged tracer concentrations
2993 ! over various portions of the right and left columns in arbitrary
2994 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2995 real :: dT_top, dT_bottom, dT_top_int, dT_bot_int ! Differences in tracer concentrations
2996 ! at various positions between the right and left columns in arbitrary
2997 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature).
2998 real :: khtr_ave ! An averaged diffusivity in normalized units [nondim] if coeff_l and coeff_r are
2999 ! absent or in units copied from coeff_l and coeff_r [L2 ~> m2] or [nondim]
30000 real, dimension(nk+1) :: Til !< Left-column interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3001 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30020 real, dimension(nk+1) :: Tir !< Right-column interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3003 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30040 real, dimension(nk) :: aL_l !< Left-column left edge value of tracer in arbitrary concentration
3005 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30060 real, dimension(nk) :: aR_l !< Left-column right edge value of tracer in arbitrary concentration
3007 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30080 real, dimension(nk) :: aL_r !< Right-column left edge value of tracer in arbitrary concentration
3009 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30100 real, dimension(nk) :: aR_r !< Right-column right edge value of tracer in arbitrary concentration
3011 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3012 ! Discontinuous reconstruction
3013 integer :: iMethod
30140 real, dimension(nk,2) :: Tid_l !< Left-column interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3015 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30160 real, dimension(nk,2) :: Tid_r !< Right-column interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3017 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30180 real, dimension(nk,deg+1) :: ppoly_r_coeffs_l ! Coefficients of the polynomial descriptions of
3019 ! sub-gridscale tracer concentrations in the left column, in arbitrary
3020 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30210 real, dimension(nk,deg+1) :: ppoly_r_coeffs_r ! Coefficients of the polynomial descriptions of
3022 ! sub-gridscale tracer concentrations in the right column, in arbitrary
3023 ! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
30240 real, dimension(nk,deg+1) :: ppoly_r_S_l ! Reconstruction slopes that are unused here, in units of a vertical
3025 ! gradient, which for temperature would be [C H-1 ~> degC m-1 or degC m2 kg-1].
30260 real, dimension(nk,deg+1) :: ppoly_r_S_r ! Reconstruction slopes that are unused here, in units of a vertical
3027 ! gradient, which for temperature would be [C H-1 ~> degC m-1 or degC m2 kg-1].
3028 logical :: down_flux, tapering
3029
30300 tapering = .false.
30310 if (present(coeff_l) .and. present(coeff_r)) tapering = .true.
30320 khtr_ave = 1.0
3033
3034 ! Setup reconstruction edge values
30350 if (continuous) then
30360 call interface_scalar(nk, hl, Tl, Til, 2, h_neglect)
30370 call interface_scalar(nk, hr, Tr, Tir, 2, h_neglect)
30380 call ppm_left_right_edge_values(nk, Tl, Til, aL_l, aR_l)
30390 call ppm_left_right_edge_values(nk, Tr, Tir, aL_r, aR_r)
3040 else
30410 ppoly_r_coeffs_l(:,:) = 0.
30420 ppoly_r_coeffs_r(:,:) = 0.
30430 Tid_l(:,:) = 0.
30440 Tid_r(:,:) = 0.
3045
3046 call build_reconstructions_1d( remap_CS, nk, hl, Tl, ppoly_r_coeffs_l, Tid_l, &
30470 ppoly_r_S_l, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
3048 call build_reconstructions_1d( remap_CS, nk, hr, Tr, ppoly_r_coeffs_r, Tid_r, &
30490 ppoly_r_S_r, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
3050 endif
3051
30520 do k_sublayer = 1, nsurf-1
30530 if (hEff(k_sublayer) == 0.) then
30540 Flx(k_sublayer) = 0.
3055 else
30560 if (tapering) then
30570 klb = KoL(k_sublayer+1)
30580 klt = KoL(k_sublayer)
30590 krb = KoR(k_sublayer+1)
30600 krt = KoR(k_sublayer)
3061 ! these are added in this order to preserve vertically-uniform diffusivity answers
30620 khtr_ave = 0.25 * ((coeff_l(klb) + coeff_l(klt)) + (coeff_r(krb) + coeff_r(krt)))
3063 endif
30640 if (continuous) then
30650 klb = KoL(k_sublayer+1)
30660 T_left_bottom = ( 1. - PiL(k_sublayer+1) ) * Til(klb) + PiL(k_sublayer+1) * Til(klb+1)
30670 klt = KoL(k_sublayer)
30680 T_left_top = ( 1. - PiL(k_sublayer) ) * Til(klt) + PiL(k_sublayer) * Til(klt+1)
3069 T_left_layer = ppm_ave(PiL(k_sublayer), PiL(k_sublayer+1) + real(klb-klt), &
30700 aL_l(klt), aR_l(klt), Tl(klt))
3071
30720 krb = KoR(k_sublayer+1)
30730 T_right_bottom = ( 1. - PiR(k_sublayer+1) ) * Tir(krb) + PiR(k_sublayer+1) * Tir(krb+1)
30740 krt = KoR(k_sublayer)
30750 T_right_top = ( 1. - PiR(k_sublayer) ) * Tir(krt) + PiR(k_sublayer) * Tir(krt+1)
3076 T_right_layer = ppm_ave(PiR(k_sublayer), PiR(k_sublayer+1) + real(krb-krt), &
30770 aL_r(krt), aR_r(krt), Tr(krt))
30780 dT_top = T_right_top - T_left_top
30790 dT_bottom = T_right_bottom - T_left_bottom
30800 dT_ave = 0.5 * ( dT_top + dT_bottom )
30810 dT_layer = T_right_layer - T_left_layer
30820 if (signum(1.,dT_top) * signum(1.,dT_bottom) <= 0. .or. signum(1.,dT_ave) * signum(1.,dT_layer) <= 0.) then
30830 dT_ave = 0.
3084 else
30850 dT_ave = dT_layer
3086 endif
30870 Flx(k_sublayer) = dT_ave * hEff(k_sublayer) * khtr_ave
3088 else ! Discontinuous reconstruction
3089 ! Calculate tracer values on left and right side of the neutral surface
3090 call neutral_surface_T_eval(nk, nsurf, k_sublayer, KoL, PiL, Tl, Tid_l, deg, iMethod, &
3091 ppoly_r_coeffs_l, T_left_top, T_left_bottom, T_left_sub, &
30920 T_left_top_int, T_left_bot_int, T_left_layer)
3093 call neutral_surface_T_eval(nk, nsurf, k_sublayer, KoR, PiR, Tr, Tid_r, deg, iMethod, &
3094 ppoly_r_coeffs_r, T_right_top, T_right_bottom, T_right_sub, &
30950 T_right_top_int, T_right_bot_int, T_right_layer)
3096
30970 dT_top = T_right_top - T_left_top
30980 dT_bottom = T_right_bottom - T_left_bottom
30990 dT_sublayer = T_right_sub - T_left_sub
31000 dT_top_int = T_right_top_int - T_left_top_int
31010 dT_bot_int = T_right_bot_int - T_left_bot_int
3102 ! Enforcing the below criterion incorrectly zero out fluxes
3103 !dT_layer = T_right_layer - T_left_layer
3104
3105 down_flux = dT_top <= 0. .and. dT_bottom <= 0. .and. &
3106 dT_sublayer <= 0. .and. dT_top_int <= 0. .and. &
31070 dT_bot_int <= 0.
3108 down_flux = down_flux .or. &
3109 (dT_top >= 0. .and. dT_bottom >= 0. .and. &
3110 dT_sublayer >= 0. .and. dT_top_int >= 0. .and. &
31110 dT_bot_int >= 0.)
31120 if (down_flux) then
31130 Flx(k_sublayer) = dT_sublayer * hEff(k_sublayer) * khtr_ave
3114 else
31150 Flx(k_sublayer) = 0.
3116 endif
3117 endif
3118 endif
3119 enddo
3120
31210end subroutine neutral_surface_flux
3122
3123!> Returns one column's tracer values at the top and bottom of a neutral layer and the average of its
3124!! continuous PPM reconstruction over the part of a model layer that the neutral layer spans.
31250elemental subroutine neutral_column_values(Po_t, Po_b, dk, Ti_t, Ti_tp1, Ti_b, Ti_bp1, aL, aR, T, &
3126 T_top, T_bot, T_layer, bad)
3127 real, intent(in) :: Po_t !< Fractional position of the top neutral surface within its layer [nondim]
3128 real, intent(in) :: Po_b !< Fractional position of the bottom neutral surface within its layer [nondim]
3129 integer, intent(in) :: dk !< The bottom surface's layer index minus the top surface's layer index
3130 real, intent(in) :: Ti_t !< Tracer at the interface above the top neutral surface in arbitrary
3131 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3132 real, intent(in) :: Ti_tp1 !< Tracer at the interface below the top neutral surface [A ~> a]
3133 real, intent(in) :: Ti_b !< Tracer at the interface above the bottom neutral surface [A ~> a]
3134 real, intent(in) :: Ti_bp1 !< Tracer at the interface below the bottom neutral surface [A ~> a]
3135 real, intent(in) :: aL !< Upper PPM edge value of the top neutral surface's layer [A ~> a]
3136 real, intent(in) :: aR !< Lower PPM edge value of the top neutral surface's layer [A ~> a]
3137 real, intent(in) :: T !< Layer-averaged tracer of the top neutral surface's layer [A ~> a]
3138 real, intent(out) :: T_top !< Tracer at the top neutral surface [A ~> a]
3139 real, intent(out) :: T_bot !< Tracer at the bottom neutral surface [A ~> a]
3140 real, intent(out) :: T_layer !< Average of the reconstruction between the two neutral surfaces [A ~> a]
3141 logical, intent(out) :: bad !< True if the bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked are out of order
3142 !! or more than one layer apart, which ppm_ave would stop on
3143 !DIR$ ATTRIBUTES FORCEINLINE :: neutral_column_values
3144 ! Local variables
3145 real :: xR ! The position of the bottom neutral surface in the top surface's layer [nondim]
3146
31470 T_bot = ( 1. - Po_b ) * Ti_b + Po_b * Ti_bp1
31480 T_top = ( 1. - Po_t ) * Ti_t + Po_t * Ti_tp1
31490 xR = Po_b + real(dk)
31500 T_layer = ppm_ave_unchecked(Po_t, xR, aL, aR, T)
31510 bad = ((xR - Po_t) < 0.) .or. ((xR - Po_t) > 1.)
3152
31530end subroutine neutral_column_values
3154
3155!> Returns the limited neutral diffusive flux of a tracer through one neutral layer, from the tracer
3156!! values in the columns on either side of a face.
31570elemental real function neutral_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, hEff, khtr)
3158 real, intent(in) :: TL_top !< Left-column tracer at the top neutral surface in arbitrary
3159 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3160 real, intent(in) :: TL_bot !< Left-column tracer at the bottom neutral surface [A ~> a]
3161 real, intent(in) :: TL_layer !< Left-column tracer averaged over the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3162 real, intent(in) :: TR_top !< Right-column tracer at the top neutral surface [A ~> a]
3163 real, intent(in) :: TR_bot !< Right-column tracer at the bottom neutral surface [A ~> a]
3164 real, intent(in) :: TR_layer !< Right-column tracer averaged over the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3165 real, intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness of the neutral layer [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3166 real, intent(in) :: khtr !< An averaged diffusivity, 1 [nondim] without tapering or vertical structure
3167 !DIR$ ATTRIBUTES FORCEINLINE :: neutral_flux_value
3168 ! Local variables
3169 real :: dT_top, dT_bottom ! Differences in tracer concentrations at the top and bottom of the neutral layer
3170 ! between the right and left columns [A ~> a]
3171 real :: dT_ave, dT_layer ! Differences in averaged tracer concentrations between the columns [A ~> a]
3172
31730 dT_top = TR_top - TL_top
31740 dT_bottom = TR_bot - TL_bot
31750 dT_ave = 0.5 * ( dT_top + dT_bottom )
31760 dT_layer = TR_layer - TL_layer
31770 if (signum(1.,dT_top) * signum(1.,dT_bottom) <= 0. .or. signum(1.,dT_ave) * signum(1.,dT_layer) <= 0.) then
31780 dT_ave = 0.
3179 else
31800 dT_ave = dT_layer
3181 endif
31820 neutral_flux_value = dT_ave * hEff * khtr
3183
31840end function neutral_flux_value
3185
3186!> Returns one column's tracer values at the top and bottom of a neutral layer, averaged over it and from the
3187!! polynomial at its top and bottom, from the discontinuous reconstruction of the model layer kl that the
3188!! neutral layer lies in. The expressions are those of neutral_surface_T_eval, with the reconstruction of
3189!! layer kl passed as one-layer arrays.
31900subroutine neutral_disc_column_values(nk, kl, kl_bot, Po_t, Po_b, T_mean, T_int, T_int_above, T_int_below, &
31910 deg, iMethod, T_poly, T_top, T_bot, T_sub, T_top_int, T_bot_int)
3192 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of layers in the column
3193 integer, intent(in) :: kl !< The layer of the top neutral surface
3194 integer, intent(in) :: kl_bot !< The layer of the bottom neutral surface
3195 real, intent(in) :: Po_t !< Fractional position of the top neutral surface [nondim]
3196 real, intent(in) :: Po_b !< Fractional position of the bottom neutral surface [nondim]
3197 real, dimension(1), intent(in) :: T_mean !< Layer average of tracer in layer kl in arbitrary
3198 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3199 real, dimension(1,2), intent(in) :: T_int !< Edge values of the reconstruction in layer kl [A ~> a]
3200 real, intent(in) :: T_int_above !< Lower edge value of the reconstruction in layer kl-1,
3201 !! used if kl > 1 [A ~> a]
3202 real, intent(in) :: T_int_below !< Upper edge value of the reconstruction in layer kl+1,
3203 !! used if kl < nk [A ~> a]
3204 integer, intent(in) :: deg !< Degree of reconstruction polynomial (e.g. 1 is linear)
3205 integer, intent(in) :: iMethod !< Method of integration to use
3206 real, dimension(1,deg+1), intent(in) :: T_poly !< Coefficients of the polynomial reconstruction in
3207 !! layer kl [A ~> a]
3208 real, intent(out) :: T_top !< Tracer value at top (across discontinuity if
3209 !! necessary) [A ~> a]
3210 real, intent(out) :: T_bot !< Tracer value at bottom (across discontinuity if
3211 !! necessary) [A ~> a]
3212 real, intent(out) :: T_sub !< Average of the tracer value over the sublayer [A ~> a]
3213 real, intent(out) :: T_top_int !< Tracer value at the top interface of a neutral layer [A ~> a]
3214 real, intent(out) :: T_bot_int !< Tracer value at the bottom interface of a neutral
3215 !! layer [A ~> a]
3216
32170 if ( kl /= kl_bot ) then
32180 call MOM_error(FATAL, "Neutral surfaces span more than one layer")
3219 endif
3220 ! First if the neutral surfaces spans the entirety of a cell, then do not search across the discontinuity
32210 if ( (Po_t == 0.) .and. (Po_b == 1.)) then
32220 T_top = T_int(1,1)
32230 T_bot = T_int(1,2)
3224 else
3225 ! Search across potential discontinuity at top
32260 if ( (kl > 1) .and. (Po_t == 0.) ) then
32270 T_top = T_int_above
3228 else
32290 T_top = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(1,:), deg+1, Po_t )
3230 endif
3231 ! Search across potential discontinuity at bottom
32320 if ( (kl < nk) .and. (Po_b == 1.) ) then
32330 T_bot = T_int_below
3234 else
32350 T_bot = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(1,:), deg+1, Po_b )
3236 endif
3237 endif
32380 T_sub = average_value_ppoly(1, T_mean, T_int, T_poly, iMethod, 1, Po_t, Po_b)
32390 T_top_int = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(1,:), deg+1, Po_t)
32400 T_bot_int = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(1,:), deg+1, Po_b)
3241
32420end subroutine neutral_disc_column_values
3243
3244!> Returns the neutral diffusive flux of a tracer through one neutral layer from the discontinuous
3245!! reconstructions in the columns on either side of a face, or zero if the differences across the face
3246!! are not all of the same sign.
32470pure real function neutral_disc_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int, &
3248 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int, hEff, khtr)
3249 real, intent(in) :: TL_top !< Left-column tracer at the top of the neutral layer in arbitrary
3250 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3251 real, intent(in) :: TL_bot !< Left-column tracer at the bottom of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3252 real, intent(in) :: TL_sub !< Left-column tracer averaged over the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3253 real, intent(in) :: TL_top_int !< Left-column polynomial at the top of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3254 real, intent(in) :: TL_bot_int !< Left-column polynomial at the bottom of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3255 real, intent(in) :: TR_top !< Right-column tracer at the top of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3256 real, intent(in) :: TR_bot !< Right-column tracer at the bottom of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3257 real, intent(in) :: TR_sub !< Right-column tracer averaged over the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3258 real, intent(in) :: TR_top_int !< Right-column polynomial at the top of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3259 real, intent(in) :: TR_bot_int !< Right-column polynomial at the bottom of the neutral layer [A ~> a]
3260 real, intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness of the neutral layer [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3261 real, intent(in) :: khtr !< An averaged diffusivity, 1 [nondim] without tapering or vertical structure
3262 ! Local variables
3263 real :: dT_top, dT_bottom, dT_sublayer, dT_top_int, dT_bot_int ! Differences in tracer concentrations
3264 ! between the right and left columns [A ~> a]
3265 logical :: down_flux
3266
32670 dT_top = TR_top - TL_top
32680 dT_bottom = TR_bot - TL_bot
32690 dT_sublayer = TR_sub - TL_sub
32700 dT_top_int = TR_top_int - TL_top_int
32710 dT_bot_int = TR_bot_int - TL_bot_int
3272
3273 down_flux = dT_top <= 0. .and. dT_bottom <= 0. .and. &
3274 dT_sublayer <= 0. .and. dT_top_int <= 0. .and. &
32750 dT_bot_int <= 0.
3276 down_flux = down_flux .or. &
3277 (dT_top >= 0. .and. dT_bottom >= 0. .and. &
3278 dT_sublayer >= 0. .and. dT_top_int >= 0. .and. &
32790 dT_bot_int >= 0.)
32800 if (down_flux) then
32810 neutral_disc_flux_value = dT_sublayer * hEff * khtr
3282 else
32830 neutral_disc_flux_value = 0.
3284 endif
3285
32860end function neutral_disc_flux_value
3287
3288!> Calculates the neutral diffusion fluxes of a tracer through the zonal faces, a block of faces at a
3289!! time. The diffusivity factors of tapering or a vertical structure of the diffusivity are always
3290!! found on the host, and the fluxes of discontinuous reconstructions are calculated there as well.
32910subroutine zonal_neutral_flux(G, GV, h, T, nsurf, PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, uFlx, h_neglect, nIIB, njj, &
32920 continuous, tapering, vert_struct, deg, remap_CS, h_neglect_edge, hbl, Coef_h)
3293 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3294 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< Ocean vertical grid structure
3295 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3296 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: T !< Layer tracer in arbitrary concentration
3297 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3298 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
3299 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: PoL !< Fractional position of each neutral surface
3300 !! within layer KoL of the western column [nondim]
3301 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: PoR !< Fractional position of each neutral surface
3302 !! within layer KoR of the eastern column [nondim]
3303 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: KoL !< Index of the first western interface above
3304 !! each neutral surface
3305 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: KoR !< Index of the first eastern interface above
3306 !! each neutral surface
3307 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
3308 !! surfaces [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3309 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf-1), intent(inout) :: uFlx !< Zonal flux of tracer between pairs of
3310 !! neutral layers in units of conc H, e.g.
3311 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature
3312 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3313 integer, intent(in) :: nIIB !< I block size for array calculations
3314 integer, intent(in) :: njj !< j block size for array calculations
3315 logical, intent(in) :: continuous !< If true, use continuous reconstructions
3316 logical, intent(in) :: tapering !< If true, taper the diffusivity within the boundary layers
3317 logical, intent(in) :: vert_struct !< If true, the diffusivity has a vertical structure
3318 integer, intent(in) :: deg !< Degree of the discontinuous reconstruction polynomials
3319 type(remapping_CS), intent(in) :: remap_CS !< Remapping control structure used for the discontinuous
3320 !! reconstructions
3321 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect_edge !< A negligibly small width used for the edge values of
3322 !! discontinuous reconstructions [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3323 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), optional, intent(in) :: hbl !< Boundary layer depth [H ~> m or kg m-2],
3324 !! used if tapering is true
3325 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), optional, intent(in) :: Coef_h !< The diffusivity coefficients
3326 !! at tracer points [L2 ~> m2], used if vert_struct is true
3327
3328 ! Local variables
3329 ! The block-sized work arrays below are dimensioned (nIIB+1,njj,nz[+1]). Their first index covers the
3330 ! tracer columns IsbB to IebB+1 on either side of the block's zonal faces.
33310 real, dimension(nIIB+1,njj,SZK_(GV)+1) :: Ti_b ! Interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3332 ! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
33330 real, dimension(nIIB+1,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: aL_b ! Upper PPM edge values of the tracer [A ~> a]
33340 real, dimension(nIIB+1,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: aR_b ! Lower PPM edge values of the tracer [A ~> a]
3335 real :: TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer ! Western tracer at the top and bottom of a neutral layer and averaged
3336 ! over it, in arbitrary concentration units [A ~> a]
3337 real :: TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer ! Eastern tracer at the top and bottom of a neutral layer and averaged
3338 ! over it, in arbitrary concentration units [A ~> a]
3339 logical :: badL, badR ! True if the western or eastern bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked are out of range
3340 logical :: bad ! True if any bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked are out of range
3341 ! Variables used with tapering or a vertical structure of the diffusivity
33420 real, dimension(nIIB,njj,SZK_(GV)+1) :: cL_b, cR_b ! Diffusivity factors at the interfaces of the western
3343 ! and eastern column of each face, [nondim] or [L2 ~> m2]
33440 real, dimension(SZK_(GV)+1) :: cl, cr ! Tapering coefficients of the western and eastern columns [nondim]
3345 real :: khtr ! The averaged diffusivity factor of a neutral layer, [nondim] or [L2 ~> m2]
3346 ! Variables used with discontinuous reconstructions
33470 real, dimension(nIIB+1,njj,SZK_(GV),deg+1) :: Tpoly_b ! Coefficients of the polynomial reconstructions in the layers
3348 ! of the block's columns [A ~> a]
33490 real, dimension(nIIB+1,njj,SZK_(GV),2) :: Tid_b ! Edge values of the reconstructions in the layers of the
3350 ! block's columns [A ~> a]
33510 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),deg+1) :: Tpoly_c ! A column's polynomial reconstruction coefficients [A ~> a]
33520 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),2) :: Tid_c ! A column's reconstruction edge values [A ~> a]
33530 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),2) :: Tslope_c ! A column's reconstruction edge slopes, unused here, in units of
3354 ! a vertical gradient [A H-1 ~> a m-1 or a m2 kg-1]
3355 real :: TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int ! Western tracer averaged over a neutral layer and from the
3356 ! polynomial at its top and bottom [A ~> a]
3357 real :: TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int ! Eastern tracer averaged over a neutral layer and from the
3358 ! polynomial at its top and bottom [A ~> a]
3359 integer :: iMethod ! The integration method of the discontinuous reconstructions
3360 integer :: I, j, k, m, ks, klt, klb, krt, krb, nk, II, jj
3361 integer :: jsb, jeb ! The j-index bounds of the current block.
3362 integer :: IsbB, IebB ! The I-index bounds of the current block.
3363 integer :: jje, IIe ! The block-local j- and I-index end bounds.
3364
33650 nk = GV%ke
33660 bad = .false.
3367
3368 !$omp target update from(T, PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, uFlx) if(.not.continuous)
3369 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3370
33710 do jsb=G%jsc,G%jec,njj ; do IsbB=G%isc-1,G%iec,nIIB
33720 IebB = min(IsbB + nIIB - 1, G%iec)
33730 jeb = min(jsb + njj - 1, G%jec)
33740 IIe = IebB - IsbB + 1
33750 jje = jeb - jsb + 1
3376
3377 ! Diffusivity factors at the interfaces of the columns on either side of each face
33780 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) then
33790 do jj=1,jje ; do II=1,IIe
33800 I = IsbB + II - 1
33810 j = jsb + jj - 1
3382
33830 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.) then
33840 if (tapering) call compute_tapering_coeffs(nk+1, hbl(I,j), hbl(I+1,j), cl, cr, h(I,j,:), h(I+1,j,:))
33850 do k=1,nk+1
33860 if (tapering .and. vert_struct) then
33870 cL_b(II,jj,k) = cl(k)*Coef_h(i,j,k) ; cR_b(II,jj,k) = cr(k)*Coef_h(i+1,j,k)
33880 elseif (tapering) then
33890 cL_b(II,jj,k) = cl(k) ; cR_b(II,jj,k) = cr(k)
3390 else
33910 cL_b(II,jj,k) = Coef_h(i,j,k) ; cR_b(II,jj,k) = Coef_h(i+1,j,k)
3392 endif
3393 enddo
3394 endif
3395 enddo ; enddo
3396 !$omp target update to(cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3397 endif
3398
33990 if (continuous) then
3400 call neutral_reconstruction_block(G, IsbB, IebB+1, jsb, jeb, nk, h, T, IsbB, IsbB+nIIB, jsb, jsb+njj-1, &
34010 Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, h_neglect)
3402 else
3403 ! Reconstructions of the columns on either side of the block's faces
34040 do jj=1,jje ; do II=1,IIe+1
34050 i = IsbB + II - 1
34060 j = jsb + jj - 1
3407
34080 Tpoly_c(:,:) = 0.
34090 Tid_c(:,:) = 0.
3410 call build_reconstructions_1d( remap_CS, nk, h(i,j,:), T(i,j,:), Tpoly_c, Tid_c, &
34110 Tslope_c, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
34120 do m=1,deg+1 ; do k=1,nk ; Tpoly_b(II,jj,k,m) = Tpoly_c(k,m) ; enddo ; enddo
34130 do m=1,2 ; do k=1,nk ; Tid_b(II,jj,k,m) = Tid_c(k,m) ; enddo ; enddo
3414 enddo ; enddo
3415 endif
3416
34170 if (continuous) then
3418 do concurrent (ks=1:nsurf-1, jj=1:jje, II=1:IIe, G%mask2dCu(IsbB+II-1,jsb+jj-1) > 0.) &
3419 DO_LOCALITY(local(I, j, klt, klb, krt, krb, badL, badR, khtr)) &
3420 DO_LOCALITY(local(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer)) &
34210 DO_LOCALITY(reduce(.or.: bad))
34220 I = IsbB + II - 1
34230 j = jsb + jj - 1
3424
34250 if (hEff(I,j,ks) == 0.) then
34260 uFlx(I,j,ks) = 0.
3427 else
34280 klt = KoL(I,j,ks) ; klb = KoL(I,j,ks+1)
34290 krt = KoR(I,j,ks) ; krb = KoR(I,j,ks+1)
34300 khtr = 1.0
3431 ! these are added in this order to preserve vertically-uniform diffusivity answers
34320 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) &
34330 khtr = 0.25 * ((cL_b(II,jj,klb) + cL_b(II,jj,klt)) + (cR_b(II,jj,krb) + cR_b(II,jj,krt)))
3434 call neutral_column_values(PoL(I,j,ks), PoL(I,j,ks+1), klb-klt, Ti_b(II,jj,klt), Ti_b(II,jj,klt+1), &
3435 Ti_b(II,jj,klb), Ti_b(II,jj,klb+1), aL_b(II,jj,klt), aR_b(II,jj,klt), &
34360 T(i,j,klt), TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, badL)
3437 call neutral_column_values(PoR(I,j,ks), PoR(I,j,ks+1), krb-krt, Ti_b(II+1,jj,krt), &
3438 Ti_b(II+1,jj,krt+1), Ti_b(II+1,jj,krb), Ti_b(II+1,jj,krb+1), &
3439 aL_b(II+1,jj,krt), aR_b(II+1,jj,krt), T(i+1,j,krt), &
34400 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, badR)
3441 uFlx(I,j,ks) = neutral_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, &
34420 hEff(I,j,ks), khtr)
34430 bad = bad .or. (badL .or. badR)
3444 endif
3445 enddo
3446 else
34470 do jj=1,jje ; do ks=1,nsurf-1 ; do II=1,IIe
34480 I = IsbB + II - 1
34490 j = jsb + jj - 1
3450
34510 if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.) then
34520 if (hEff(I,j,ks) == 0.) then
34530 uFlx(I,j,ks) = 0.
3454 else
34550 klt = KoL(I,j,ks) ; klb = KoL(I,j,ks+1)
34560 krt = KoR(I,j,ks) ; krb = KoR(I,j,ks+1)
34570 khtr = 1.0
3458 ! these are added in this order to preserve vertically-uniform diffusivity answers
34590 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) &
34600 khtr = 0.25 * ((cL_b(II,jj,klb) + cL_b(II,jj,klt)) + (cR_b(II,jj,krb) + cR_b(II,jj,krt)))
3461 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, klt, klb, PoL(I,j,ks), PoL(I,j,ks+1), T(i,j,klt:klt), &
3462 Tid_b(II,jj,klt:klt,:), Tid_b(II,jj,max(klt-1,1),2), Tid_b(II,jj,min(klt+1,nk),1), &
34630 deg, iMethod, Tpoly_b(II,jj,klt:klt,:), TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int)
3464 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, krt, krb, PoR(I,j,ks), PoR(I,j,ks+1), T(i+1,j,krt:krt), &
3465 Tid_b(II+1,jj,krt:krt,:), Tid_b(II+1,jj,max(krt-1,1),2), Tid_b(II+1,jj,min(krt+1,nk),1), &
34660 deg, iMethod, Tpoly_b(II+1,jj,krt:krt,:), TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int)
3467 uFlx(I,j,ks) = neutral_disc_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int, &
3468 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int, &
34690 hEff(I,j,ks), khtr)
3470 endif
3471 endif
3472 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
3473 endif
3474 enddo ; enddo
3475
3476 !$omp target exit data map(release: Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3477 !$omp target update to(uFlx) if(.not.continuous)
3478
34790 if (bad) call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM_neutral_diffusion, zonal_neutral_flux: the bounds passed to "//&
34800 "ppm_ave are out of order or more than one layer apart.")
3481
34820end subroutine zonal_neutral_flux
3483
3484!> Calculates the neutral diffusion fluxes of a tracer through the meridional faces, a block of faces at a
3485!! time. The diffusivity factors of tapering or a vertical structure of the diffusivity are always
3486!! found on the host, and the fluxes of discontinuous reconstructions are calculated there as well.
34870subroutine meridional_neutral_flux(G, GV, h, T, nsurf, PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, vFlx, h_neglect, nii, nJJB, &
34880 continuous, tapering, vert_struct, deg, remap_CS, h_neglect_edge, hbl, Coef_h)
3489 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3490 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< Ocean vertical grid structure
3491 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
