3492 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: T !< Layer tracer in arbitrary concentration
3493 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3494 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
3495 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: PoL !< Fractional position of each neutral surface
3496 !! within layer KoL of the southern column [nondim]
3497 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: PoR !< Fractional position of each neutral surface
3498 !! within layer KoR of the northern column [nondim]
3499 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: KoL !< Index of the first southern interface above
3500 !! each neutral surface
3501 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: KoR !< Index of the first northern interface above
3502 !! each neutral surface
3503 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
3504 !! surfaces [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3505 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),nsurf-1), intent(inout) :: vFlx !< Meridional flux of tracer between pairs of
3506 !! neutral layers in units of conc H, e.g.
3507 !! [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature
3508 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3509 integer, intent(in) :: nii !< i block size for array calculations
3510 integer, intent(in) :: nJJB !< J block size for array calculations
3511 logical, intent(in) :: continuous !< If true, use continuous reconstructions
3512 logical, intent(in) :: tapering !< If true, taper the diffusivity within the boundary layers
3513 logical, intent(in) :: vert_struct !< If true, the diffusivity has a vertical structure
3514 integer, intent(in) :: deg !< Degree of the discontinuous reconstruction polynomials
3515 type(remapping_CS), intent(in) :: remap_CS !< Remapping control structure used for the discontinuous
3516 !! reconstructions
3517 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect_edge !< A negligibly small width used for the edge values of
3518 !! discontinuous reconstructions [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3519 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), optional, intent(in) :: hbl !< Boundary layer depth [H ~> m or kg m-2],
3520 !! used if tapering is true
3521 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), optional, intent(in) :: Coef_h !< The diffusivity coefficients
3522 !! at tracer points [L2 ~> m2], used if vert_struct is true
3523
3524 ! Local variables
3525 ! The block-sized work arrays below are dimensioned (nii,nJJB+1,nz[+1]). Their second index covers the
3526 ! tracer rows JsbB to JebB+1 on either side of the block's meridional faces.
35270 real, dimension(nii,nJJB+1,SZK_(GV)+1) :: Ti_b ! Interface tracer in arbitrary concentration
3528 ! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
35290 real, dimension(nii,nJJB+1,SZK_(GV)) :: aL_b ! Upper PPM edge values of the tracer [A ~> a]
35300 real, dimension(nii,nJJB+1,SZK_(GV)) :: aR_b ! Lower PPM edge values of the tracer [A ~> a]
3531 real :: TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer ! Southern tracer at the top and bottom of a neutral layer and averaged
3532 ! over it, in arbitrary concentration units [A ~> a]
3533 real :: TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer ! Northern tracer at the top and bottom of a neutral layer and averaged
3534 ! over it, in arbitrary concentration units [A ~> a]
3535 logical :: badL, badR ! True if the southern or northern bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked are out of range
3536 logical :: bad ! True if any bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked are out of range
3537 ! Variables used with tapering or a vertical structure of the diffusivity
35380 real, dimension(nii,nJJB,SZK_(GV)+1) :: cL_b, cR_b ! Diffusivity factors at the interfaces of the southern
3539 ! and northern column of each face, [nondim] or [L2 ~> m2]
35400 real, dimension(SZK_(GV)+1) :: cl, cr ! Tapering coefficients of the southern and northern columns [nondim]
3541 real :: khtr ! The averaged diffusivity factor of a neutral layer, [nondim] or [L2 ~> m2]
3542 ! Variables used with discontinuous reconstructions
35430 real, dimension(nii,nJJB+1,SZK_(GV),deg+1) :: Tpoly_b ! Coefficients of the polynomial reconstructions in the layers
3544 ! of the block's columns [A ~> a]
35450 real, dimension(nii,nJJB+1,SZK_(GV),2) :: Tid_b ! Edge values of the reconstructions in the layers of the
3546 ! block's columns [A ~> a]
35470 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),deg+1) :: Tpoly_c ! A column's polynomial reconstruction coefficients [A ~> a]
35480 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),2) :: Tid_c ! A column's reconstruction edge values [A ~> a]
35490 real, dimension(SZK_(GV),2) :: Tslope_c ! A column's reconstruction edge slopes, unused here, in units of
3550 ! a vertical gradient [A H-1 ~> a m-1 or a m2 kg-1]
3551 real :: TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int ! Southern tracer averaged over a neutral layer and from the
3552 ! polynomial at its top and bottom [A ~> a]
3553 real :: TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int ! Northern tracer averaged over a neutral layer and from the
3554 ! polynomial at its top and bottom [A ~> a]
3555 integer :: iMethod ! The integration method of the discontinuous reconstructions
3556 integer :: i, J, k, m, ks, klt, klb, krt, krb, nk, ii, JJ
3557 integer :: isb, ieb ! The i-index bounds of the current block.
3558 integer :: JsbB, JebB ! The J-index bounds of the current block.
3559 integer :: iie, JJe ! The block-local i- and J-index end bounds.
3560
35610 nk = GV%ke
35620 bad = .false.
3563
3564 !$omp target update from(T, PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, vFlx) if(.not.continuous)
3565 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3566
35670 do JsbB=G%jsc-1,G%jec,nJJB ; do isb=G%isc,G%iec,nii
35680 ieb = min(isb + nii - 1, G%iec)
35690 JebB = min(JsbB + nJJB - 1, G%jec)
35700 iie = ieb - isb + 1
35710 JJe = JebB - JsbB + 1
3572
3573 ! Diffusivity factors at the interfaces of the columns on either side of each face
35740 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) then
35750 do JJ=1,JJe ; do ii=1,iie
35760 i = isb + ii - 1
35770 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
3578
35790 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.) then
35800 if (tapering) call compute_tapering_coeffs(nk+1, hbl(i,J), hbl(i,J+1), cl, cr, h(i,J,:), h(i,J+1,:))
35810 do k=1,nk+1
35820 if (tapering .and. vert_struct) then
35830 cL_b(ii,JJ,k) = cl(k)*Coef_h(i,j,k) ; cR_b(ii,JJ,k) = cr(k)*Coef_h(i,j+1,k)
35840 elseif (tapering) then
35850 cL_b(ii,JJ,k) = cl(k) ; cR_b(ii,JJ,k) = cr(k)
3586 else
35870 cL_b(ii,JJ,k) = Coef_h(i,j,k) ; cR_b(ii,JJ,k) = Coef_h(i,j+1,k)
3588 endif
3589 enddo
3590 endif
3591 enddo ; enddo
3592 !$omp target update to(cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3593 endif
3594
35950 if (continuous) then
3596 call neutral_reconstruction_block(G, isb, ieb, JsbB, JebB+1, nk, h, T, isb, isb+nii-1, JsbB, JsbB+nJJB, &
35970 Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, h_neglect)
3598 else
3599 ! Reconstructions of the columns on either side of the block's faces
36000 do JJ=1,JJe+1 ; do ii=1,iie
36010 i = isb + ii - 1
36020 j = JsbB + JJ - 1
3603
36040 Tpoly_c(:,:) = 0.
36050 Tid_c(:,:) = 0.
3606 call build_reconstructions_1d( remap_CS, nk, h(i,j,:), T(i,j,:), Tpoly_c, Tid_c, &
36070 Tslope_c, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect_edge )
36080 do m=1,deg+1 ; do k=1,nk ; Tpoly_b(ii,JJ,k,m) = Tpoly_c(k,m) ; enddo ; enddo
36090 do m=1,2 ; do k=1,nk ; Tid_b(ii,JJ,k,m) = Tid_c(k,m) ; enddo ; enddo
3610 enddo ; enddo
3611 endif
3612
36130 if (continuous) then
3614 do concurrent (ks=1:nsurf-1, JJ=1:JJe, ii=1:iie, G%mask2dCv(isb+ii-1,JsbB+JJ-1) > 0.) &
3615 DO_LOCALITY(local(i, J, klt, klb, krt, krb, badL, badR, khtr)) &
3616 DO_LOCALITY(local(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer)) &
36170 DO_LOCALITY(reduce(.or.: bad))
36180 i = isb + ii - 1
36190 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
3620
36210 if (hEff(i,J,ks) == 0.) then
36220 vFlx(i,J,ks) = 0.
3623 else
36240 klt = KoL(i,J,ks) ; klb = KoL(i,J,ks+1)
36250 krt = KoR(i,J,ks) ; krb = KoR(i,J,ks+1)
36260 khtr = 1.0
3627 ! these are added in this order to preserve vertically-uniform diffusivity answers
36280 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) &
36290 khtr = 0.25 * ((cL_b(ii,JJ,klb) + cL_b(ii,JJ,klt)) + (cR_b(ii,JJ,krb) + cR_b(ii,JJ,krt)))
3630 call neutral_column_values(PoL(i,J,ks), PoL(i,J,ks+1), klb-klt, Ti_b(ii,JJ,klt), Ti_b(ii,JJ,klt+1), &
3631 Ti_b(ii,JJ,klb), Ti_b(ii,JJ,klb+1), aL_b(ii,JJ,klt), aR_b(ii,JJ,klt), &
36320 T(i,j,klt), TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, badL)
3633 call neutral_column_values(PoR(i,J,ks), PoR(i,J,ks+1), krb-krt, Ti_b(ii,JJ+1,krt), &
3634 Ti_b(ii,JJ+1,krt+1), Ti_b(ii,JJ+1,krb), Ti_b(ii,JJ+1,krb+1), &
3635 aL_b(ii,JJ+1,krt), aR_b(ii,JJ+1,krt), T(i,j+1,krt), &
36360 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, badR)
3637 vFlx(i,J,ks) = neutral_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, &
36380 hEff(i,J,ks), khtr)
36390 bad = bad .or. (badL .or. badR)
3640 endif
3641 enddo
3642 else
36430 do JJ=1,JJe ; do ks=1,nsurf-1 ; do ii=1,iie
36440 i = isb + ii - 1
36450 J = JsbB + JJ - 1
3646
36470 if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.) then
36480 if (hEff(i,J,ks) == 0.) then
36490 vFlx(i,J,ks) = 0.
3650 else
36510 klt = KoL(i,J,ks) ; klb = KoL(i,J,ks+1)
36520 krt = KoR(i,J,ks) ; krb = KoR(i,J,ks+1)
36530 khtr = 1.0
3654 ! these are added in this order to preserve vertically-uniform diffusivity answers
36550 if (tapering .or. vert_struct) &
36560 khtr = 0.25 * ((cL_b(ii,JJ,klb) + cL_b(ii,JJ,klt)) + (cR_b(ii,JJ,krb) + cR_b(ii,JJ,krt)))
3657 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, klt, klb, PoL(i,J,ks), PoL(i,J,ks+1), T(i,j,klt:klt), &
3658 Tid_b(ii,JJ,klt:klt,:), Tid_b(ii,JJ,max(klt-1,1),2), Tid_b(ii,JJ,min(klt+1,nk),1), &
36590 deg, iMethod, Tpoly_b(ii,JJ,klt:klt,:), TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int)
3660 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, krt, krb, PoR(i,J,ks), PoR(i,J,ks+1), T(i,j+1,krt:krt), &
3661 Tid_b(ii,JJ+1,krt:krt,:), Tid_b(ii,JJ+1,max(krt-1,1),2), Tid_b(ii,JJ+1,min(krt+1,nk),1), &
36620 deg, iMethod, Tpoly_b(ii,JJ+1,krt:krt,:), TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int)
3663 vFlx(i,J,ks) = neutral_disc_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int, &
3664 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int, &
36650 hEff(i,J,ks), khtr)
3666 endif
3667 endif
3668 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
3669 endif
3670 enddo ; enddo
3671
3672 !$omp target exit data map(release: Ti_b, aL_b, aR_b, cL_b, cR_b) if(continuous)
3673 !$omp target update to(vFlx) if(.not.continuous)
3674
36750 if (bad) call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM_neutral_diffusion, meridional_neutral_flux: the bounds passed to "//&
36760 "ppm_ave are out of order or more than one layer apart.")
3677
36780end subroutine meridional_neutral_flux
3679
3680!> Updates a tracer with the convergence of its neutral diffusion fluxes, a block of tracer columns at a
3681!! time. With a vertical structure of the diffusivity, the fluxes already include the diffusivity and this
3682!! is done on the host.
36830subroutine neutral_flux_divergence(G, GV, h, Coef_x, Coef_y, nsurf, uKoL, uKoR, vKoL, vKoR, uFlx, vFlx, &
36840 ndiff_answer_date, conc_underflow, T, do_tend, tendency, Idt, nii, njj, &
3685 vert_struct)
3686 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3687 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< Ocean vertical grid structure
3688 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3689 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(in) :: Coef_x !< dt * Kh * dy / dx at u-points [L2 ~> m2]
3690 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), intent(in) :: Coef_y !< dt * Kh * dx / dy at v-points [L2 ~> m2]
3691 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
3692 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: uKoL !< Index of the first western interface above
3693 !! each neutral surface at u-points
3694 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: uKoR !< Index of the first eastern interface above
3695 !! each neutral surface at u-points
3696 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: vKoL !< Index of the first southern interface above
3697 !! each neutral surface at v-points
3698 integer, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf), intent(in) :: vKoR !< Index of the first northern interface above
3699 !! each neutral surface at v-points
3700 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),nsurf-1), intent(in) :: uFlx !< Zonal flux of tracer between pairs of neutral
3701 !! layers [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature, or
3702 !! [C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg] if vert_struct is true
3703 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),nsurf-1), intent(in) :: vFlx !< Meridional flux of tracer between pairs of
3704 !! neutral layers [C H ~> degC m or degC kg m-2] for temperature,
3705 !! or [C H L2 ~> degC m3 or degC kg] if vert_struct is true
3706 integer, intent(in) :: ndiff_answer_date !< The vintage of the order of arithmetic to use
3707 real, intent(in) :: conc_underflow !< Tracer concentrations smaller in magnitude than
3708 !! this are set to 0, in the same units as T [conc]
3709 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(inout) :: T !< Layer tracer concentration, in arbitrary
3710 !! units [conc] (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3711 logical, intent(in) :: do_tend !< If true, store the tracer content tendency in tendency
3712 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(inout) :: tendency !< The tracer content tendency for
3713 !! diagnostics [H conc T-1 ~> m conc s-1 or kg m-2 conc s-1]
3714 real, intent(in) :: Idt !< The inverse of the time step [T-1 ~> s-1]
3715 integer, intent(in) :: nii !< i block size for array calculations
3716 integer, intent(in) :: njj !< j block size for array calculations
3717 logical, intent(in) :: vert_struct !< If true, the diffusivity has a vertical structure
3718
3719 ! Local variables
3720 ! The block-sized work arrays below are dimensioned (nii,njj,nz) and hold the changes in tracer content of
3721 ! the layers of each column of the block, in [H L2 conc ~> m3 conc or kg conc].
37220 real, dimension(nii,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: dTr_b ! Total change in tracer content
37230 real, dimension(nii,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: dTrN_b ! Change in tracer content via the logically northern face
37240 real, dimension(nii,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: dTrS_b ! Change in tracer content via the logically southern face
37250 real, dimension(nii,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: dTrE_b ! Change in tracer content via the logically eastern face
37260 real, dimension(nii,njj,SZK_(GV)) :: dTrW_b ! Change in tracer content via the logically western face
3727 real :: H_subroundoff ! A thickness that is so small it is usually lost in roundoff [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3728 integer :: i, j, k, ks, nk, ii, jj
3729 integer :: isb, ieb, jsb, jeb ! The i- and j-index bounds of the current block.
3730 integer :: iie, jje ! The block-local i- and j-index end bounds.
3731
37320 nk = GV%ke
37330 H_subroundoff = GV%H_subroundoff
3734
3735 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: dTr_b, dTrN_b, dTrS_b, dTrE_b, dTrW_b) if(.not.vert_struct)
3736
37370 if (vert_struct) then
3738 !$omp target update from(uKoL, uKoR, vKoL, vKoR, uFlx, vFlx, T)
37390 do jsb=G%jsc,G%jec,njj ; do isb=G%isc,G%iec,nii
37400 ieb = min(isb + nii - 1, G%iec)
37410 jeb = min(jsb + njj - 1, G%jec)
37420 iie = ieb - isb + 1
37430 jje = jeb - jsb + 1
3744
37450 if (ndiff_answer_date <= 20240330) then
37460 do k=1,nk ; do jj=1,jje ; do ii=1,iie
37470 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.
3748 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
37490 do jj=1,jje ; do ks=1,nsurf-1 ; do ii=1,iie
37500 i = isb + ii - 1
37510 j = jsb + jj - 1
3752
37530 if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.) then
37540 k = uKoL(I,j,ks)
37550 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) + uFlx(I,j,ks)
37560 k = uKoR(I-1,j,ks)
37570 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) - uFlx(I-1,j,ks)
37580 k = vKoL(i,J,ks)
37590 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) + vFlx(i,J,ks)
37600 k = vKoR(i,J-1,ks)
37610 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) - vFlx(i,J-1,ks)
3762 endif
3763 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
3764 else ! This form recovers rotational symmetry.
37650 do k=1,nk ; do jj=1,jje ; do ii=1,iie
37660 dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0
3767 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
37680 do jj=1,jje ; do ks=1,nsurf-1 ; do ii=1,iie
37690 i = isb + ii - 1
37700 j = jsb + jj - 1
3771
37720 if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.) then
37730 k = uKoL(I,j,ks)
37740 dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) + uFlx(I,j,ks)
37750 k = uKoR(I-1,j,ks)
37760 dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) - uFlx(I-1,j,ks)
37770 k = vKoL(i,J,ks)
37780 dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) + vFlx(i,J,ks)
37790 k = vKoR(i,J-1,ks)
37800 dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) - vFlx(i,J-1,ks)
3781 endif
3782 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
37830 do k=1,nk ; do jj=1,jje ; do ii=1,iie
37840 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = (dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) + dTrS_b(ii,jj,k)) + (dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) + dTrW_b(ii,jj,k))
3785 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
3786 endif
3787
37880 do jj=1,jje ; do k=1,nk ; do ii=1,iie
37890 i = isb + ii - 1
37900 j = jsb + jj - 1
3791
37920 if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.) then
37930 T(i,j,k) = T(i,j,k) + dTr_b(ii,jj,k) * ( G%IareaT(i,j) / ( h(i,j,k) + H_subroundoff ) )
37940 if (abs(T(i,j,k)) < conc_underflow) T(i,j,k) = 0.0
37950 if (do_tend) tendency(i,j,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) * G%IareaT(i,j) * Idt
3796 endif
3797 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
3798 enddo ; enddo
3799 !$omp target update to(T)
3800 !$omp target update to(tendency) if(do_tend)
38010 return
3802 endif
3803
38040 do jsb=G%jsc,G%jec,njj ; do isb=G%isc,G%iec,nii
38050 ieb = min(isb + nii - 1, G%iec)
38060 jeb = min(jsb + njj - 1, G%jec)
38070 iie = ieb - isb + 1
38080 jje = jeb - jsb + 1
3809
38100 if (ndiff_answer_date <= 20240330) then
38110 do concurrent (k=1:nk, jj=1:jje, ii=1:iie)
38120 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.
3813 enddo
38140 do concurrent (jj=1:jje) DO_LOCALITY(local(ks))
38150 do ks=1,nsurf-1
38160 do concurrent (ii=1:iie, G%mask2dT(isb+ii-1,jsb+jj-1) > 0.) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, j, k))
38170 i = isb + ii - 1
38180 j = jsb + jj - 1
3819
38200 k = uKoL(I,j,ks)
38210 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) + Coef_x(I,j,1) * uFlx(I,j,ks)
38220 k = uKoR(I-1,j,ks)
38230 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) - Coef_x(I-1,j,1) * uFlx(I-1,j,ks)
38240 k = vKoL(i,J,ks)
38250 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) + Coef_y(i,J,1) * vFlx(i,J,ks)
38260 k = vKoR(i,J-1,ks)
38270 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) - Coef_y(i,J-1,1) * vFlx(i,J-1,ks)
3828 enddo
3829 enddo
3830 enddo
3831 else ! This form recovers rotational symmetry.
38320 do concurrent (k=1:nk, jj=1:jje, ii=1:iie)
38330 dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0 ; dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) = 0.0
3834 enddo
38350 do concurrent (jj=1:jje) DO_LOCALITY(local(ks))
38360 do ks=1,nsurf-1
38370 do concurrent (ii=1:iie, G%mask2dT(isb+ii-1,jsb+jj-1) > 0.) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, j, k))
38380 i = isb + ii - 1
38390 j = jsb + jj - 1
3840
38410 k = uKoL(I,j,ks)
38420 dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) + Coef_x(I,j,1) * uFlx(I,j,ks)
38430 k = uKoR(I-1,j,ks)
38440 dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrW_b(ii,jj,k) - Coef_x(I-1,j,1) * uFlx(I-1,j,ks)
38450 k = vKoL(i,J,ks)
38460 dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) + Coef_y(i,J,1) * vFlx(i,J,ks)
38470 k = vKoR(i,J-1,ks)
38480 dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) = dTrS_b(ii,jj,k) - Coef_y(i,J-1,1) * vFlx(i,J-1,ks)
3849 enddo
3850 enddo
3851 enddo
38520 do concurrent (k=1:nk, jj=1:jje, ii=1:iie)
38530 dTr_b(ii,jj,k) = (dTrN_b(ii,jj,k) + dTrS_b(ii,jj,k)) + (dTrE_b(ii,jj,k) + dTrW_b(ii,jj,k))
3854 enddo
3855 endif
3856
38570 do concurrent (k=1:nk, jj=1:jje, ii=1:iie, G%mask2dT(isb+ii-1,jsb+jj-1) > 0.) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, j))
38580 i = isb + ii - 1
38590 j = jsb + jj - 1
3860
38610 T(i,j,k) = T(i,j,k) + dTr_b(ii,jj,k) * ( G%IareaT(i,j) / ( h(i,j,k) + H_subroundoff ) )
38620 if (abs(T(i,j,k)) < conc_underflow) T(i,j,k) = 0.0
3863 enddo
3864
38650 if (do_tend) then
38660 do concurrent (k=1:nk, jj=1:jje, ii=1:iie, G%mask2dT(isb+ii-1,jsb+jj-1) > 0.) DO_LOCALITY(local(i, j))
38670 i = isb + ii - 1
38680 j = jsb + jj - 1
3869
38700 tendency(i,j,k) = dTr_b(ii,jj,k) * G%IareaT(i,j) * Idt
3871 enddo
3872 endif
3873 enddo ; enddo
3874
3875 !$omp target exit data map(release: dTr_b, dTrN_b, dTrS_b, dTrE_b, dTrW_b)
3876
3877end subroutine neutral_flux_divergence
3878
3879!> Evaluate various parts of the reconstructions to calculate gradient-based flux limiter
38800subroutine neutral_surface_T_eval(nk, ns, k_sub, Ks, Ps, T_mean, T_int, deg, iMethod, T_poly, &
3881 T_top, T_bot, T_sub, T_top_int, T_bot_int, T_layer)
3882 integer, intent(in ) :: nk !< Number of cell averages
3883 integer, intent(in ) :: ns !< Number of neutral surfaces
3884 integer, intent(in ) :: k_sub !< Index of current neutral layer
3885 integer, dimension(ns), intent(in ) :: Ks !< List of the layers associated with each neutral surface
3886 real, dimension(ns), intent(in ) :: Ps !< List of the positions within a layer of each surface [nondim]
3887 real, dimension(nk), intent(in ) :: T_mean !< Layer average of tracer in arbitrary concentration
3888 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3889 real, dimension(nk,2), intent(in ) :: T_int !< Layer interface values of tracer from reconstruction
3890 !! in concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3891 integer, intent(in ) :: deg !< Degree of reconstruction polynomial (e.g. 1 is linear)
3892 integer, intent(in ) :: iMethod !< Method of integration to use
3893 real, dimension(nk,deg+1), intent(in ) :: T_poly !< Coefficients of polynomial reconstructions in arbitrary
3894 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3895 real, intent( out) :: T_top !< Tracer value at top (across discontinuity if necessary) in
3896 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3897 real, intent( out) :: T_bot !< Tracer value at bottom (across discontinuity if necessary)
3898 !! in concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3899 real, intent( out) :: T_sub !< Average of the tracer value over the sublayer in arbitrary
3900 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3901 real, intent( out) :: T_top_int !< Tracer value at the top interface of a neutral layer in
3902 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3903 real, intent( out) :: T_bot_int !< Tracer value at the bottom interface of a neutral layer in
3904 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3905 real, intent( out) :: T_layer !< Cell-average tracer concentration in a layer that
3906 !! the reconstruction belongs to in concentration
3907 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3908
3909 integer :: kl, ks_top, ks_bot
3910
39110 ks_top = k_sub
39120 ks_bot = k_sub + 1
39130 if ( Ks(ks_top) /= Ks(ks_bot) ) then
39140 call MOM_error(FATAL, "Neutral surfaces span more than one layer")
3915 endif
39160 kl = Ks(k_sub)
3917 ! First if the neutral surfaces spans the entirety of a cell, then do not search across the discontinuity
39180 if ( (Ps(ks_top) == 0.) .and. (Ps(ks_bot) == 1.)) then
39190 T_top = T_int(kl,1)
39200 T_bot = T_int(kl,2)
3921 else
3922 ! Search across potential discontinuity at top
39230 if ( (kl > 1) .and. (Ps(ks_top) == 0.) ) then
39240 T_top = T_int(kl-1,2)
3925 else
39260 T_top = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(kl,:), deg+1, Ps(ks_top) )
3927 endif
3928 ! Search across potential discontinuity at bottom
39290 if ( (kl < nk) .and. (Ps(ks_bot) == 1.) ) then
39300 T_bot = T_int(kl+1,1)
3931 else
39320 T_bot = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(kl,:), deg+1, Ps(ks_bot) )
3933 endif
3934 endif
39350 T_sub = average_value_ppoly(nk, T_mean, T_int, T_poly, iMethod, kl, Ps(ks_top), Ps(ks_bot))
39360 T_top_int = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(kl,:), deg+1, Ps(ks_top))
39370 T_bot_int = evaluation_polynomial( T_poly(kl,:), deg+1, Ps(ks_bot))
39380 T_layer = T_mean(kl)
3939
39400end subroutine neutral_surface_T_eval
3941
3942!> Discontinuous PPM reconstructions of the left/right edge values within a cell
39430subroutine ppm_left_right_edge_values(nk, Tl, Ti, aL, aR)
3944 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
3945 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tl !< Layer tracer (conc, e.g. degC) in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
3946 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Ti !< Interface tracer (conc, e.g. degC) in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
3947 real, dimension(nk), intent(inout) :: aL !< Left edge value of tracer (conc, e.g. degC)
3948 !! in the same arbitrary units as Tl and Ti [A ~> a]
3949 real, dimension(nk), intent(inout) :: aR !< Right edge value of tracer (conc, e.g. degC)
3950 !! in the same arbitrary units as Tl and Ti [A ~> a]
3951
3952 integer :: k
3953 ! Setup reconstruction edge values
39540 do k = 1, nk
39550 aL(k) = Ti(k)
39560 aR(k) = Ti(k+1)
39570 if ( signum(1., aR(k) - Tl(k))*signum(1., Tl(k) - aL(k)) <= 0.0 ) then
39580 aL(k) = Tl(k)
39590 aR(k) = Tl(k)
39600 elseif ( sign(3., aR(k) - aL(k)) * ( (Tl(k) - aL(k)) + (Tl(k) - aR(k))) > abs(aR(k) - aL(k)) ) then
39610 aL(k) = Tl(k) + 2.0 * ( Tl(k) - aR(k) )
39620 elseif ( sign(3., aR(k) - aL(k)) * ( (Tl(k) - aL(k)) + (Tl(k) - aR(k))) < -abs(aR(k) - aL(k)) ) then
39630 aR(k) = Tl(k) + 2.0 * ( Tl(k) - aL(k) )
3964 endif
3965 enddo
39660end subroutine ppm_left_right_edge_values
3967
3968!> Calculates the continuous reconstruction of a tracer used for neutral diffusion in a block of columns:
3969!! the interface values and the PPM edge values within each layer.
39700subroutine neutral_reconstruction_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, h, T, isdw, iedw, jsdw, jedw, &
39710 Ti, aL, aR, h_neglect)
3972 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
3973 integer, intent(in) :: isb !< This block's starting i index
3974 integer, intent(in) :: ieb !< This block's ending i index
3975 integer, intent(in) :: jsb !< This block's starting j index
3976 integer, intent(in) :: jeb !< This block's ending j index
3977 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
3978 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: h !< Layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3979 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: T !< Layer tracer in arbitrary concentration
3980 !! units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3981 integer, intent(in) :: isdw !< The lower i bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
3982 integer, intent(in) :: iedw !< The upper i bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
3983 integer, intent(in) :: jsdw !< The lower j bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
3984 integer, intent(in) :: jedw !< The upper j bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
3985 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk+1), intent(inout) :: Ti !< Interface tracer in arbitrary
3986 !! concentration units (e.g. [C ~> degC] for temperature)
3987 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk), intent(inout) :: aL !< Upper PPM edge value of the tracer in
3988 !! each layer, in the same arbitrary units as T [A ~> a]
3989 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk), intent(inout) :: aR !< Lower PPM edge value of the tracer in
3990 !! each layer, in the same arbitrary units as T [A ~> a]
3991 real, intent(in) :: h_neglect !< A negligibly small thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
3992
39930 call interface_scalar_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, h, T, isdw, iedw, jsdw, jedw, Ti, 2, h_neglect)
39940 call ppm_left_right_edge_values_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, T, isdw, iedw, jsdw, jedw, Ti, aL, aR)
3995
39960end subroutine neutral_reconstruction_block
3997
3998!> Discontinuous PPM reconstructions of the left/right edge values within the cells of a block
39990subroutine ppm_left_right_edge_values_block(G, isb, ieb, jsb, jeb, nk, Tl, isdw, iedw, jsdw, jedw, Ti, aL, aR)
4000 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid structure
4001 integer, intent(in) :: isb !< This block's starting i index
4002 integer, intent(in) :: ieb !< This block's ending i index
4003 integer, intent(in) :: jsb !< This block's starting j index
4004 integer, intent(in) :: jeb !< This block's ending j index
4005 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
4006 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nk), intent(in) :: Tl !< Layer tracer (conc, e.g. degC) in arbitrary
4007 !! units [A ~> a]
4008 integer, intent(in) :: isdw !< The lower i bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
4009 integer, intent(in) :: iedw !< The upper i bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
4010 integer, intent(in) :: jsdw !< The lower j bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
4011 integer, intent(in) :: jedw !< The upper j bound of the memory of Ti, aL and aR
4012 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk+1), intent(in) :: Ti !< Interface tracer (conc, e.g. degC) in
4013 !! arbitrary units [A ~> a]
4014 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk), intent(inout) :: aL !< Left edge value of tracer (conc, e.g. degC)
4015 !! in the same arbitrary units as Tl and Ti [A ~> a]
4016 real, dimension(isdw:iedw,jsdw:jedw,nk), intent(inout) :: aR !< Right edge value of tracer (conc, e.g. degC)
4017 !! in the same arbitrary units as Tl and Ti [A ~> a]
4018 ! Local variables
4019 integer :: i, j, k
4020
4021 ! Setup reconstruction edge values
40220 do concurrent (k=1:nk, j=jsb:jeb, i=isb:ieb)
40230 aL(i,j,k) = Ti(i,j,k)
40240 aR(i,j,k) = Ti(i,j,k+1)
40250 if ( signum(1., aR(i,j,k) - Tl(i,j,k))*signum(1., Tl(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) <= 0.0 ) then
40260 aL(i,j,k) = Tl(i,j,k)
40270 aR(i,j,k) = Tl(i,j,k)
40280 elseif ( sign(3., aR(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) * ( (Tl(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) + (Tl(i,j,k) - aR(i,j,k))) > &
4029 abs(aR(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) ) then
40300 aL(i,j,k) = Tl(i,j,k) + 2.0 * ( Tl(i,j,k) - aR(i,j,k) )
40310 elseif ( sign(3., aR(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) * ( (Tl(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) + (Tl(i,j,k) - aR(i,j,k))) < &
4032 -abs(aR(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k)) ) then
40330 aR(i,j,k) = Tl(i,j,k) + 2.0 * ( Tl(i,j,k) - aL(i,j,k) )
4034 endif
4035 enddo
4036
40370end subroutine ppm_left_right_edge_values_block

4349
43500logical function ndiff_unit_tests_discontinuous(verbose)
4351 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< It true, write results to stdout
4352 ! Local variables
4353 integer, parameter :: nk = 3
4354 integer, parameter :: ns = nk*4
4355 real, dimension(nk) :: Sl, Sr ! Salinities [ppt] and temperatures [degC]
4356 real, dimension(nk) :: hl, hr ! Thicknesses in pressure units [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other
4357 ! arbitrary units [arbitrary]
4358 real, dimension(nk,2) :: TiL, SiL, TiR, SiR ! Cell edge salinities [ppt] and temperatures [degC]
4359 real, dimension(nk,2) :: Pres_l, Pres_r ! Interface pressures [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4360 integer, dimension(ns) :: KoL, KoR ! Index of the layer where the interface is found in the
4361 ! left and right columns
4362 real, dimension(ns) :: PoL, PoR ! Fractional position of neutral surface within layer KoL
4363 ! of the left column or KoR of the right column [nondim]
4364 real, dimension(ns-1) :: hEff ! Effective thickness between two neutral surfaces
4365 ! in the same units as hl and hr [arbitrary]
43660 type(neutral_diffusion_CS) :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
4367 real, dimension(nk,2) :: ppoly_T_l, ppoly_T_r ! Linear reconstruction for T [degC]
4368 real, dimension(nk,2) :: ppoly_S_l, ppoly_S_r ! Linear reconstruction for S [ppt]
4369 logical, dimension(nk) :: stable_l, stable_r
4370 integer :: k
4371 logical :: v
4372
43730 v = verbose
43740 ndiff_unit_tests_discontinuous = .false. ! Normally return false
43750 write(stdout,*) '==== MOM_neutral_diffusion: ndiff_unit_tests_discontinuous ='
4376
4377 ! Unit tests for find_neutral_surface_positions_discontinuous
4378 ! Salinity is 0 for all these tests
43790 allocate(CS%EOS)
43800 call EOS_manual_init(CS%EOS, form_of_EOS=EOS_LINEAR, dRho_dT=-1., dRho_dS=0.)
43810 Sl(:) = 0. ; Sr(:) = 0. ; ; SiL(:,:) = 0. ; SiR(:,:) = 0.
43820 ppoly_T_l(:,:) = 0. ; ppoly_T_r(:,:) = 0.
43830 ppoly_S_l(:,:) = 0. ; ppoly_S_r(:,:) = 0.
4384 ! Intialize any control structures needed for unit tests
43850 CS%ref_pres = -1.
4386
43870 hL = (/10.,10.,10./) ; hR = (/10.,10.,10./)
43880 Pres_l(1,1) = 0. ; Pres_l(1,2) = hL(1) ; Pres_r(1,1) = 0. ; Pres_r(1,2) = hR(1)
43890 do k = 2,nk
43900 Pres_l(k,1) = Pres_l(k-1,2)
43910 Pres_l(k,2) = Pres_l(k,1) + hL(k)
43920 Pres_r(k,1) = Pres_r(k-1,2)
43930 Pres_r(k,2) = Pres_r(k,1) + hR(k)
4394 enddo
43950 CS%delta_rho_form = 'mid_pressure'
43960 CS%neutral_pos_method = 1
4397
43980 TiL(1,:) = (/ 22.00, 18.00 /) ; TiL(2,:) = (/ 18.00, 14.00 /) ; TiL(3,:) = (/ 14.00, 10.00 /)
43990 TiR(1,:) = (/ 22.00, 18.00 /) ; TiR(2,:) = (/ 18.00, 14.00 /) ; TiR(3,:) = (/ 14.00, 10.00 /)

4612 find_neutral_pos_linear(CS, 0., 10., 35., 0., 0.8, &
4613 0., 1.0, 0., 0.5, &
46140 (/12.,0./), (/34.,2./)), "Salt stratified Linearized Alpha/Beta"))
46150 if (.not. ndiff_unit_tests_discontinuous) write(stdout,*) 'Pass'
4616
46170end function ndiff_unit_tests_discontinuous

4715!> Returns true if comparison of Po and Ptrue fails, and conditionally writes results to stream
47160logical function test_data1d(verbose, nk, Po, Ptrue, title)
4717 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< If true, write results to stdout

4721 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< Title for messages
4722
4723 ! Local variables
4724 integer :: k, stdunit
4725

4729 enddo
4730
47310 if (test_data1d .or. verbose) then
47320 stdunit = stdout

47390 'k=',k,'Po=',Po(k),'Ptrue=',Ptrue(k),'err=',Po(k)-Ptrue(k),'WRONG!'
4740 else
47410 if (verbose) &
4742 write(stdunit,'(a,I0,2(1x,a,f20.16),1x,a,1pe22.15)') &
47430 'k=',k,'Po=',Po(k),'Ptrue=',Ptrue(k),'err=',Po(k)-Ptrue(k)
4744 endif
4745 enddo
4746 endif
4747
47480end function test_data1d

4783!> Returns true if output of find_neutral_surface_positions() does not match correct values,
4784!! and conditionally writes results to stream
47850logical function test_nsp(verbose, ns, KoL, KoR, pL, pR, hEff, KoL0, KoR0, pL0, pR0, hEff0, title)
4786 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< If true, write results to stdout
4787 integer, intent(in) :: ns !< Number of surfaces
4788 integer, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
4789 integer, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
4790 real, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: pL !< Fractional position of neutral surface within layer
4791 !! KoL of left column [nondim]
4792 real, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: pR !< Fractional position of neutral surface within layer
4793 !! KoR of right column [nondim]
4794 real, dimension(ns-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral surfaces [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4795 integer, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: KoL0 !< Correct value for KoL
4796 integer, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: KoR0 !< Correct value for KoR
4797 real, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: pL0 !< Correct value for pL [nondim]
4798 real, dimension(ns), intent(in) :: pR0 !< Correct value for pR [nondim]
4799 real, dimension(ns-1), intent(in) :: hEff0 !< Correct value for hEff [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4800 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< Title for messages
4801
4802 ! Local variables
4803 integer :: k, stdunit
4804 logical :: this_row_failed
4805
48060 test_nsp = .false.
48070 do k = 1,ns
48080 test_nsp = test_nsp .or. compare_nsp_row(KoL(k), KoR(k), pL(k), pR(k), KoL0(k), KoR0(k), pL0(k), pR0(k))
48090 if (k < ns) then
48100 if (hEff(k) /= hEff0(k)) test_nsp = .true.
4811 endif
4812 enddo
4813
48140 if (test_nsp .or. verbose) then
48150 stdunit = stdout
48160 if (test_nsp) stdunit = stderr ! In case of wrong results, write to error stream
48170 write(stdunit,'(a)') title
48180 do k = 1,ns
48190 this_row_failed = compare_nsp_row(KoL(k), KoR(k), pL(k), pR(k), KoL0(k), KoR0(k), pL0(k), pR0(k))
48200 if (this_row_failed) then
48210 write(stdunit,10) k,KoL(k),pL(k),KoR(k),pR(k),' <-- WRONG!'
48220 write(stdunit,10) k,KoL0(k),pL0(k),KoR0(k),pR0(k),' <-- should be this'
4823 else
48240 write(stdunit,10) k,KoL(k),pL(k),KoR(k),pR(k)
4825 endif
48260 if (k < ns) then
48270 if (hEff(k) /= hEff0(k)) then
48280 write(stdunit,'(i3,8x,"layer hEff =",2(f20.16,a))') k,hEff(k)," .neq. ",hEff0(k),' <-- WRONG!'
4829 else
48300 write(stdunit,'(i3,8x,"layer hEff =",f20.16)') k,hEff(k)
4831 endif
4832 endif
4833 enddo
4834 endif
48350 if (test_nsp) call MOM_error(FATAL,"test_nsp failed")
4836
483710 format("ks=",i3," kL=",i3," pL=",f20.16," kR=",i3," pR=",f20.16,a)
48380end function test_nsp

4840!> Returns true if the fluxes of a tracer through the neutral layers between two columns differ in any
4841!! bit between neutral_surface_flux and the shared cores of the blocked flux routines (neutral_column_values
4842!! and neutral_flux_value), or if the cores flag the bounds passed to ppm_ave as out of range.
48430logical function test_flux_cores(verbose, nk, nsurf, hl, hr, Tl, Tr, PiL, PiR, KoL, KoR, hEff, title)
4844 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< If true, write results to stdout
4845 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of layers
4846 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
4847 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hl !< Left-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4848 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hr !< Right-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4849 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column layer tracer in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
4850 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column layer tracer in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
4851 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiL !< Fractional position of the neutral surfaces within
4852 !! layer KoL of the left column [nondim]
4853 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiR !< Fractional position of the neutral surfaces within
4854 !! layer KoR of the right column [nondim]
4855 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoL !< Left-column layer index of each neutral surface
4856 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoR !< Right-column layer index of each neutral surface
4857 real, dimension(nsurf-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
4858 !! surfaces [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4859 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< Title for messages
4860 ! Local variables
48610 real, dimension(nsurf-1) :: Flx ! Fluxes from neutral_surface_flux [A H ~> a m or a kg m-2]
48620 real, dimension(nsurf-1) :: Flx_cores ! Fluxes from the shared cores [A H ~> a m or a kg m-2]
48630 real, dimension(nk+1) :: Til, Tir ! Left- and right-column interface tracer values [A ~> a]
48640 real, dimension(nk) :: aL_l, aR_l ! Left-column PPM edge values [A ~> a]
48650 real, dimension(nk) :: aL_r, aR_r ! Right-column PPM edge values [A ~> a]
4866 real :: TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer ! Left-column tracer at and between two neutral surfaces [A ~> a]
4867 real :: TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer ! Right-column tracer at and between two neutral surfaces [A ~> a]
4868 real :: h_neglect ! A negligible thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4869 logical :: badL, badR, any_bad ! Flags for out-of-range bounds passed to ppm_ave_unchecked
4870 integer :: ks, klt, klb, krt, krb
4871
48720 h_neglect = 1.0e-30
48730 call neutral_surface_flux(nk, nsurf, 2, hl, hr, Tl, Tr, PiL, PiR, KoL, KoR, hEff, Flx, .true., h_neglect)
4874
48750 call interface_scalar(nk, hl, Tl, Til, 2, h_neglect)
48760 call interface_scalar(nk, hr, Tr, Tir, 2, h_neglect)
48770 call ppm_left_right_edge_values(nk, Tl, Til, aL_l, aR_l)
48780 call ppm_left_right_edge_values(nk, Tr, Tir, aL_r, aR_r)
48790 any_bad = .false.
48800 do ks = 1, nsurf-1
48810 if (hEff(ks) == 0.) then
48820 Flx_cores(ks) = 0.
4883 else
48840 klt = KoL(ks) ; klb = KoL(ks+1)
4885 call neutral_column_values(PiL(ks), PiL(ks+1), klb-klt, Til(klt), Til(klt+1), Til(klb), Til(klb+1), &
48860 aL_l(klt), aR_l(klt), Tl(klt), TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, badL)
48870 krt = KoR(ks) ; krb = KoR(ks+1)
4888 call neutral_column_values(PiR(ks), PiR(ks+1), krb-krt, Tir(krt), Tir(krt+1), Tir(krb), Tir(krb+1), &
48890 aL_r(krt), aR_r(krt), Tr(krt), TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, badR)
48900 Flx_cores(ks) = neutral_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_layer, TR_top, TR_bot, TR_layer, hEff(ks), 1.0)
48910 any_bad = any_bad .or. (badL .or. badR)
4892 endif
4893 enddo
4894
48950 test_flux_cores = test_data1d(verbose, nsurf-1, Flx_cores, Flx, title)
48960 if (any_bad) then
48970 test_flux_cores = .true.
48980 write(stdout,'(a)') trim(title)//': the bounds passed to ppm_ave were flagged as out of range'
4899 endif
4900
49010end function test_flux_cores

4903!> Returns true if the discontinuous flux cores used by zonal_ and meridional_neutral_flux give fluxes that
4904!! differ from those of neutral_surface_flux, and conditionally writes results to stdout.
49050logical function test_disc_flux_cores(verbose, nk, nsurf, scheme, hl, hr, Tl, Tr, PiL, PiR, KoL, KoR, hEff, title)
4906 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< If true, write results to stdout
4907 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of layers
4908 integer, intent(in) :: nsurf !< Number of neutral surfaces
4909 character(len=*), intent(in) :: scheme !< The reconstruction scheme
4910 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hl !< Left-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4911 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: hr !< Right-column layer thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4912 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column layer tracer in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
4913 real, dimension(nk), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column layer tracer in arbitrary units [A ~> a]
4914 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiL !< Fractional position of the neutral surfaces within
4915 !! layer KoL of the left column [nondim]
4916 real, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: PiR !< Fractional position of the neutral surfaces within
4917 !! layer KoR of the right column [nondim]
4918 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoL !< Left-column layer index of each neutral surface
4919 integer, dimension(nsurf), intent(in) :: KoR !< Right-column layer index of each neutral surface
4920 real, dimension(nsurf-1), intent(in) :: hEff !< Effective thickness between two neutral
4921 !! surfaces [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4922 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< Title for messages
4923 ! Local variables
4924 type(remapping_CS) :: remap_CS ! Remapping control structure for the reconstructions
49250 real, dimension(nsurf-1) :: Flx ! Fluxes from neutral_surface_flux [A H ~> a m or a kg m-2]
49260 real, dimension(nsurf-1) :: Flx_cores ! Fluxes from the shared cores [A H ~> a m or a kg m-2]
49270 real, allocatable, dimension(:,:) :: poly_l, poly_r ! Left- and right-column polynomial coefficients [A ~> a]
49280 real, dimension(nk,2) :: Tid_l, Tid_r ! Left- and right-column reconstruction edge values [A ~> a]
49290 real, dimension(nk,2) :: slope_l, slope_r ! Left- and right-column edge slopes, unused [A H-1 ~> a m-1]
4930 real :: TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int ! Left-column tracer values [A ~> a]
4931 real :: TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int ! Right-column tracer values [A ~> a]
4932 real :: h_neglect ! A negligible thickness [H ~> m or kg m-2]
4933 integer :: deg, iMethod, ks, klt, krt
4934
49350 h_neglect = 1.0e-30
49360 call initialize_remapping(remap_CS, scheme, boundary_extrapolation=.false.)
49370 call extract_member_remapping_CS(remap_CS, degree=deg)
4938 call neutral_surface_flux(nk, nsurf, deg, hl, hr, Tl, Tr, PiL, PiR, KoL, KoR, hEff, Flx, .false., h_neglect, &
49390 remap_CS, h_neglect)
4940
49410 allocate(poly_l(nk,deg+1), poly_r(nk,deg+1))
49420 poly_l(:,:) = 0. ; poly_r(:,:) = 0. ; Tid_l(:,:) = 0. ; Tid_r(:,:) = 0.
49430 call build_reconstructions_1d(remap_CS, nk, hl, Tl, poly_l, Tid_l, slope_l, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect)
49440 call build_reconstructions_1d(remap_CS, nk, hr, Tr, poly_r, Tid_r, slope_r, iMethod, h_neglect, h_neglect)
49450 do ks = 1, nsurf-1
49460 if (hEff(ks) == 0.) then
49470 Flx_cores(ks) = 0.
4948 else
49490 klt = KoL(ks) ; krt = KoR(ks)
4950 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, klt, KoL(ks+1), PiL(ks), PiL(ks+1), Tl(klt:klt), Tid_l(klt:klt,:), &
4951 Tid_l(max(klt-1,1),2), Tid_l(min(klt+1,nk),1), deg, iMethod, poly_l(klt:klt,:), &
49520 TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int)
4953 call neutral_disc_column_values(nk, krt, KoR(ks+1), PiR(ks), PiR(ks+1), Tr(krt:krt), Tid_r(krt:krt,:), &
4954 Tid_r(max(krt-1,1),2), Tid_r(min(krt+1,nk),1), deg, iMethod, poly_r(krt:krt,:), &
49550 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int)
4956 Flx_cores(ks) = neutral_disc_flux_value(TL_top, TL_bot, TL_sub, TL_top_int, TL_bot_int, &
49570 TR_top, TR_bot, TR_sub, TR_top_int, TR_bot_int, hEff(ks), 1.0)
4958 endif
4959 enddo
4960
49610 test_disc_flux_cores = test_data1d(verbose, nsurf-1, Flx_cores, Flx, title)
4962
49630end function test_disc_flux_cores
4964
4965!> Returns true if the neutral surface positions that neutral_positions_block finds for a pair of columns
4966!! differ in any bit from those of find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous, or if it reports a failure.
49670logical function test_positions_block(verbose, nk, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, Pr, Tr, Sr, dRdTr, dRdSr, title, &
4968 bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
4969 logical, intent(in) :: verbose !< If true, write results to stdout
4970 integer, intent(in) :: nk !< Number of levels
4971 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Pl !< Left-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
4972 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Tl !< Left-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
4973 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Sl !< Left-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
4974 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTl !< Left-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
4975 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSl !< Left-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
4976 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Pr !< Right-column interface pressure [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa] or other units
4977 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Tr !< Right-column interface potential temperature [C ~> degC]
4978 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: Sr !< Right-column interface salinity [S ~> ppt]
4979 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdTr !< Right-column dRho/dT [R C-1 ~> kg m-3 degC-1]
4980 real, dimension(nk+1), intent(in) :: dRdSr !< Right-column dRho/dS [R S-1 ~> kg m-3 ppt-1]
4981 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< Title for messages
4982 integer, optional, intent(in) :: bl_kl !< Layer index of the boundary layer (left)
4983 integer, optional, intent(in) :: bl_kr !< Layer index of the boundary layer (right)
4984 real, optional, intent(in) :: bl_zl !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (left) [nondim]
4985 real, optional, intent(in) :: bl_zr !< Fractional position of the boundary layer (right) [nondim]
4986 ! Local variables
49870 real, dimension(2*nk+2) :: PoL, PoR ! Fractional positions from the column search [nondim]
49880 real, dimension(2*nk+2) :: PoL_b, PoR_b ! Fractional positions from the block search [nondim]
49890 integer, dimension(2*nk+2) :: KoL, KoR ! Layer indices from the column search
49900 integer, dimension(2*nk+2) :: KoL_b, KoR_b ! Layer indices from the block search
49910 real, dimension(2*nk+1) :: hEff, hEff_b ! Effective thicknesses from the column and block searches,
4992 ! in the units of Pl and Pr [R L2 T-2 ~> Pa]
4993 logical, dimension(1,1) :: do_face ! True for the one face of the block
4994 integer, dimension(1,1) :: bkl, bkr ! Layer indices of the boundary layers
4995 real, dimension(1,1) :: bzl, bzr ! Fractional positions of the boundary layers [nondim]
4996 logical :: interior ! True if the boundary layer arguments are present
4997 integer :: ierr ! The failure code from neutral_positions_block
4998
49990 interior = present(bl_kl) .and. present(bl_kr) .and. present(bl_zl) .and. present(bl_zr)
50000 bkl(1,1) = 1 ; bkr(1,1) = 1 ; bzl(1,1) = 0. ; bzr(1,1) = 0.
50010 if (interior) then
5002 call find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous(nk, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, Pr, Tr, Sr, dRdTr, dRdSr, &
50030 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff, bl_kl, bl_kr, bl_zl, bl_zr)
50040 bkl(1,1) = bl_kl ; bkr(1,1) = bl_kr ; bzl(1,1) = bl_zl ; bzr(1,1) = bl_zr
5005 else
5006 call find_neutral_surface_positions_continuous(nk, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, Pr, Tr, Sr, dRdTr, dRdSr, &
50070 PoL, PoR, KoL, KoR, hEff)
5008 endif
50090 do_face(1,1) = .true.
50100 PoL_b(:) = 0. ; PoR_b(:) = 0. ; KoL_b(:) = 1 ; KoR_b(:) = 1 ; hEff_b(:) = 0.
5011 call neutral_positions_block(1, 1, 1, 1, nk, 2*nk+2, do_face, Pl, Tl, Sl, dRdTl, dRdSl, Pr, Tr, Sr, dRdTr, &
50120 dRdSr, interior, bkl, bkr, bzl, bzr, PoL_b, PoR_b, KoL_b, KoR_b, hEff_b, ierr)
5013 test_positions_block = test_nsp(verbose, 2*nk+2, KoL_b, KoR_b, PoL_b, PoR_b, hEff_b, &
50140 KoL, KoR, PoL, PoR, hEff, title)
50150 if (ierr /= 0) then
50160 test_positions_block = .true.
50170 write(stdout,'(a,i2)') trim(title)//': neutral_positions_block failed with code ', ierr
5018 endif
5019
50200end function test_positions_block
5021
5022!> Compares a single row, k, of output from find_neutral_surface_positions()
50230logical function compare_nsp_row(KoL, KoR, pL, pR, KoL0, KoR0, pL0, pR0)
5024 integer, intent(in) :: KoL !< Index of first left interface above neutral surface
5025 integer, intent(in) :: KoR !< Index of first right interface above neutral surface
5026 real, intent(in) :: pL !< Fractional position of neutral surface within layer KoL of left column [nondim]
5027 real, intent(in) :: pR !< Fractional position of neutral surface within layer KoR of right column [nondim]
5028 integer, intent(in) :: KoL0 !< Correct value for KoL
5029 integer, intent(in) :: KoR0 !< Correct value for KoR
5030 real, intent(in) :: pL0 !< Correct value for pL [nondim]
5031 real, intent(in) :: pR0 !< Correct value for pR [nondim]
5032
50330 compare_nsp_row = .false.
50340 if (KoL /= KoL0) compare_nsp_row = .true.
50350 if (KoR /= KoR0) compare_nsp_row = .true.
50360 if (pL /= pL0) compare_nsp_row = .true.
50370 if (pR /= pR0) compare_nsp_row = .true.
50380end function compare_nsp_row
5039
5040!> Compares output position from refine_nondim_position with an expected value
50410logical function test_rnp(expected_pos, test_pos, title)
5042 real, intent(in) :: expected_pos !< The expected position [arbitrary]
5043 real, intent(in) :: test_pos !< The position returned by the code [arbitrary]
5044 character(len=*), intent(in) :: title !< A label for this test
5045 ! Local variables
5046 integer :: stdunit
5047
50480 stdunit = stdout ! Output to standard error
50490 test_rnp = ABS(expected_pos - test_pos) > 2*EPSILON(test_pos)
50500 if (test_rnp) then
50510 write(stdunit,'(A, f20.16, " .neq. ", f20.16, " <-- WRONG")') title, expected_pos, test_pos
5052 else
50530 write(stdunit,'(A, f20.16, " == ", f20.16)') title, expected_pos, test_pos
5054 endif
50550end function test_rnp

5057!> Deallocates neutral_diffusion control structure
50581subroutine neutral_diffusion_end(CS)
5059 type(neutral_diffusion_CS), pointer :: CS !< Neutral diffusion control structure
5060
50611 if (associated(CS)) then
5062 !$omp target exit data map(delete: CS%dRdT, CS%dRdS, CS%Tint, CS%Sint, CS%Pint, &
5063 !$omp CS%uPoL, CS%uPoR, CS%uKoL, CS%uKoR, CS%uhEff, &
5064 !$omp CS%vPoL, CS%vPoR, CS%vKoL, CS%vKoR, CS%vhEff)
5065 !$omp target exit data map(delete: CS)
50660 deallocate(CS)
5067 endif
5068
50691end subroutine neutral_diffusion_end
